package com.tairitsutek.newgc;

import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.ModConfigSpec;

public class Config {
    private static final ModConfigSpec.Builder BUILDER = new ModConfigSpec.Builder();

    public static final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue LOG_DIRT_BLOCK = BUILDER
            .comment("Whether to log the dirt block on common setup")
            .define("logDirtBlock", true);

    public static final ModConfigSpec.IntValue MAGIC_NUMBER = BUILDER
            .comment("A magic number")
            .defineInRange("magicNumber", 42, 0, Integer.MAX_VALUE);

    public static final ModConfigSpec.ConfigValue<String> MAGIC_NUMBER_INTRODUCTION = BUILDER
            .comment("What you want the introduction message to be for the magic number")
            .define("magicNumberIntroduction", "The magic number is... ");

    public static final ModConfigSpec.ConfigValue<java.util.List<? extends String>> ITEM_STRINGS = BUILDER
            .comment("A list of items to log on common setup.")
            .defineListAllowEmpty("items", java.util.List.of("minecraft:iron_ingot"), () -> "", o -> o instanceof String);

    public static final ModConfigSpec SPEC = BUILDER.build();
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc;

import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class NewGalaxyClient {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientSetup(FMLClientSetupEvent event) {
        NewGalaxyMain.LOGGER.info("HELLO FROM CLIENT SETUP");
        NewGalaxyMain.LOGGER.info("MINECRAFT NAME >> {}",
                net.minecraft.client.Minecraft.getInstance().getUser().getName());
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\NewGalaxyMain.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc;

import com.mojang.logging.LogUtils;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.network.IoQuakePayload;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.network.JupiterStormPayload;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network.MarsDustStormPayload;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network.MarsVolcanoDebugPayload;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network.MarsVolcanoPayload;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.network.MercurySolarFlarePayload;   // ← 新增
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.PlanetsBlock;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.ModBlockEntities;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.config.NewGalaxyConfig;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.item.material.Material;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.MachinesBlock;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.menu.ModMenus;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.ModAttachments;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.OxygenSyncPacket;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.ResetTankPacket;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.item.ToolsItem;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe.ModRecipes;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModContainer;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.Mod;
import net.neoforged.fml.config.ModConfig;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.NeoForge;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.server.ServerStartingEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.event.RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.registration.PayloadRegistrar;
import org.slf4j.Logger;

@Mod(NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class NewGalaxyMain {
    public static final String MODID = "newgc";
    public static final Logger LOGGER = LogUtils.getLogger();

    public NewGalaxyMain(IEventBus modEventBus, ModContainer modContainer) {
        PlanetsBlock.register(modEventBus);
        ModBlockEntities.register(modEventBus);
        ModMenus.register(modEventBus);
        ToolsItem.register(modEventBus);
        com.tairitsutek.newgc.fluid.ModFluids.register(modEventBus);
        ModAttachments.register(modEventBus);
        modEventBus.addListener(this::registerPayloads);
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.register(this);

        modContainer.registerConfig(ModConfig.Type.COMMON, Config.SPEC);
        modContainer.registerConfig(ModConfig.Type.CLIENT, NewGalaxyConfig.SPEC);

        ModDimensions.register();
        MachinesBlock.register(modEventBus);
        Material.register(modEventBus);
        ModRecipes.register(modEventBus);
    }

    private void registerPayloads(final RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent event) {
        final PayloadRegistrar registrar = event.registrar("1");

        // ========== S2C ==========
        registrar.playToClient(
                OxygenSyncPacket.TYPE,
                OxygenSyncPacket.STREAM_CODEC,
                OxygenSyncPacket::handle
        );
        registrar.playToClient(
                JupiterStormPayload.TYPE,
                JupiterStormPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                JupiterStormPayload::handle
        );
        registrar.playToClient(
                MarsVolcanoPayload.TYPE,
                MarsVolcanoPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                MarsVolcanoPayload::handle
        );
        registrar.playToClient(
                MarsDustStormPayload.TYPE,
                MarsDustStormPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                MarsDustStormPayload::handle
        );

        registrar.playToClient(
                MercurySolarFlarePayload.TYPE,
                MercurySolarFlarePayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                MercurySolarFlarePayload::handle
        );

        registrar.playToServer(
                ResetTankPacket.TYPE,
                ResetTankPacket.STREAM_CODEC,
                ResetTankPacket::handle
        );
        registrar.playToServer(
                MarsVolcanoDebugPayload.TYPE,
                MarsVolcanoDebugPayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                MarsVolcanoDebugPayload::handle
        );

        registrar.playToClient(
                IoQuakePayload.TYPE,
                IoQuakePayload.STREAM_CODEC,
                IoQuakePayload::handle
        );
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onServerStarting(ServerStartingEvent event) {
        LOGGER.info("HELLO from server starting");
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block;

import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.CompressorBlockEntity;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.ModBlockEntities;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.BaseEntityBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.RenderShape;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityTicker;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

public class CompressorBlock extends BaseEntityBlock {

    public static final MapCodec<CompressorBlock> CODEC = simpleCodec(CompressorBlock::new);

    public CompressorBlock(Properties properties) {
        super(properties);
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull MapCodec<? extends BaseEntityBlock> codec() {
        return CODEC;
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull RenderShape getRenderShape(@NotNull BlockState state) {
        return RenderShape.MODEL;
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public BlockEntity newBlockEntity(@NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockState state) {
        return new CompressorBlockEntity(pos, state);
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public <T extends BlockEntity> BlockEntityTicker<T> getTicker(Level level, @NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull BlockEntityType<T> type) {
        return level.isClientSide() ? null
                : createTickerHelper(type, ModBlockEntities.COMPRESSOR.get(),
                (lvl, pos, st, be) -> be.tick());
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull InteractionResult useWithoutItem(@NotNull BlockState state, Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos,
                                                        @NotNull Player player, @NotNull BlockHitResult hit) {
        if (!level.isClientSide()) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof CompressorBlockEntity gen) {
                player.openMenu(gen, pos);
            }
        }
        return InteractionResult.sidedSuccess(level.isClientSide());
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull BlockState playerWillDestroy(Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull Player player) {
        if (!level.isClientSide()) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof CompressorBlockEntity gen) {
                gen.drops();
            }
        }
        return super.playerWillDestroy(level, pos, state, player);
    }

    @Override
    public void onRemove(BlockState state, @NotNull Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, BlockState newState, boolean isMoving) {
        if (!state.is(newState.getBlock())) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof CompressorBlockEntity gen) {
                gen.drops();
            }
        }
        super.onRemove(state, level, pos, newState, isMoving);
    }
}

================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.item.BlockItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.CreativeModeTab;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.SoundType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockBehaviour;
import net.minecraft.world.level.material.MapColor;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredBlock;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredItem;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;

public final class MachinesBlock {

    //region 注册器
    public static final DeferredRegister.Blocks BLOCKS = DeferredRegister.createBlocks(NewGalaxyMain.MODID);
    public static final DeferredRegister.Items ITEMS = DeferredRegister.createItems(NewGalaxyMain.MODID);
    public static final DeferredRegister<CreativeModeTab> CREATIVE_MODE_TABS = DeferredRegister.create(Registries.CREATIVE_MODE_TAB, NewGalaxyMain.MODID);
    //endregion

    //region 机器方块
    public static final DeferredBlock<Block> MECHANICAL_CASING = registerBlock("mechanical_casing", () -> new Block(BlockBehaviour.Properties.of().mapColor(MapColor.METAL).sound(SoundType.METAL).strength(3.5f, 6f).requiresCorrectToolForDrops()));
    public static final DeferredBlock<Block> SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR = registerBlock("solid_fuel_generator", () -> new SolidFuelGeneratorBlock(BlockBehaviour.Properties.of().mapColor(MapColor.METAL).sound(SoundType.METAL).strength(3.5f, 6f).requiresCorrectToolForDrops()));
    public static final DeferredBlock<Block> OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR = registerBlock("oxygen_extractor", () -> new OxygenExtractorBlock(BlockBehaviour.Properties.of().mapColor(MapColor.METAL).sound(SoundType.METAL).strength(3.5f, 6f).requiresCorrectToolForDrops()));
    public static final DeferredBlock<Block> WIRE = registerBlock("wire", () -> new WireBlock(BlockBehaviour.Properties.of().mapColor(MapColor.METAL).sound(SoundType.METAL).strength(1f, 3f).requiresCorrectToolForDrops()));
    public static final DeferredBlock<Block> COMPRESSOR = registerBlock("compressor", () -> new CompressorBlock(BlockBehaviour.Properties.of().mapColor(MapColor.METAL).sound(SoundType.METAL).strength(3.5f, 6f).requiresCorrectToolForDrops()));
    //endregion

    //region 方块物品
    public static final DeferredItem<BlockItem> MECHANICAL_CASING_ITEM = registerItem("mechanical_casing", MECHANICAL_CASING);
    public static final DeferredItem<BlockItem> SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR_ITEM = registerItem("solid_fuel_generator", SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR);
    public static final DeferredItem<BlockItem> OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR_ITEM = registerItem("oxygen_extractor", OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR);
    public static final DeferredItem<BlockItem> WIRE_ITEM = registerItem("wire", WIRE);
    public static final DeferredItem<BlockItem> COMPRESSOR_ITEM = registerItem("compressor", COMPRESSOR);
    //endregion

    //region 注册辅助
    private static DeferredBlock<Block> registerBlock(String name, java.util.function.Supplier<Block> supplier) {
        return BLOCKS.register(name, supplier);
    }

    private static DeferredItem<BlockItem> registerItem(String name, DeferredBlock<Block> block) {
        return ITEMS.registerSimpleBlockItem(name, block);
    }
    //endregion

    //region 机器创造模式物品栏
    public static final DeferredHolder<CreativeModeTab, CreativeModeTab> MACHINE_TAB =
            CREATIVE_MODE_TABS.register("machine_tab", () -> CreativeModeTab.builder()
                    .title(Component.translatable("itemGroup.newgc.machines"))
                    .icon(() -> new ItemStack(SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR.get()))
                    .displayItems((parameters, output) -> {
                        output.accept(MECHANICAL_CASING_ITEM.get());
                        output.accept(SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR_ITEM.get());
                        output.accept(OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR_ITEM.get());
                        output.accept(WIRE_ITEM.get());
                        output.accept(COMPRESSOR_ITEM.get());
                    })
                    .build());
    //endregion

    //region 注册入口
    public static void register(IEventBus modEventBus) {
        BLOCKS.register(modEventBus);
        ITEMS.register(modEventBus);
        CREATIVE_MODE_TABS.register(modEventBus);
    }
    //endregion
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block;

import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.ModBlockEntities;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.OxygenExtractorBlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

import net.minecraft.world.level.block.RenderShape;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityTicker;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

public class OxygenExtractorBlock extends BaseEntityBlock {

    public static final MapCodec<OxygenExtractorBlock> CODEC =
            simpleCodec(OxygenExtractorBlock::new);

    public OxygenExtractorBlock(Properties properties) {
        super(properties);
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull MapCodec<? extends BaseEntityBlock> codec() {
        return CODEC;
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull RenderShape getRenderShape(@NotNull BlockState state) {
        return RenderShape.MODEL;
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public BlockEntity newBlockEntity(@NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockState state) {
        return new OxygenExtractorBlockEntity(pos, state);
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public <T extends BlockEntity> BlockEntityTicker<T> getTicker(Level level, @NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull BlockEntityType<T> type) {
        return level.isClientSide() ? null
                : createTickerHelper(type, ModBlockEntities.OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR.get(),
                        (lvl, pos, st, be) -> be.tick());
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull InteractionResult useWithoutItem(@NotNull BlockState state, Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos,
                                                        @NotNull Player player, @NotNull BlockHitResult hit) {
        if (!level.isClientSide()) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof OxygenExtractorBlockEntity gen) {
                player.openMenu(gen, pos);
            }
        }
        return InteractionResult.sidedSuccess(level.isClientSide());
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull BlockState playerWillDestroy(Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull Player player) {
        if (!level.isClientSide()) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof OxygenExtractorBlockEntity gen) {
                gen.drops();
            }
        }
        return super.playerWillDestroy(level, pos, state, player);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block;

import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.ModBlockEntities;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.BaseEntityBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.RenderShape;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityTicker;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

public class SolidFuelGeneratorBlock extends BaseEntityBlock {

    public static final MapCodec<SolidFuelGeneratorBlock> CODEC =
            simpleCodec(SolidFuelGeneratorBlock::new);

    public SolidFuelGeneratorBlock(Properties properties) {
        super(properties);
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull MapCodec<? extends BaseEntityBlock> codec() {
        return CODEC;
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull RenderShape getRenderShape(@NotNull BlockState state) {
        return RenderShape.MODEL;
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public BlockEntity newBlockEntity(@NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockState state) {
        return new SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity(pos, state);
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public <T extends BlockEntity> BlockEntityTicker<T> getTicker(Level level, @NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull BlockEntityType<T> type) {
        return level.isClientSide() ? null
                : createTickerHelper(type, ModBlockEntities.SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR.get(),
                (lvl, pos, st, be) -> be.tick());
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull InteractionResult useWithoutItem(@NotNull BlockState state, Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos,
                                                        @NotNull Player player, @NotNull BlockHitResult hit) {
        if (!level.isClientSide()) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity gen) {
                player.openMenu(gen, pos);
            }
        }
        return InteractionResult.sidedSuccess(level.isClientSide());
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull BlockState playerWillDestroy(Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull Player player) {
        if (!level.isClientSide()) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity gen) {
                gen.drops();
            }
        }
        return super.playerWillDestroy(level, pos, state, player);
    }

    @Override
    public void onRemove(BlockState state, @NotNull Level level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, BlockState newState, boolean isMoving) {
        if (!state.is(newState.getBlock())) {
            BlockEntity be = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (be instanceof SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity gen) {
                gen.drops();
            }
        }
        super.onRemove(state, level, pos, newState, isMoving);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\block\WireBlock.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block;

import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.ModBlockEntities;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.WireBlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.world.item.context.BlockPlaceContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.BlockGetter;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.LevelAccessor;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.BaseEntityBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.RenderShape;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityTicker;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.StateDefinition;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BooleanProperty;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.CollisionContext;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.Shapes;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.VoxelShape;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

public class WireBlock extends BaseEntityBlock {

    public static final MapCodec<WireBlock> CODEC = simpleCodec(WireBlock::new);

    public static final BooleanProperty UP    = BlockStateProperties.UP;
    public static final BooleanProperty DOWN  = BlockStateProperties.DOWN;
    public static final BooleanProperty NORTH = BlockStateProperties.NORTH;
    public static final BooleanProperty SOUTH = BlockStateProperties.SOUTH;
    public static final BooleanProperty EAST  = BlockStateProperties.EAST;
    public static final BooleanProperty WEST  = BlockStateProperties.WEST;

    private static final VoxelShape CENTER = box(6, 6, 6, 10, 10, 10);

    public WireBlock(Properties properties) {
        super(properties);
        registerDefaultState(stateDefinition.any()
                .setValue(UP, false).setValue(DOWN, false)
                .setValue(NORTH, false).setValue(SOUTH, false)
                .setValue(EAST, false).setValue(WEST, false));
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull MapCodec<? extends BaseEntityBlock> codec() {
        return CODEC;
    }

    @Override
    protected void createBlockStateDefinition(StateDefinition.Builder<Block, BlockState> builder) {
        builder.add(UP, DOWN, NORTH, SOUTH, EAST, WEST);
    }

    private boolean canConnect(BlockGetter level, BlockPos pos, Direction dir) {
        Block block = level.getBlockState(pos.relative(dir)).getBlock();
        return block instanceof WireBlock
                || block instanceof SolidFuelGeneratorBlock
                || block instanceof OxygenExtractorBlock;
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public BlockState getStateForPlacement(@NotNull BlockPlaceContext context) {
        BlockState state = defaultBlockState();
        for (Direction dir : Direction.values()) {
            state = state.setValue(propertyFor(dir),
                    canConnect(context.getLevel(), context.getClickedPos(), dir));
        }
        return state;
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull BlockState updateShape(BlockState state, @NotNull Direction dir, @NotNull BlockState neighborState,
                                              @NotNull LevelAccessor level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockPos neighborPos) {
        return state.setValue(propertyFor(dir), canConnect(level, pos, dir));
    }

    private static BooleanProperty propertyFor(Direction dir) {
        return switch (dir) {
            case UP -> UP;
            case DOWN -> DOWN;
            case NORTH -> NORTH;
            case SOUTH -> SOUTH;
            case EAST -> EAST;
            case WEST -> WEST;
        };
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull VoxelShape getShape(BlockState state, @NotNull BlockGetter level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull CollisionContext context) {
        VoxelShape shape = CENTER;
        if (state.getValue(NORTH)) shape = Shapes.or(shape, box(6, 6, 0, 10, 10, 6));
        if (state.getValue(SOUTH)) shape = Shapes.or(shape, box(6, 6, 10, 10, 10, 16));
        if (state.getValue(EAST))  shape = Shapes.or(shape, box(10, 6, 6, 16, 10, 10));
        if (state.getValue(WEST))  shape = Shapes.or(shape, box(0, 6, 6, 6, 10, 10));
        if (state.getValue(UP))    shape = Shapes.or(shape, box(6, 10, 6, 10, 16, 10));
        if (state.getValue(DOWN))  shape = Shapes.or(shape, box(6, 0, 6, 10, 6, 10));
        return shape;
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull VoxelShape getCollisionShape(@NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull BlockGetter level, @NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull CollisionContext context) {
        return Shapes.empty();
    }

    @Override
    protected @NotNull RenderShape getRenderShape(@NotNull BlockState state) {
        return RenderShape.MODEL;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean propagatesSkylightDown(@NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull BlockGetter level, @NotNull BlockPos pos) {
        return true;
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public BlockEntity newBlockEntity(@NotNull BlockPos pos, @NotNull BlockState state) {
        return new WireBlockEntity(pos, state);
    }

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public <T extends BlockEntity> BlockEntityTicker<T> getTicker(Level level, @NotNull BlockState state, @NotNull BlockEntityType<T> type) {
        return level.isClientSide() ? null
                : createTickerHelper(type, ModBlockEntities.WIRE.get(),
                (lvl, pos, st, be) -> be.tick());
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.energy.SimpleEnergyStorage;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.menu.CompressorMenu;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe.CompressorRecipe;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe.ModRecipes;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.Connection;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ClientGamePacketListener;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket;
import net.minecraft.world.Containers;
import net.minecraft.world.MenuProvider;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.AbstractContainerMenu;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.ContainerData;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.SingleRecipeInput;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.capabilities.Capabilities;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.energy.IEnergyStorage;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.items.ItemStackHandler;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class CompressorBlockEntity extends BlockEntity implements MenuProvider {

    public static final int ENERGY_CAPACITY = 50_000;
    public static final int ENERGY_RECEIVE  = 200;
    public static final int ENERGY_EXTRACT  = 50_000;
    public static final int FE_PER_COMPRESS = 100;
    public static final int COMPRESS_TICKS  = 200;

    public static final int INPUT_SLOT  = 0;
    public static final int OUTPUT_SLOT = 1;

    private int progress = 0;

    private final SimpleEnergyStorage energy =
            new SimpleEnergyStorage(ENERGY_CAPACITY, ENERGY_RECEIVE, ENERGY_EXTRACT);

    private final ItemStackHandler items = new ItemStackHandler(2) {
        @Override
        protected void onContentsChanged(int slot) {
            setChanged();
            if (level != null && !level.isClientSide()) {
                level.sendBlockUpdated(worldPosition, getBlockState(), getBlockState(), 3);
            }
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isItemValid(int slot, @NotNull ItemStack stack) {
            if (slot != INPUT_SLOT) return false;
            if (stack.isEmpty() || level == null) return false;
            return findRecipe(level, stack) != null;
        }
    };

    private final ContainerData data = new ContainerData() {
        @Override
        public int get(int index) {
            return switch (index) {
                case 0 -> progress;
                case 1 -> COMPRESS_TICKS;
                case 2 -> energy.getEnergyStored();
                case 3 -> energy.getMaxEnergyStored();
                default -> 0;
            };
        }

        @Override
        public void set(int index, int value) {
            switch (index) {
                case 0 -> progress = value;
                case 2 -> energy.setEnergy(value);
            }
        }

        @Override
        public int getCount() {
            return 4;
        }
    };

    public CompressorBlockEntity(BlockPos pos, BlockState state) {
        super(ModBlockEntities.COMPRESSOR.get(), pos, state);
    }

    // ==================== 公共方法 ====================
    public ItemStackHandler getItems() { return items; }
    public IEnergyStorage getEnergyStorage() { return energy; }
    public ContainerData getData() { return data; }
    public int getProgress() { return progress; }
    public int getMaxProgress() { return COMPRESS_TICKS; }

    private static CompressorRecipe findRecipe(Level level, ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return null;
        return level.getRecipeManager()
                .getRecipeFor(ModRecipes.COMPRESSING.get(), new SingleRecipeInput(stack), level)
                .map(RecipeHolder::value)
                .orElse(null);
    }

    // ==================== tick ====================
    public void tick() {
        if (level == null || level.isClientSide()) return;

        pullEnergy();

        ItemStack input = items.getStackInSlot(INPUT_SLOT);
        CompressorRecipe recipe = findRecipe(level, input);
        boolean canCompress = recipe != null
                && input.getCount() >= recipe.getInputCount()
                && energy.getEnergyStored() >= FE_PER_COMPRESS;

        boolean changed = false;

        if (canCompress) {
            ItemStack result = recipe.getResultItem(level.registryAccess());
            ItemStack output = items.getStackInSlot(OUTPUT_SLOT);
            boolean outputOk = output.isEmpty()
                    || (ItemStack.isSameItemSameComponents(output, result)
                    && output.getCount() + result.getCount() <= output.getMaxStackSize());

            if (outputOk) {
                progress++;
                if (progress >= COMPRESS_TICKS) {
                    progress = 0;
                    energy.extractEnergy(FE_PER_COMPRESS, false);
                    items.extractItem(INPUT_SLOT, recipe.getInputCount(), false);
                    if (output.isEmpty()) {
                        items.setStackInSlot(OUTPUT_SLOT, result.copy());
                    } else {
                        output.grow(result.getCount());
                    }
                }
                changed = true;
            }
        }

        if (!canCompress && progress != 0) {
            progress = 0;
            changed = true;
        }

        if (changed) setChanged();
        if (level.getGameTime() % 20 == 0) {
            level.sendBlockUpdated(worldPosition, getBlockState(), getBlockState(), 3);
        }
    }

    private void pullEnergy() {
        if (level == null) return;
        if (energy.getEnergyStored() >= energy.getMaxEnergyStored()) return;

        for (Direction dir : Direction.values()) {
            BlockPos neighbor = worldPosition.relative(dir);
            IEnergyStorage other = level.getCapability(
                    Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                    neighbor,
                    dir.getOpposite()
            );
            if (other != null && other.canExtract()) {
                int space = energy.getMaxEnergyStored() - energy.getEnergyStored();
                int want = Math.min(ENERGY_RECEIVE, space);
                int got = other.extractEnergy(want, false);
                if (got > 0) {
                    energy.receiveEnergy(got, false);
                    setChanged();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ==================== 掉落物品 ====================
    public void drops() {
        if (level == null) return;
        for (int i = 0; i < items.getSlots(); i++) {
            ItemStack stack = items.getStackInSlot(i);
            if (!stack.isEmpty()) {
                Containers.dropItemStack(level,
                        worldPosition.getX() + 0.5,
                        worldPosition.getY() + 0.5,
                        worldPosition.getZ() + 0.5,
                        stack.copy());
                items.setStackInSlot(i, ItemStack.EMPTY);
            }
        }
    }

    // ==================== MenuProvider ====================
    @Override
    public @NotNull Component getDisplayName() {
        return Component.translatable("block.newgc.compressor");
    }

    @Override
    public AbstractContainerMenu createMenu(int id, @NotNull Inventory playerInv, @NotNull Player player) {
        return new CompressorMenu(id, playerInv, this, data);
    }

    // ==================== NBT ====================
    @Override
    protected void saveAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.saveAdditional(tag, registries);
        tag.put("Items", items.serializeNBT(registries));
        tag.putInt("Energy", energy.getEnergyStored());
        tag.putInt("Progress", progress);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.loadAdditional(tag, registries);
        if (tag.contains("Items")) {
            items.deserializeNBT(registries, tag.getCompound("Items"));
        }
        energy.setEnergy(tag.getInt("Energy"));
        progress = tag.getInt("Progress");
    }

    // ==================== 网络同步 ====================
    @Override
    public @NotNull CompoundTag getUpdateTag(HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();
        saveAdditional(tag, registries);
        return tag;
    }

    @Override
    public Packet<ClientGamePacketListener> getUpdatePacket() {
        return ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket.create(this);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDataPacket(@NotNull Connection net, ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket pkt, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        CompoundTag tag = pkt.getTag();
        loadAdditional(tag, registries);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.MachinesBlock;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntityType;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;

public class ModBlockEntities {

    //region 注册器
    public static final DeferredRegister<BlockEntityType<?>> BLOCK_ENTITIES =
            DeferredRegister.create(Registries.BLOCK_ENTITY_TYPE, NewGalaxyMain.MODID);
    //endregion

    //region 方块实体类型 - 机器

    public static final DeferredHolder<BlockEntityType<?>, BlockEntityType<SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity>> SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR =
            BLOCK_ENTITIES.register("solid_fuel_generator", () ->
                    BlockEntityType.Builder.of(
                            SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity::new,
                            MachinesBlock.SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR.get()
                    ).build(null));

    public static final DeferredHolder<BlockEntityType<?>, BlockEntityType<OxygenExtractorBlockEntity>> OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR =
            BLOCK_ENTITIES.register("oxygen_extractor", () ->
                    BlockEntityType.Builder.of(
                            OxygenExtractorBlockEntity::new,
                            MachinesBlock.OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR.get()
                    ).build(null));

    public static final DeferredHolder<BlockEntityType<?>, BlockEntityType<WireBlockEntity>> WIRE =
            BLOCK_ENTITIES.register("wire", () ->
                    BlockEntityType.Builder.of(
                            WireBlockEntity::new,
                            MachinesBlock.WIRE.get()
                    ).build(null));

    public static final DeferredHolder<BlockEntityType<?>, BlockEntityType<CompressorBlockEntity>> COMPRESSOR =
            BLOCK_ENTITIES.register("compressor", () ->
                    BlockEntityType.Builder.of(
                            CompressorBlockEntity::new,
                            MachinesBlock.COMPRESSOR.get()
                    ).build(null));
    //endregion

    //region 注册入口
    public static void register(IEventBus bus) {
        BLOCK_ENTITIES.register(bus);
    }
    //endregion
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.energy.SimpleEnergyStorage;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.ModAttachments;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.OxygenData;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.item.ToolsItem;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.menu.OxygenExtractorMenu;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.Connection;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ClientGamePacketListener;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.tags.ItemTags;
import net.minecraft.world.MenuProvider;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.AbstractContainerMenu;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.ContainerData;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.capabilities.Capabilities;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.energy.IEnergyStorage;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.items.ItemStackHandler;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.List;

public class OxygenExtractorBlockEntity extends BlockEntity implements MenuProvider {

    // 常量
    public static final int BASE_RATE = 1;
    public static final int FE_PER_OXYGEN = 2;
    public static final int FE_PER_TANK_DAMAGE = 2;
    public static final int ENERGY_CAPACITY = 50_000;
    public static final int ENERGY_RECEIVE = 200;
    public static final int ENERGY_EXTRACT = 50_000;
    public static final double PLAYER_RADIUS = 8.0;
    public static final int OXYGEN_BUFFER_MAX = 10_000;
    public static final int TANK_REFILL_PER_TICK = 5;

    // 状态字段
    private int oxygenBuffer = 0;

    private final SimpleEnergyStorage energy =
            new SimpleEnergyStorage(ENERGY_CAPACITY, ENERGY_RECEIVE, ENERGY_EXTRACT);

    private final ItemStackHandler items = new ItemStackHandler(1) {
        @Override
        protected void onContentsChanged(int slot) {
            setChanged();
            if (level != null && !level.isClientSide()) {
                level.sendBlockUpdated(worldPosition, getBlockState(), getBlockState(), 3);
            }
        }
    };

    private final ContainerData data = new ContainerData() {
        @Override
        public int get(int index) {
            return switch (index) {
                case 0 -> getCurrentRate();
                case 1 -> getPlantMultiplier();
                case 2 -> energy.getEnergyStored();
                case 3 -> energy.getMaxEnergyStored();
                case 4 -> oxygenBuffer;
                case 5 -> OXYGEN_BUFFER_MAX;
                default -> 0;
            };
        }

        @Override
        public void set(int index, int value) {
            if (index == 2) energy.setEnergy(value);
            if (index == 4) oxygenBuffer = value;
        }

        @Override
        public int getCount() {
            return 6;
        }
    };

    // 构造
    public OxygenExtractorBlockEntity(BlockPos pos, BlockState state) {
        super(ModBlockEntities.OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR.get(), pos, state);
    }

    // 公共访问器
    public ItemStackHandler getItems() { return items; }
    public IEnergyStorage getEnergyStorage() { return energy; }

    // 静态判定
    public static boolean isPlant(ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return false;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.LEAVES)) return true;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.SAPLINGS)) return true;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.FLOWERS)) return true;
        if (stack.is(Items.KELP) || stack.is(Items.SEAGRASS)) return true;
        if (stack.is(Items.SHORT_GRASS) || stack.is(Items.FERN)) return true;
        if (stack.is(Items.WHEAT)) return true;
        if (stack.is(Items.SUGAR_CANE)) return true;
        return false;
    }

    public static boolean isOxygenTank(ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return false;
        return stack.is(ToolsItem.OXYGEN_TANK_BASIC.get())
                || stack.is(ToolsItem.OXYGEN_TANK_INTERMEDIATE.get())
                || stack.is(ToolsItem.OXYGEN_TANK_ADVANCED.get())
                || stack.is(ToolsItem.OXYGEN_TANK_ULTIMATE.get())
                || stack.is(ToolsItem.OXYGEN_TANK_SUPREME.get());
    }

    // 倍率与速率
    public int getPlantMultiplier() {
        ItemStack stack = items.getStackInSlot(0);
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return 1;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.LEAVES)) return 3;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.SAPLINGS)) return 5;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.FLOWERS)) return 5;
        if (stack.is(Items.KELP) || stack.is(Items.SEAGRASS)) return 8;
        if (stack.is(Items.SHORT_GRASS) || stack.is(Items.FERN)) return 3;
        if (stack.is(Items.WHEAT)) return 4;
        if (stack.is(Items.SUGAR_CANE)) return 4;
        return 1;
    }

    public int getCurrentRate() {
        return BASE_RATE * getPlantMultiplier();
    }

    // tick
    public void tick() {
        if (level == null || level.isClientSide()) return;
        if (!(level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel)) return;

        pullEnergy();

        ItemStack stack = items.getStackInSlot(0);
        boolean hasTank = isOxygenTank(stack) && stack.isDamageableItem() && stack.getDamageValue() > 0;
        boolean hasPlant = isPlant(stack);

        int rate = hasPlant ? getCurrentRate() : BASE_RATE;
        int feNeeded = rate * FE_PER_OXYGEN;

        boolean changed = false;

        // 产氧
        if (energy.getEnergyStored() >= feNeeded && oxygenBuffer < OXYGEN_BUFFER_MAX) {
            energy.extractEnergy(feNeeded, false);
            int space = OXYGEN_BUFFER_MAX - oxygenBuffer;
            int add = Math.min(rate, space);
            oxygenBuffer += add;
            changed = true;
        }

        // 充罐
        if (hasTank && oxygenBuffer > 0) {
            int damage = stack.getDamageValue();
            int refillMax = Math.min(damage, TANK_REFILL_PER_TICK);
            int canAfford = energy.getEnergyStored() / FE_PER_TANK_DAMAGE;
            int refill = Math.min(refillMax, Math.min(oxygenBuffer, canAfford));

            if (refill > 0) {
                stack.setDamageValue(damage - refill);
                oxygenBuffer -= refill;
                energy.extractEnergy(refill * FE_PER_TANK_DAMAGE, false);
                changed = true;
            }
        }

        // 给玩家
        if (!hasTank && oxygenBuffer > 0) {
            AABB area = new AABB(worldPosition).inflate(PLAYER_RADIUS);
            List<ServerPlayer> players = serverLevel.getEntitiesOfClass(ServerPlayer.class, area);
            for (ServerPlayer player : players) {
                if (oxygenBuffer <= 0) break;
                OxygenData oxygenData = player.getData(ModAttachments.OXYGEN.get());
                if (oxygenData.getTankMax() > 0 && oxygenData.getTankOxygen() < oxygenData.getTankMax()) {
                    int space = oxygenData.getTankMax() - oxygenData.getTankOxygen();
                    int give = Math.min(oxygenBuffer, space);
                    oxygenData.setTankOxygen(oxygenData.getTankOxygen() + give);
                    oxygenBuffer -= give;
                    changed = true;
                }
            }
        }

        if (changed) setChanged();
        if (level.getGameTime() % 20 == 0) {
            level.sendBlockUpdated(worldPosition, getBlockState(), getBlockState(), 3);
        }
    }

    // 能量拉取
    private void pullEnergy() {
        if (level == null) return;
        if (energy.getEnergyStored() >= energy.getMaxEnergyStored()) return;

        for (Direction dir : Direction.values()) {
            BlockPos neighbor = worldPosition.relative(dir);
            IEnergyStorage other = level.getCapability(
                    Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                    neighbor,
                    dir.getOpposite()
            );
            if (other != null && other.canExtract()) {
                int space = energy.getMaxEnergyStored() - energy.getEnergyStored();
                int want = Math.min(ENERGY_RECEIVE, space);
                int got = other.extractEnergy(want, false);
                if (got > 0) {
                    energy.receiveEnergy(got, false);
                    setChanged();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 掉落
    public void drops() {
        if (level == null) return;
        for (int i = 0; i < items.getSlots(); i++) {
            ItemStack stack = items.getStackInSlot(i);
            if (!stack.isEmpty()) {
                net.minecraft.world.Containers.dropItemStack(level,
                        worldPosition.getX() + 0.5,
                        worldPosition.getY() + 0.5,
                        worldPosition.getZ() + 0.5,
                        stack.copy());
                items.setStackInSlot(i, ItemStack.EMPTY);
            }
        }
    }

    // 菜单
    @Override
    public @NotNull Component getDisplayName() {
        return Component.translatable("block.newgc.oxygen_extractor");
    }

    @Override
    public AbstractContainerMenu createMenu(int id, @NotNull Inventory playerInv, @NotNull Player player) {
        return new OxygenExtractorMenu(id, playerInv, this, data);
    }

    // 存档
    @Override
    protected void saveAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.saveAdditional(tag, registries);
        tag.put("Items", items.serializeNBT(registries));
        tag.putInt("Energy", energy.getEnergyStored());
        tag.putInt("OxygenBuffer", oxygenBuffer);
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.loadAdditional(tag, registries);
        if (tag.contains("Items")) {
            items.deserializeNBT(registries, tag.getCompound("Items"));
        }
        energy.setEnergy(tag.getInt("Energy"));
        oxygenBuffer = tag.getInt("OxygenBuffer");
    }

    // 网络同步
    @Override
    public @NotNull CompoundTag getUpdateTag(HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();
        saveAdditional(tag, registries);
        return tag;
    }

    @Override
    public Packet<ClientGamePacketListener> getUpdatePacket() {
        return ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket.create(this);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDataPacket(@NotNull Connection net, ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket pkt, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        loadAdditional(pkt.getTag(), registries);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.energy.SimpleEnergyStorage;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.menu.SolidFuelGeneratorMenu;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.Connection;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ClientGamePacketListener;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket;
import net.minecraft.tags.ItemTags;
import net.minecraft.world.Containers;
import net.minecraft.world.MenuProvider;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.AbstractContainerMenu;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.ContainerData;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.energy.IEnergyStorage;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.items.ItemStackHandler;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity extends BlockEntity implements MenuProvider {

    public static final int CAPACITY = 100_000;
    public static final int GENERATE_PER_TICK = 40;

    private int burnTime = 0;
    private int maxBurnTime = 0;
    private int syncCooldown = 0;

    private final SimpleEnergyStorage energy =
            new SimpleEnergyStorage(CAPACITY, CAPACITY, CAPACITY);

    private final ItemStackHandler items = new ItemStackHandler(1) {
        @Override
        protected void onContentsChanged(int slot) {
            setChanged();
            if (level != null && !level.isClientSide()) {
                level.sendBlockUpdated(worldPosition, getBlockState(), getBlockState(), 3);
            }
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isItemValid(int slot, @NotNull ItemStack stack) {
            return getBurnTime(stack) > 0;
        }
    };

    private final ContainerData data = new ContainerData() {
        @Override
        public int get(int index) {
            return switch (index) {
                case 0 -> burnTime;
                case 1 -> maxBurnTime;
                case 2 -> energy.getEnergyStored();
                case 3 -> energy.getMaxEnergyStored();
                default -> 0;
            };
        }

        @Override
        public void set(int index, int value) {
            switch (index) {
                case 0 -> burnTime = value;
                case 1 -> maxBurnTime = value;
                case 2 -> energy.setEnergy(value);
            }
        }

        @Override
        public int getCount() {
            return 4;
        }
    };

    public SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity(BlockPos pos, BlockState state) {
        super(ModBlockEntities.SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR.get(), pos, state);
    }

    public IEnergyStorage getEnergyStorage() { return energy; }
    public ItemStackHandler getItems() { return items; }
    public ContainerData getData() { return data; }
    public int getEnergyStored() { return energy.getEnergyStored(); }
    public int getMaxEnergyStored() { return energy.getMaxEnergyStored(); }
    public int getBurnTime() { return burnTime; }
    public int getMaxBurnTime() { return maxBurnTime; }

    // ==================== 每 tick ====================
    public void tick() {
        if (level == null || level.isClientSide()) return;

        boolean changed = false;
        boolean energyFull = energy.getEnergyStored() >= energy.getMaxEnergyStored();

        if (burnTime > 0 && !energyFull) {
            burnTime--;
            energy.generate(GENERATE_PER_TICK);
            changed = true;
        }

        if (burnTime <= 0) {
            ItemStack fuel = items.getStackInSlot(0);
            int burn = getBurnTime(fuel);
            if (burn > 0) {
                burnTime = burn;
                maxBurnTime = burn;
                fuel.shrink(1);
                if (fuel.isEmpty()) {
                    items.setStackInSlot(0, ItemStack.EMPTY);
                }
                changed = true;
            }
        }

        if (changed) {
            setChanged();

            if (syncCooldown <= 0) {
                syncCooldown = 20;
                if (level != null) {
                    level.sendBlockUpdated(worldPosition, getBlockState(), getBlockState(), 3);
                }
            } else {
                syncCooldown--;
            }
        }
    }

    public void drops() {
        if (level == null) return;
        for (int i = 0; i < items.getSlots(); i++) {
            ItemStack stack = items.getStackInSlot(i);
            if (!stack.isEmpty()) {
                Containers.dropItemStack(level,
                        worldPosition.getX(),
                        worldPosition.getY(),
                        worldPosition.getZ(),
                        stack);
            }
        }
    }

    public static int getBurnTime(ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return 0;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.COALS)) return 1600;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.LOGS)) return 300;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.PLANKS)) return 300;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.WOODEN_STAIRS)) return 300;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.WOODEN_SLABS)) return 150;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.SAPLINGS)) return 100;
        if (stack.is(ItemTags.WOOL)) return 100;
        if (stack.is(Items.BLAZE_ROD)) return 2400;
        if (stack.is(Items.LAVA_BUCKET)) return 20000;
        if (stack.is(Items.COAL_BLOCK)) return 16000;
        if (stack.is(Items.DRIED_KELP_BLOCK)) return 4001;
        if (stack.is(Items.STICK)) return 100;
        if (stack.is(Items.BAMBOO)) return 50;
        return 0;
    }

    // ==================== MenuProvider ====================
    @Override
    public @NotNull Component getDisplayName() {
        return Component.translatable("block.newgc.solid_fuel_generator");
    }

    @Override
    public AbstractContainerMenu createMenu(int id, @NotNull Inventory playerInv, @NotNull Player player) {
        return new SolidFuelGeneratorMenu(id, playerInv, this, data);
    }

    // ==================== NBT ====================
    @Override
    protected void saveAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.saveAdditional(tag, registries);
        tag.putInt("BurnTime", burnTime);
        tag.putInt("MaxBurnTime", maxBurnTime);
        tag.putInt("Energy", energy.getEnergyStored());
        tag.put("Items", items.serializeNBT(registries));
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.loadAdditional(tag, registries);
        burnTime = tag.getInt("BurnTime");
        maxBurnTime = tag.getInt("MaxBurnTime");
        energy.setEnergy(tag.getInt("Energy"));
        if (tag.contains("Items")) {
            items.deserializeNBT(registries, tag.getCompound("Items"));
        }
    }

    // ==================== 网络同步 ====================
    @Override
    public @NotNull CompoundTag getUpdateTag(HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();
        saveAdditional(tag, registries);
        return tag;
    }

    @Override
    public Packet<ClientGamePacketListener> getUpdatePacket() {
        return ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket.create(this);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDataPacket(@NotNull Connection net, ClientboundBlockEntityDataPacket pkt, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        CompoundTag tag = pkt.getTag();
        loadAdditional(tag, registries);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\block\entity\WireBlockEntity.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.energy.SimpleEnergyStorage;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.capabilities.Capabilities;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.energy.IEnergyStorage;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class WireBlockEntity extends BlockEntity {

    public static final int CAPACITY = 5000;
    public static final int TRANSFER_PER_TICK = 500;

    private final SimpleEnergyStorage energy =
            new SimpleEnergyStorage(CAPACITY, TRANSFER_PER_TICK, TRANSFER_PER_TICK);

    public WireBlockEntity(BlockPos pos, BlockState state) {
        super(ModBlockEntities.WIRE.get(), pos, state);
    }

    public IEnergyStorage getEnergyStorage() {
        return energy;
    }

    public void tick() {
        if (level == null || level.isClientSide()) return;

        for (Direction dir : Direction.values()) {
            if (energy.getEnergyStored() >= energy.getMaxEnergyStored()) break;

            BlockPos neighborPos = worldPosition.relative(dir);
            IEnergyStorage other = level.getCapability(
                    Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                    neighborPos,
                    dir.getOpposite()
            );
            if (other == null || !other.canExtract()) continue;

            int space = energy.getMaxEnergyStored() - energy.getEnergyStored();
            int want = Math.min(TRANSFER_PER_TICK, space);
            int got = other.extractEnergy(want, true);   // simulate
            if (got <= 0) continue;
            got = other.extractEnergy(got, false);
            if (got > 0) {
                energy.receiveEnergy(got, false);
                setChanged();
            }
        }

        if (energy.getEnergyStored() <= 0) return;

        for (Direction dir : Direction.values()) {
            if (energy.getEnergyStored() <= 0) break;

            BlockPos neighborPos = worldPosition.relative(dir);
            IEnergyStorage other = level.getCapability(
                    Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                    neighborPos,
                    dir.getOpposite()
            );
            if (other == null || !other.canReceive()) continue;

            int available = energy.getEnergyStored();
            int want = Math.min(TRANSFER_PER_TICK, available);
            int sent = other.receiveEnergy(want, true);  // simulate
            if (sent <= 0) continue;
            sent = other.receiveEnergy(sent, false);
            if (sent > 0) {
                energy.extractEnergy(sent, false);
                setChanged();
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void saveAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.saveAdditional(tag, registries);
        tag.putInt("Energy", energy.getEnergyStored());
    }

    @Override
    protected void loadAdditional(@NotNull CompoundTag tag, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        super.loadAdditional(tag, registries);
        energy.setEnergy(tag.getInt("Energy"));
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\capability\CapabilitySetup.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.capability;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.ModBlockEntities;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.capabilities.Capabilities;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.capabilities.RegisterCapabilitiesEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class CapabilitySetup {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void registerCapabilities(RegisterCapabilitiesEvent event) {
        // 固体燃料发电机
        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );

        // 导线
        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.WIRE.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );

        // 制氧机
        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );

        // 压缩机
        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.COMPRESSOR.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\BlockHoverInfo.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.CompressorBlockEntity;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.OxygenExtractorBlockEntity;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class BlockHoverInfo {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.player == null || mc.level == null) return;
        if (mc.options.hideGui) return;

        HitResult hit = mc.hitResult;
        if (hit == null || hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.BLOCK) return;

        BlockHitResult blockHit = (BlockHitResult) hit;
        BlockPos pos = blockHit.getBlockPos();
        BlockEntity be = mc.level.getBlockEntity(pos);

        if (be instanceof SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity gen) {
            renderGeneratorInfo(event, mc, gen);
        } else if (be instanceof OxygenExtractorBlockEntity extractor) {
            renderExtractorInfo(event, mc, extractor);
        } else if (be instanceof CompressorBlockEntity compressor) {
            renderCompressorInfo(event, mc, compressor);
        }
    }

    private static void renderGeneratorInfo(RenderGuiEvent.Post event, Minecraft mc,
                                            SolidFuelGeneratorBlockEntity gen) {
        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int screenWidth = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int screenHeight = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        int centerX = screenWidth / 2;
        int centerY = screenHeight / 2 + 20;

        int stored = gen.getEnergyStorage().getEnergyStored();
        int max = gen.getEnergyStorage().getMaxEnergyStored();
        int burn = gen.getBurnTime();
        int maxBurn = gen.getMaxBurnTime();

        Component title = Component.translatable("block.newgc.solid_fuel_generator")
                .withStyle(ChatFormatting.AQUA);
        g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, title, centerX, centerY, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        Component energy = Component.literal("⚡ " + stored + " / " + max + " FE")
                .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD);
        g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, energy, centerX, centerY + 12, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        drawBar(g, centerX - 40, centerY + 26, 80, 6, stored, max, 0xFFFFB300);

        if (burn > 0) {
            Component burnText = Component.literal("🔥 燃烧中 " + burn + " tick")
                    .withStyle(ChatFormatting.RED);
            g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, burnText, centerX, centerY + 40, 0xFFFFFFFF);
            drawBar(g, centerX - 40, centerY + 52, 80, 4, burn, maxBurn, 0xFFFF5500);
        } else {
            Component idle = Component.literal("○ 待机").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GRAY);
            g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, idle, centerX, centerY + 40, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }
    }

    private static void renderExtractorInfo(RenderGuiEvent.Post event, Minecraft mc,
                                            OxygenExtractorBlockEntity ext) {
        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int screenWidth = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int screenHeight = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        int centerX = screenWidth / 2;
        int centerY = screenHeight / 2 + 20;

        int stored = ext.getEnergyStorage().getEnergyStored();
        int max = ext.getEnergyStorage().getMaxEnergyStored();
        int rate = ext.getCurrentRate();
        int mult = ext.getPlantMultiplier();

        Component title = Component.translatable("block.newgc.oxygen_extractor")
                .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GREEN);
        g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, title, centerX, centerY, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        Component energy = Component.literal("⚡ " + stored + " / " + max + " FE")
                .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD);
        g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, energy, centerX, centerY + 12, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        drawBar(g, centerX - 40, centerY + 26, 80, 6, stored, max, 0xFF00FF88);

        Component plantText = Component.literal("🌿 倍数 ×" + mult + "  |  速度 +" + rate + "/tick")
                .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GREEN);
        g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, plantText, centerX, centerY + 40, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        if (stored < rate * OxygenExtractorBlockEntity.FE_PER_OXYGEN) {
            Component warning = Component.literal("⚠ 能量不足").withStyle(ChatFormatting.RED);
            g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, warning, centerX, centerY + 54, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        } else {
            Component ok = Component.literal("● 运行中").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GREEN);
            g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, ok, centerX, centerY + 54, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }
    }

    private static void renderCompressorInfo(RenderGuiEvent.Post event, Minecraft mc,
                                             CompressorBlockEntity compressor) {
        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int screenWidth = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int screenHeight = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        int centerX = screenWidth / 2;
        int centerY = screenHeight / 2 + 20;

        int stored = compressor.getEnergyStorage().getEnergyStored();
        int max = compressor.getEnergyStorage().getMaxEnergyStored();
        int progress = compressor.getProgress();

        Component title = Component.translatable("block.newgc.compressor")
                .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD);
        g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, title, centerX, centerY, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        Component energy = Component.literal("⚡ " + stored + " / " + max + " FE")
                .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD);
        g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, energy, centerX, centerY + 12, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        drawBar(g, centerX - 40, centerY + 26, 80, 6, stored, max, 0xFFFF8C00);

        if (progress > 0) {
            Component progressText = Component.literal("⇉ 压缩中 " + progress + " / "
                            + compressor.getMaxProgress())
                    .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD);
            g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, progressText, centerX, centerY + 40, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        } else {
            Component idle = Component.literal("○ 待机").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GRAY);
            g.drawCenteredString(mc.font, idle, centerX, centerY + 40, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }
    }

    private static void drawBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, int h, int stored, int max, int fillColor) {
        g.fill(x - 1, y - 1, x + w + 1, y + h + 1, 0xFF000000);
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h, 0xFF101010);
        if (max > 0 && stored > 0) {
            int fillW = (int) ((float) stored / max * w);
            g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + h, fillColor);
        }
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, 0xFF00B8D0);
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, 0xFF00B8D0);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, 0xFF00B8D0);
        g.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, 0xFF00B8D0);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug.HudInteractionState;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.config.NewGalaxyConfig;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.ResetTankPacket;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.item.ToolsItem;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.gui.StarMapScreen;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import net.minecraft.client.KeyMapping;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RegisterKeyMappingsEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ScreenEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.settings.KeyConflictContext;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class KeyBindings {

    public static final KeyMapping OPEN_STAR_MAP = new KeyMapping(
            "key.newgc.star_map",
            KeyConflictContext.IN_GAME,
            InputConstants.Type.KEYSYM,
            GLFW.GLFW_KEY_M,
            "key.categories.newgc"
    );

    public static final KeyMapping RESET_TANK = new KeyMapping(
            "key.newgc.reset_tank",
            KeyConflictContext.IN_GAME,
            InputConstants.Type.KEYSYM,
            GLFW.GLFW_KEY_R,
            "key.categories.newgc"
    );

    // ===== 新增：左 Alt 呼出鼠标（仅调试模式有效）=====
    public static final KeyMapping TOGGLE_HUD_MOUSE = new KeyMapping(
            "key.newgc.toggle_hud_mouse",
            KeyConflictContext.IN_GAME,
            InputConstants.Type.KEYSYM,
            GLFW.GLFW_KEY_LEFT_ALT,
            "key.categories.newgc"
    );

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void registerKeys(RegisterKeyMappingsEvent event) {
        event.register(OPEN_STAR_MAP);
        event.register(RESET_TANK);
        event.register(TOGGLE_HUD_MOUSE);   // ← 新增
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onKeyInput(InputEvent.Key event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.player == null) return;

        if (OPEN_STAR_MAP.consumeClick()) {
            ItemStack mainHand = mc.player.getMainHandItem();
            ItemStack offHand = mc.player.getOffhandItem();

            boolean holdingTeleporter =
                    mainHand.is(ToolsItem.TELEPORTER.get()) ||
                            offHand.is(ToolsItem.TELEPORTER.get());

            if (!holdingTeleporter) {
                mc.player.displayClientMessage(
                        Component.translatable("message.newgc.need_teleporter"),
                        true
                );
                return;
            }

            mc.setScreen(new StarMapScreen());
        }

        if (RESET_TANK.consumeClick()) {
            PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new ResetTankPacket());
        }

        // ===== 新增：左 Alt 呼出/锁定鼠标 =====
        // 只有开启调试模式才响应
        if (NewGalaxyConfig.CLIENT.debugMode.get() && TOGGLE_HUD_MOUSE.consumeClick()) {
            HudInteractionState.toggle();
        }
    }

    // ===== 新增：打开任意 GUI 时自动锁定鼠标 =====
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onScreenOpening(ScreenEvent.Opening event) {
        if (event.getNewScreen() != null) {
            HudInteractionState.deactivate();
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\debug\DebugState.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug;

public final class DebugState {

    // ===== 木星 =====
    public static final int JUPITER_NONE   = -1;
    public static final int JUPITER_CALM   = 0;
    public static final int JUPITER_RISING = 1;
    public static final int JUPITER_PEAK   = 2;
    public static final int JUPITER_SUDDEN = 3;
    public static final int JUPITER_FALLING= 4;

    private static int jupiterTarget = JUPITER_NONE;
    private static float jupiterTargetStrength = -1f;
    private static float jupiterSmoothed = -1f;

    // ===== 火星沙尘暴 =====
    public static final int MARS_DUST_NONE    = -1;
    public static final int MARS_DUST_CALM    = 0;
    public static final int MARS_DUST_RISING  = 1;
    public static final int MARS_DUST_PEAK    = 2;
    public static final int MARS_DUST_FALLING = 3;

    private static int marsDustTarget = MARS_DUST_NONE;
    private static float marsDustTargetStrength = -1f;
    private static float marsDustSmoothed = -1f;

    // ===== 水星耀斑 =====
    public static final int MERCURY_FLARE_NONE    = -1;
    public static final int MERCURY_FLARE_CALM    = 0;
    public static final int MERCURY_FLARE_BUILDUP = 1;
    public static final int MERCURY_FLARE_FLARE   = 2;
    public static final int MERCURY_FLARE_DECAY   = 3;

    private static int mercuryFlareTarget = MERCURY_FLARE_NONE;
    private static float mercuryFlareTargetIntensity = -1f;
    private static float mercuryFlareSmoothed = -1f;

    private DebugState() {}

    // ===== 木星 =====
    public static void setJupiterPhase(int phase) {
        jupiterTarget = phase;
        jupiterTargetStrength = switch (phase) {
            case JUPITER_CALM    -> 0.05f;
            case JUPITER_RISING  -> 0.55f;
            case JUPITER_PEAK    -> 1.25f;
            case JUPITER_SUDDEN  -> 2.3f;
            case JUPITER_FALLING -> 0.55f;
            default -> -1f;
        };
        if (jupiterTargetStrength < 0) {
            jupiterSmoothed = -1f;
        } else if (jupiterSmoothed < 0) {
            jupiterSmoothed = 0f;
        }
    }

    public static int getJupiterPhase() { return jupiterTarget; }
    public static float getJupiterForcedStrength() { return jupiterTargetStrength; }

    public static float tickJupiterSmooth() {
        if (jupiterTargetStrength < 0) {
            jupiterSmoothed = -1f;
            return -1f;
        }
        if (jupiterSmoothed < 0) {
            jupiterSmoothed = 0f;
        }
        jupiterSmoothed += (jupiterTargetStrength - jupiterSmoothed) * 0.03f;
        return jupiterSmoothed;
    }

    // ===== 火星沙尘暴 =====
    public static void setMarsDustPhase(int phase) {
        marsDustTarget = phase;
        marsDustTargetStrength = switch (phase) {
            case MARS_DUST_CALM    -> 0.05f;
            case MARS_DUST_RISING  -> 0.5f;
            case MARS_DUST_PEAK    -> 1.3f;
            case MARS_DUST_FALLING -> 0.5f;
            default -> -1f;
        };
        if (marsDustTargetStrength < 0) {
            marsDustSmoothed = -1f;
        } else if (marsDustSmoothed < 0) {
            marsDustSmoothed = 0f;
        }
    }

    public static int getMarsDustPhase() { return marsDustTarget; }
    public static float getMarsDustForcedStrength() { return marsDustTargetStrength; }

    public static float tickMarsDustSmooth() {
        if (marsDustTargetStrength < 0) {
            marsDustSmoothed = -1f;
            return -1f;
        }
        if (marsDustSmoothed < 0) {
            marsDustSmoothed = 0f;
        }
        marsDustSmoothed += (marsDustTargetStrength - marsDustSmoothed) * 0.03f;
        return marsDustSmoothed;
    }

    // ===== 水星耀斑 =====
    public static void setMercuryFlarePhase(int phase) {
        mercuryFlareTarget = phase;
        mercuryFlareTargetIntensity = switch (phase) {
            case MERCURY_FLARE_CALM    -> 0.0f;
            case MERCURY_FLARE_BUILDUP -> 0.6f;
            case MERCURY_FLARE_FLARE   -> 1.3f;
            case MERCURY_FLARE_DECAY   -> 0.4f;
            default -> -1f;
        };
        if (mercuryFlareTargetIntensity < 0) {
            mercuryFlareSmoothed = -1f;
        } else if (mercuryFlareSmoothed < 0) {
            mercuryFlareSmoothed = 0f;
        }
    }

    public static int getMercuryFlarePhase() { return mercuryFlareTarget; }
    public static float getMercuryFlareForcedIntensity() { return mercuryFlareTargetIntensity; }

    public static float tickMercuryFlareSmooth() {
        if (mercuryFlareTargetIntensity < 0) {
            mercuryFlareSmoothed = -1f;
            return -1f;
        }
        if (mercuryFlareSmoothed < 0) {
            mercuryFlareSmoothed = 0f;
        }
        mercuryFlareSmoothed += (mercuryFlareTargetIntensity - mercuryFlareSmoothed) * 0.05f;
        return mercuryFlareSmoothed;
    }

    // ===== 恢复 =====
    public static void resetAll() {
        jupiterTarget = JUPITER_NONE;
        jupiterTargetStrength = -1f;
        jupiterSmoothed = -1f;

        marsDustTarget = MARS_DUST_NONE;
        marsDustTargetStrength = -1f;
        marsDustSmoothed = -1f;

        mercuryFlareTarget = MERCURY_FLARE_NONE;
        mercuryFlareTargetIntensity = -1f;
        mercuryFlareSmoothed = -1f;
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\debug\HudInteractionState.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;

public final class HudInteractionState {

    private static boolean active = false;

    private HudInteractionState() {}

    public static boolean isActive() {
        return active;
    }

    public static void toggle() {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.screen != null) return;
        if (mc.level == null) return;

        active = !active;

        if (active) {
            mc.mouseHandler.releaseMouse();
        } else {
            mc.mouseHandler.grabMouse();
        }
    }

    public static void deactivate() {
        if (!active) return;
        active = false;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        // 断开连接 / 有 GUI 时不调 grabMouse
        if (mc.screen != null) return;
        if (mc.level == null) return;

        mc.mouseHandler.grabMouse();
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\gui\CompressorScreen.java
