package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.gui;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.menu.CompressorMenu;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractContainerScreen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class CompressorScreen extends AbstractContainerScreen<CompressorMenu> {

    private static final int ENERGY_X = 10;
    private static final int ENERGY_Y = 20;
    private static final int ENERGY_W = 16;
    private static final int ENERGY_H = 46;

    private static final int PROGRESS_X = 78;
    private static final int PROGRESS_Y = 37;
    private static final int PROGRESS_W = 34;
    private static final int PROGRESS_H = 10;

    public CompressorScreen(CompressorMenu menu, Inventory inv, Component title) {
        super(menu, inv, title);
        this.imageWidth = 176;
        this.imageHeight = 166;
        this.inventoryLabelY = 72;
    }

    @Override
    protected void renderBg(@NotNull GuiGraphics g, float partialTick, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        int x = (width - imageWidth) / 2;
        int y = (height - imageHeight) / 2;

        // 背景渐变
        for (int i = 0; i < imageHeight; i++) {
            float t = (float) i / imageHeight;
            int r = (int) (14 + 14 * t);
            int gr = (int) (16 + 16 * t);
            int b = (int) (22 + 20 * t);
            g.fill(x, y + i, x + imageWidth, y + i + 1, 0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b);
        }

        // 网格
        for (int gx = 0; gx < imageWidth; gx += 8) {
            g.fill(x + gx, y, x + gx + 1, y + imageHeight, 0x10FFFFFF);
        }
        for (int gy = 0; gy < imageHeight; gy += 8) {
            g.fill(x, y + gy, x + imageWidth, y + gy + 1, 0x10FFFFFF);
        }

        // 边框
        g.fill(x, y, x + imageWidth, y + 2, 0xFFFF8C00);
        g.fill(x, y + imageHeight - 2, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFFFF8C00);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 2, y + imageHeight, 0xFFFF8C00);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - 2, y, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFFFF8C00);

        // 四角
        int corner = 8;
        g.fill(x, y, x + corner, y + 4, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 4, y + corner, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - corner, y, x + imageWidth, y + 4, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - 4, y, x + imageWidth, y + corner, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(x, y + imageHeight - 4, x + corner, y + imageHeight, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(x, y + imageHeight - corner, x + 4, y + imageHeight, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - corner, y + imageHeight - 4, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - 4, y + imageHeight - corner, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFFFFB040);

        // 输入/输出槽
        drawSlot(g, x + 56, y + 34);
        drawSlot(g, x + 116, y + 34);

        // 玩家背包槽
        for (int row = 0; row < 3; row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < 9; col++) {
                int sx = x + 7 + col * 18;
                int sy = y + 83 + row * 18;
                g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0xFF0A1418);
                g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 1, 0x60FFB040);
                g.fill(sx, sy + 17, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x60FFB040);
                g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 1, sy + 18, 0x60FFB040);
                g.fill(sx + 17, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x60FFB040);
            }
        }
        for (int col = 0; col < 9; col++) {
            int sx = x + 7 + col * 18;
            int sy = y + 141;
            g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0xFF0A1418);
            g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 1, 0x60FFB040);
            g.fill(sx, sy + 17, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x60FFB040);
            g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 1, sy + 18, 0x60FFB040);
            g.fill(sx + 17, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x60FFB040);
        }

        // 能量条
        drawBar(g, x + ENERGY_X, y + ENERGY_Y, ENERGY_W, ENERGY_H,
                menu.getEnergyStored(), menu.getMaxEnergyStored(),
                0xFFFF8C00, 0x30FF8C00, 0x80FFB040, true);

        // 进度条
        drawProgressBar(g, x + PROGRESS_X, y + PROGRESS_Y, PROGRESS_W, PROGRESS_H,
                menu.getProgress(), menu.getMaxProgress());

        // ===== 标注文字 =====
        g.drawCenteredString(font, "能量", x + 18, y + 8, 0xFFFFB040);          // 能量条上方
        g.drawCenteredString(font, "进度", x + 95, y + 25, 0xFFFFB040);         // 进度条上方
        g.drawCenteredString(font, "输入", x + 65, y + 55, 0xFFD8D8D8);         // 输入槽下方
        g.drawCenteredString(font, "输出", x + 125, y + 55, 0xFFD8D8D8);        // 输出槽下方
    }

    private void drawSlot(GuiGraphics g, int sx, int sy) {
        g.fill(sx - 2, sy - 2, sx + 20, sy + 20, 0x40FF8C00);
        g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0xFF0A1418);
        g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 1, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(sx, sy + 17, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 1, sy + 18, 0xFFFFB040);
        g.fill(sx + 17, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0xFFFFB040);
    }

    private void drawBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, int h,
                         int stored, int max,
                         int borderColor, int glowColor, int tickColor,
                         boolean tickLeft) {
        g.fill(x - 2, y - 2, x + w + 2, y + h + 2, glowColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h, 0xFF0A1418);

        for (int i = 1; i < 6; i++) {
            int gy = y + (h * i / 6);
            g.fill(x + 1, gy, x + w - 1, gy + 1, 0x20000000 | (borderColor & 0x00FFFFFF));
        }

        if (max > 0 && stored > 0) {
            int fillH = (int) ((float) stored / max * (h - 4));
            int fillY = y + h - 2 - fillH;
            int baseR = (borderColor >> 16) & 0xFF;
            int baseG = (borderColor >> 8) & 0xFF;
            int baseB = borderColor & 0xFF;
            for (int i = 0; i < fillH; i++) {
                float t = (float) i / fillH;
                int r = (int) (baseR * (0.4f + 0.6f * t));
                int gr = (int) (baseG * (0.4f + 0.6f * t));
                int b = (int) (baseB * (0.4f + 0.6f * t));
                g.fill(x + 2, fillY + i, x + w - 2, fillY + i + 1,
                        0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b);
            }
            g.fill(x + 2, fillY, x + w - 2, fillY + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }

        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, borderColor);
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, borderColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, borderColor);
        g.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, borderColor);

        for (int i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
            int ty = y + (h * i / 4);
            if (tickLeft) {
                g.fill(x - 2, ty, x, ty + 1, tickColor);
            } else {
                g.fill(x + w, ty, x + w + 2, ty + 1, tickColor);
            }
        }
    }

    private void drawProgressBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, int h, int progress, int max) {
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h, 0xFF0A1418);
        if (max > 0 && progress > 0) {
            int fillW = (int) ((float) progress / max * w);
            for (int i = 0; i < fillW; i++) {
                float t = (float) i / w;
                int r = 255;
                int gr = (int) (90 + 130 * t);
                int b = 0;
                g.fill(x + i, y + 1, x + i + 1, y + h - 1, 0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b);
            }
        }
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, 0xFFFF8C00);
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, 0xFFFF8C00);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, 0xFFFF8C00);
        g.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, 0xFFFF8C00);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(GuiGraphics g, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        renderBackground(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
        super.render(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);

        int x = (width - imageWidth) / 2;
        int y = (height - imageHeight) / 2;

        if (isHovering(mouseX, mouseY, x + ENERGY_X, y + ENERGY_Y, ENERGY_W, ENERGY_H)) {
            drawTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY,
                    "⚡ " + menu.getEnergyStored() + " / " + menu.getMaxEnergyStored() + " FE",
                    0xFFFFB040);
        }

        if (isHovering(mouseX, mouseY, x + PROGRESS_X, y + PROGRESS_Y, PROGRESS_W, PROGRESS_H)) {
            drawTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY,
                    "进度 " + menu.getProgress() + " / " + menu.getMaxProgress(),
                    0xFFFF8C00);
        }

        renderTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY);
    }

    private boolean isHovering(int mouseX, int mouseY, int x, int y, int w, int h) {
        return mouseX >= x && mouseX <= x + w && mouseY >= y && mouseY <= y + h;
    }

    private void drawTooltip(GuiGraphics g, int mouseX, int mouseY, String text, int color) {
        int tw = font.width(text);
        int tx = mouseX + 8;
        int ty = mouseY - 4;
        int bw = tw + 8;
        int bh = 14;

        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty + bh, 0xE0000D14);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty, color);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty + bh - 1, tx + bw, ty + bh, color);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx, ty + bh, color);
        g.fill(tx + bw - 1, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty + bh, color);

        g.drawString(font, text, tx + 2, ty + 2, color, true);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\gui\MagnetometerHud.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.gui;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.magnetometer.MagnetometerClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.magnetometer.MagnetometerData;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.item.ToolsItem;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MagnetometerHud {

    // ===== 布局 =====
    private static final int WIDTH  = 150;
    private static final int HEIGHT = 66;
    private static final int MARGIN_LEFT   = 10;
    private static final int MARGIN_BOTTOM = 90;

    // ===== 主色 =====
    private static final int COLOR_BG          = 0xD0081420;
    private static final int COLOR_BORDER      = 0xFF3AA0C8;
    private static final int COLOR_ACCENT      = 0xFF4AD8FF;
    private static final int COLOR_GLOW        = 0x403AA0C8;
    private static final int COLOR_TITLE       = 0xFFA0F0FF;
    private static final int COLOR_LABEL       = 0xFF8898B0;
    private static final int COLOR_VALUE       = 0xFFE0F8FF;
    private static final int COLOR_SEPARATOR   = 0x604AD8FF;
    private static final int COLOR_TITLE_BG    = 0xFF0A1A2A;

    private static final int COLOR_BAR_BG      = 0xFF060C14;
    private static final int COLOR_BAR_BORDER  = 0xFF1A2A3C;

    private static final int COLOR_STRENGTH_WEAK    = 0xFF4A5A6E;
    private static final int COLOR_STRENGTH_LOW     = 0xFF7A8FA6;
    private static final int COLOR_STRENGTH_MID     = 0xFF5A9BD0;
    private static final int COLOR_STRENGTH_HIGH    = 0xFFE0A050;
    private static final int COLOR_STRENGTH_EXTREME = 0xFFFF5B3A;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;
        if (mc.options.hideGui) return;

        ItemStack main = mc.player.getMainHandItem();
        ItemStack off  = mc.player.getOffhandItem();
        boolean holding = main.is(ToolsItem.MAGNETOMETER.get())
                || off.is(ToolsItem.MAGNETOMETER.get());
        if (!holding) return;

        MagnetometerClientCache.tick();

        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int screenH = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        int x0 = MARGIN_LEFT;
        int y0 = screenH - HEIGHT - MARGIN_BOTTOM;
        int x1 = x0 + WIDTH;
        int y1 = y0 + HEIGHT;

        g.pose().pushPose();
        g.pose().last().pose().identity();

        // 外发光
        g.fill(x0 - 2, y0 - 2, x1 + 2, y1 + 2, COLOR_GLOW);

        // 背景
        g.fill(x0, y0, x1, y1, COLOR_BG);

        // 边框
        drawRect2(g, x0, y0, x1, y1, COLOR_BORDER);

        // 四角装饰
        drawCornerBrackets(g, x0, y0, x1, y1, COLOR_ACCENT);

        // ===== 标题行（0~12）=====
        g.fill(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, x1 - 1, y0 + 12, COLOR_TITLE_BG);
        g.fill(x0 + 1, y0 + 11, x1 - 1, y0 + 12, COLOR_SEPARATOR);

        g.drawString(mc.font, "⧗ 磁场计", x0 + 6, y0 + 3, COLOR_TITLE, false);

        float strength = MagnetometerClientCache.getStrength();

        // 强度等级（右上角，短文字）
        String level = MagnetometerData.levelText(strength);
        int lvlW = mc.font.width(level);
        g.drawString(mc.font, level, x1 - 6 - lvlW, y0 + 3,
                MagnetometerClientCache.getStatusColor(), false);

        // ===== 强度条（14~21）=====
        int barX = x0 + 6;
        int barY = y0 + 14;
        int barW = WIDTH - 12;
        int barH = 7;

        g.fill(barX, barY, barX + barW, barY + barH, COLOR_BAR_BG);

        float t = MagnetometerData.normalize(strength);
        int fillW = (int) (barW * t);
        int fillColor = strengthColor(strength);

        if (fillW > 0) {
            g.fill(barX, barY, barX + fillW, barY + barH, fillColor);
            g.fill(barX, barY, barX + fillW, barY + 1, lighten(fillColor, 0x40));
            g.fill(barX, barY + barH - 1, barX + fillW, barY + barH, darken(fillColor, 0x40));
        }

        // 刻度
        for (int i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
            int tickX = barX + barW * i / 6;
            g.fill(tickX, barY, tickX + 1, barY + barH, 0x50FFFFFF);
        }

        drawRect2(g, barX, barY, barX + barW, barY + barH, COLOR_BAR_BORDER);

        // ===== 数值行（24~32）=====
        int valueY = barY + barH + 3;
        String strengthStr = String.format("%.2f", strength);
        g.drawString(mc.font, strengthStr, barX, valueY, COLOR_VALUE, false);

        // 右侧显示"噪声"（小字）
        String noiseStr = String.format("n=%.2f", MagnetometerClientCache.getNoise());
        int noiseW = mc.font.width(noiseStr);
        g.drawString(mc.font, noiseStr, barX + barW - noiseW, valueY, COLOR_LABEL, false);

        // ===== 下半部分（36~64）=====
        int halfY = y0 + 36;

        // 罗盘（半径缩到 11）
        int compassCX = x0 + 20;
        int compassCY = halfY + 10;
        int compassR = 11;
        drawCompassRose(g, compassCX, compassCY, compassR);

        // 右侧文字
        int infoX = x0 + 42;
        int infoY = halfY;
        int infoRight = x1 - 6;

        // 朝向
        float angle = MagnetometerClientCache.getAngle();
        String arrow = angleToArrow(angle);
        String angleStr = String.format("%s %d°", arrow, (int) angle);
        drawRow(g, mc, infoX, infoY, infoRight, "朝向", angleStr, COLOR_VALUE);
        infoY += 11;

        // 状态
        drawRow(g, mc, infoX, infoY, infoRight, "状态",
                MagnetometerClientCache.getStatus(),
                MagnetometerClientCache.getStatusColor());

        // 矿脉提示（如果检测到，放最下方一行）
        if (MagnetometerClientCache.isIronNearby()) {
            g.drawString(mc.font, "🜨 铁矿", infoX, infoY + 12, 0xFFC89650, false);
        }

        g.pose().popPose();
    }

    // ================= 罗盘（简化版）=================
    private static void drawCompassRose(GuiGraphics g, int cx, int cy, int r) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();

        // 外圈
        drawCircleOutline(g, cx, cy, r, COLOR_BORDER);

        // 十字线
        g.fill(cx - r, cy, cx + r, cy + 1, 0x40FFFFFF);
        g.fill(cx, cy - r, cx + 1, cy + r, 0x40FFFFFF);

        // 方位标记（用点代替字母，更紧凑）
        g.fill(cx - 1, cy - r - 2, cx + 1, cy - r, 0xFFFF8080);  // N（红点）
        g.fill(cx - 1, cy + r, cx + 1, cy + r + 2, 0xFF8898B0);  // S（灰点）
        g.fill(cx - r - 2, cy - 1, cx - r, cy + 1, 0xFF8898B0);  // W（灰点）
        g.fill(cx + r, cy - 1, cx + r + 2, cy + 1, 0xFF8898B0);  // E（灰点）

        // 指针
        float angle = MagnetometerClientCache.getAngle();
        double rad = Math.toRadians(angle - 90);
        int px = cx + (int) (Math.cos(rad) * (r - 3));
        int py = cy + (int) (Math.sin(rad) * (r - 3));
        drawLine(g, cx, cy, px, py, 0xFFFF4040);

        // 中心点
        g.fill(cx - 1, cy - 1, cx + 1, cy + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF);
    }

    // ================= 工具 =================
    private static void drawRow(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y, int rightEdge,
                                String label, String value, int valueColor) {
        g.drawString(mc.font, label, x, y, COLOR_LABEL, false);
        int vW = mc.font.width(value);
        g.drawString(mc.font, value, rightEdge - vW, y, valueColor, false);
    }

    private static void drawRect2(GuiGraphics g, int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, int color) {
        g.fill(x1, y1, x2, y1 + 2, color);
        g.fill(x1, y2 - 2, x2, y2, color);
        g.fill(x1, y1, x1 + 2, y2, color);
        g.fill(x2 - 2, y1, x2, y2, color);
    }

    private static void drawCornerBrackets(GuiGraphics g, int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, int color) {
        int s = 6, t = 2;
        g.fill(x1, y1, x1 + s, y1 + t, color);
        g.fill(x1, y1, x1 + t, y1 + s, color);
        g.fill(x2 - s, y1, x2, y1 + t, color);
        g.fill(x2 - t, y1, x2, y1 + s, color);
        g.fill(x1, y2 - t, x1 + s, y2, color);
        g.fill(x1, y2 - s, x1 + t, y2, color);
        g.fill(x2 - s, y2 - t, x2, y2, color);
        g.fill(x2 - t, y2 - s, x2, y2, color);
    }

    private static void drawCircleOutline(GuiGraphics g, int cx, int cy, int r, int color) {
        int segments = 48;
        for (int i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            double t0 = (i / (double) segments) * Math.PI * 2;
            int px = cx + (int) Math.round(Math.cos(t0) * r);
            int py = cy + (int) Math.round(Math.sin(t0) * r);
            g.fill(px, py, px + 1, py + 1, color);
        }
    }

    private static void drawLine(GuiGraphics g, int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1, int color) {
        int dx = Math.abs(x1 - x0);
        int dy = Math.abs(y1 - y0);
        int sx = x0 < x1 ? 1 : -1;
        int sy = y0 < y1 ? 1 : -1;
        int err = dx - dy;

        while (true) {
            g.fill(x0, y0, x0 + 1, y0 + 1, color);
            if (x0 == x1 && y0 == y1) break;
            int e2 = 2 * err;
            if (e2 > -dy) { err -= dy; x0 += sx; }
            if (e2 < dx)  { err += dx; y0 += sy; }
        }
    }

    private static int strengthColor(float s) {
        if (s < 0.05f) return COLOR_STRENGTH_WEAK;
        if (s < 0.5f)  return COLOR_STRENGTH_LOW;
        if (s < 1.5f)  return COLOR_STRENGTH_MID;
        if (s < 2.2f)  return COLOR_STRENGTH_HIGH;
        return COLOR_STRENGTH_EXTREME;
    }

    private static String angleToArrow(float angle) {
        int sector = (int) ((angle + 22.5f) / 45f) % 8;
        if (sector < 0) sector += 8;
        return switch (sector) {
            case 0 -> "↑";
            case 1 -> "↗";
            case 2 -> "→";
            case 3 -> "↘";
            case 4 -> "↓";
            case 5 -> "↙";
            case 6 -> "←";
            default -> "↖";
        };
    }

    private static int lighten(int color, int amount) {
        int a = (color >>> 24) & 0xFF;
        int r = Math.min(255, ((color >>> 16) & 0xFF) + amount);
        int g = Math.min(255, ((color >>> 8) & 0xFF) + amount);
        int b = Math.min(255, (color & 0xFF) + amount);
        return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
    }

    private static int darken(int color, int amount) {
        int a = (color >>> 24) & 0xFF;
        int r = Math.max(0, ((color >>> 16) & 0xFF) - amount);
        int g = Math.max(0, ((color >>> 8) & 0xFF) - amount);
        int b = Math.max(0, (color & 0xFF) - amount);
        return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.gui;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.menu.OxygenExtractorMenu;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractContainerScreen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class OxygenExtractorScreen extends AbstractContainerScreen<OxygenExtractorMenu> {

    private static final int ENERGY_X = 10;
    private static final int ENERGY_Y = 20;
    private static final int ENERGY_W = 16;
    private static final int ENERGY_H = 46;

    private static final int OXYGEN_X = 150;
    private static final int OXYGEN_Y = 20;
    private static final int OXYGEN_W = 16;
    private static final int OXYGEN_H = 46;

    public OxygenExtractorScreen(OxygenExtractorMenu menu, Inventory inv, Component title) {
        super(menu, inv, title);
        this.imageWidth = 176;
        this.imageHeight = 166;
        this.inventoryLabelY = 72;
    }

    @Override
    protected void renderBg(@NotNull GuiGraphics g, float partialTick, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        int x = (width - imageWidth) / 2;
        int y = (height - imageHeight) / 2;

        // 背景
        for (int i = 0; i < imageHeight; i++) {
            float t = (float) i / imageHeight;
            int r = (int) (8 + 20 * t);
            int gr = (int) (20 + 30 * t);
            int b = (int) (25 + 20 * t);
            g.fill(x, y + i, x + imageWidth, y + i + 1, 0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b);
        }

        // 网格
        for (int gx = 0; gx < imageWidth; gx += 8) {
            g.fill(x + gx, y, x + gx + 1, y + imageHeight, 0x10FFFFFF);
        }
        for (int gy = 0; gy < imageHeight; gy += 8) {
            g.fill(x, y + gy, x + imageWidth, y + gy + 1, 0x10FFFFFF);
        }

        // 边框
        g.fill(x, y, x + imageWidth, y + 2, 0xFF00FF88);
        g.fill(x, y + imageHeight - 2, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFF00FF88);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 2, y + imageHeight, 0xFF00FF88);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - 2, y, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFF00FF88);

        // 四角
        int corner = 8;
        g.fill(x, y, x + corner, y + 4, 0xFF00FFCC);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 4, y + corner, 0xFF00FFCC);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - corner, y, x + imageWidth, y + 4, 0xFF00FFCC);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - 4, y, x + imageWidth, y + corner, 0xFF00FFCC);
        g.fill(x, y + imageHeight - 4, x + corner, y + imageHeight, 0xFF00FFCC);
        g.fill(x, y + imageHeight - corner, x + 4, y + imageHeight, 0xFF00FFCC);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - corner, y + imageHeight - 4, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFF00FFCC);
        g.fill(x + imageWidth - 4, y + imageHeight - corner, x + imageWidth, y + imageHeight, 0xFF00FFCC);

        // 植物槽
        int slotX = x + 79;
        int slotY = y + 34;
        g.fill(slotX - 2, slotY - 2, slotX + 20, slotY + 20, 0x4000FF88);
        g.fill(slotX, slotY, slotX + 18, slotY + 18, 0xFF0A1818);
        g.fill(slotX, slotY, slotX + 18, slotY + 1, 0xFF00FF88);
        g.fill(slotX, slotY + 17, slotX + 18, slotY + 18, 0xFF00FF88);
        g.fill(slotX, slotY, slotX + 1, slotY + 18, 0xFF00FF88);
        g.fill(slotX + 17, slotY, slotX + 18, slotY + 18, 0xFF00FF88);

        // 玩家背包
        for (int row = 0; row < 3; row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < 9; col++) {
                int sx = x + 7 + col * 18;
                int sy = y + 83 + row * 18;
                g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0xFF0A1818);
                g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 1, 0x6000FF88);
                g.fill(sx, sy + 17, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x6000FF88);
                g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 1, sy + 18, 0x6000FF88);
                g.fill(sx + 17, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x6000FF88);
            }
        }
        for (int col = 0; col < 9; col++) {
            int sx = x + 7 + col * 18;
            int sy = y + 141;
            g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0xFF0A1818);
            g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 18, sy + 1, 0x6000FF88);
            g.fill(sx, sy + 17, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x6000FF88);
            g.fill(sx, sy, sx + 1, sy + 18, 0x6000FF88);
            g.fill(sx + 17, sy, sx + 18, sy + 18, 0x6000FF88);
        }

        // 能量条
        drawBar(g, x + ENERGY_X, y + ENERGY_Y, ENERGY_W, ENERGY_H,
                menu.getEnergyStored(), menu.getMaxEnergyStored(),
                0xFF00FF88, 0x3000FF88, 0x8000FF88, true);

        // 氧气条
        drawBar(g, x + OXYGEN_X, y + OXYGEN_Y, OXYGEN_W, OXYGEN_H,
                menu.getOxygenBuffer(), menu.getOxygenBufferMax(),
                0xFF00FFFF, 0x3000FFFF, 0x8000FFFF, false);

        // 图标
        g.drawString(font, "⚡", x + ENERGY_X + 2, y + 8, 0xFF00FF88, true);
        g.drawString(font, "O₂", x + OXYGEN_X + 2, y + 8, 0xFF00FFFF, true);
    }

    private void drawBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, int h,
                         int stored, int max,
                         int borderColor, int glowColor, int tickColor,
                         boolean tickLeft) {
        g.fill(x - 2, y - 2, x + w + 2, y + h + 2, glowColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h, 0xFF0A1818);

        for (int i = 1; i < 6; i++) {
            int gy = y + (h * i / 6);
            g.fill(x + 1, gy, x + w - 1, gy + 1, 0x20000000 | (borderColor & 0x00FFFFFF));
        }

        if (max > 0 && stored > 0) {
            int fillH = (int) ((float) stored / max * (h - 4));
            int fillY = y + h - 2 - fillH;
            int baseR = (borderColor >> 16) & 0xFF;
            int baseG = (borderColor >> 8) & 0xFF;
            int baseB = borderColor & 0xFF;
            for (int i = 0; i < fillH; i++) {
                float t = (float) i / fillH;
                int r = (int) (baseR * (0.4f + 0.6f * t));
                int gr = (int) (baseG * (0.4f + 0.6f * t));
                int b = (int) (baseB * (0.4f + 0.6f * t));
                g.fill(x + 2, fillY + i, x + w - 2, fillY + i + 1,
                        0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b);
            }
            g.fill(x + 2, fillY, x + w - 2, fillY + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }

        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, borderColor);
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, borderColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, borderColor);
        g.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, borderColor);

        for (int i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
            int ty = y + (h * i / 4);
            if (tickLeft) {
                g.fill(x - 2, ty, x, ty + 1, tickColor);
            } else {
                g.fill(x + w, ty, x + w + 2, ty + 1, tickColor);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void render(@NotNull GuiGraphics g, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        renderBackground(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
        super.render(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);

        int x = (width - imageWidth) / 2;
        int y = (height - imageHeight) / 2;

        // 悬停能量条
        if (isHovering(mouseX, mouseY, x + ENERGY_X, y + ENERGY_Y, ENERGY_W, ENERGY_H)) {
            drawTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY,
                    "⚡ " + menu.getEnergyStored() + " / " + menu.getMaxEnergyStored() + " FE",
                    0xFF00FF88);
        }

        if (isHovering(mouseX, mouseY, x + OXYGEN_X, y + OXYGEN_Y, OXYGEN_W, OXYGEN_H)) {
            drawTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY,
                    "O₂ " + menu.getOxygenBuffer() + " / " + menu.getOxygenBufferMax(),
                    0xFF00FFFF);
        }

        // 悬停植物槽
        if (isHovering(mouseX, mouseY, x + 79, y + 34, 18, 18)) {
            drawTwoLineTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY,
                    "🌿 倍数 ×" + menu.getPlantMultiplier(),
                    "速度 +" + menu.getCurrentRate() + " /tick",
                    0xFF00CC44);
        }

        renderTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY);
    }

    private boolean isHovering(int mouseX, int mouseY, int x, int y, int w, int h) {
        return mouseX >= x && mouseX <= x + w && mouseY >= y && mouseY <= y + h;
    }

    private void drawTooltip(GuiGraphics g, int mouseX, int mouseY, String text, int color) {
        int tw = font.width(text);
        int tx = mouseX + 8;
        int ty = mouseY - 4;
        int bw = tw + 8;
        int bh = 14;

        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty + bh, 0xE0000D14);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty, color);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty + bh - 1, tx + bw, ty + bh, color);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx, ty + bh, color);
        g.fill(tx + bw - 1, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty + bh, color);

        g.drawString(font, text, tx + 2, ty + 2, color, true);
    }

    private void drawTwoLineTooltip(GuiGraphics g, int mouseX, int mouseY,
                                    String line1, String line2, int color) {
        int w1 = font.width(line1);
        int w2 = font.width(line2);
        int maxW = Math.max(w1, w2);
        int tx = mouseX + 8;
        int ty = mouseY - 4;
        int bw = maxW + 8;
        int bh = 24;

        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty + bh, 0xE0000D14);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty, color);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty + bh - 1, tx + bw, ty + bh, color);
        g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx, ty + bh, color);
        g.fill(tx + bw - 1, ty - 2, tx + bw, ty + bh, color);

        g.drawString(font, line1, tx + 2, ty + 2, color, true);
        g.drawString(font, line2, tx + 2, ty + 13, 0x8000FF88, false);
    }
}

        g.fill(energyX, energyY + energyH - 1, energyX + energyW, energyY + energyH, 0xFF00E5FF);
        g.fill(energyX, energyY, energyX + 1, energyY + energyH, 0xFF00E5FF);
        g.fill(energyX + energyW - 1, energyY, energyX + energyW, energyY + energyH, 0xFF00E5FF);

        for (int i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
            int ty = energyY + (energyH * i / 4);
            g.fill(energyX - 2, ty, energyX, ty + 1, 0x8000B8D0);
        }

        // ==================== 燃烧进度 ====================
        int burnX = x + 65;
        int burnY = y + 60;
        int burnW = 46;
        int burnH = 6;

        g.fill(burnX, burnY, burnX + burnW, burnY + burnH, 0xFF0A1418);

        int burn = menu.getBurnTime();
        int maxBurn = menu.getMaxBurnTime();
        if (maxBurn > 0 && burn > 0) {
            int fillW = (int) ((float) burn / maxBurn * burnW);
            for (int i = 0; i < fillW; i++) {
                float t = (float) i / burnW;
                int r = 255;
                int gr = (int) (80 + 140 * t);
                int b = 0;
                int color = 0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b;
                g.fill(burnX + i, burnY + 1, burnX + i + 1, burnY + burnH - 1, color);
            }
        }

        g.fill(burnX, burnY, burnX + burnW, burnY + 1, 0xFF00E5FF);
        g.fill(burnX, burnY + burnH - 1, burnX + burnW, burnY + burnH, 0xFF00E5FF);
        g.fill(burnX, burnY, burnX + 1, burnY + burnH, 0xFF00E5FF);
        g.fill(burnX + burnW - 1, burnY, burnX + burnW, burnY + burnH, 0xFF00E5FF);

        // ==================== 图标 ====================
        g.drawString(font, "⚡", x + 12, y + 8, 0xFFFFB300, true);
        g.drawString(font, "🔥", x + 59, y + 58, 0xFFFF5500, true);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(GuiGraphics g, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        renderBackground(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
        super.render(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);

        int x = (width - imageWidth) / 2;
        int y = (height - imageHeight) / 2;

        int energyX = x + 10;
        int energyY = y + 20;
        int energyW = 16;
        int energyH = 46;

        if (mouseX >= energyX && mouseX <= energyX + energyW
                && mouseY >= energyY && mouseY <= energyY + energyH) {
            int stored = menu.getEnergyStored();
            int max = menu.getMaxEnergyStored();

            String text1 = "⚡ " + stored;
            String text2 = "/ " + max + " FE";

            int tw1 = font.width(text1);
            int tw2 = font.width(text2);

            int tx = mouseX + 8;
            int ty = mouseY - 4;

            // 背景框
            int boxW = Math.max(tw1, tw2) + 8;
            int boxH = 24;
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + boxW, ty + boxH, 0xE0000D14);
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + boxW, ty, 0xFF00E5FF);
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty + boxH - 1, tx + boxW, ty + boxH, 0xFF00E5FF);
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx, ty + boxH, 0xFF00E5FF);
            g.fill(tx + boxW - 1, ty - 2, tx + boxW, ty + boxH, 0xFF00E5FF);

            g.drawString(font, text1, tx + 2, ty + 2, 0xFFFFB300, true);
            g.drawString(font, text2, tx + 2, ty + 12, 0x80FFB300, false);
        }

        int burnX = x + 65;
        int burnY = y + 60;
        int burnW = 46;
        int burnH = 6;

        if (mouseX >= burnX && mouseX <= burnX + burnW
                && mouseY >= burnY && mouseY <= burnY + burnH) {
            int burn = menu.getBurnTime();
            int maxBurn = menu.getMaxBurnTime();

            String text1 = "🔥 " + burn + " tick";
            String text2 = "/ " + maxBurn;

            int tw1 = font.width(text1);
            int tw2 = font.width(text2);

            int tx = mouseX + 8;
            int ty = mouseY - 4;

            int boxW = Math.max(tw1, tw2) + 8;
            int boxH = 24;
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + boxW, ty + boxH, 0xE0000D14);
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx + boxW, ty, 0xFF00E5FF);
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty + boxH - 1, tx + boxW, ty + boxH, 0xFF00E5FF);
            g.fill(tx - 2, ty - 2, tx, ty + boxH, 0xFF00E5FF);
            g.fill(tx + boxW - 1, ty - 2, tx + boxW, ty + boxH, 0xFF00E5FF);

            g.drawString(font, text1, tx + 2, ty + 2, 0xFFFF5500, true);
            g.drawString(font, text2, tx + 2, ty + 12, 0x80FF5500, false);
        }

        renderTooltip(g, mouseX, mouseY);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.jei;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.MachinesBlock;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe.CompressorRecipe;
import mezz.jei.api.gui.builder.IRecipeLayoutBuilder;
import mezz.jei.api.gui.drawable.IDrawable;
import mezz.jei.api.gui.ingredient.IRecipeSlotsView;
import mezz.jei.api.helpers.IGuiHelper;
import mezz.jei.api.recipe.IFocusGroup;
import mezz.jei.api.recipe.RecipeType;
import mezz.jei.api.recipe.category.IRecipeCategory;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class CompressorRecipeCategory implements IRecipeCategory<CompressorRecipe> {

    public static final RecipeType<CompressorRecipe> TYPE = RecipeType.create(
            NewGalaxyMain.MODID,
            "compressing",
            CompressorRecipe.class
    );

    private final IDrawable icon;

    public CompressorRecipeCategory(IGuiHelper guiHelper) {
        this.icon = guiHelper.createDrawableItemStack(
                new ItemStack(MachinesBlock.COMPRESSOR_ITEM.get()));
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull RecipeType<CompressorRecipe> getRecipeType() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull Component getTitle() {
        return Component.translatable("block.newgc.compressor");
    }

    @Override
    public int getWidth() {
        return 120;
    }

    @Override
    public int getHeight() {
        return 40;
    }

    @Override
    public IDrawable getIcon() {
        return icon;
    }

    @Override
    public void setRecipe(IRecipeLayoutBuilder builder, CompressorRecipe recipe, @NotNull IFocusGroup focuses) {
        builder.addInputSlot(1, 11).addIngredients(recipe.getIngredient());
        builder.addOutputSlot(101, 11).addItemStack(recipe.getResult());
    }

    @Override
    public void draw(CompressorRecipe recipe, @NotNull IRecipeSlotsView recipeSlotsView,
                     GuiGraphics guiGraphics, double mouseX, double mouseY) {
        guiGraphics.drawString(Minecraft.getInstance().font, "→", 58, 15, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);

        if (recipe.getInputCount() > 1) {
            guiGraphics.drawString(Minecraft.getInstance().font, "×" + recipe.getInputCount(),
                    4, 30, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\jei\NewgcJeiPlugin.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.jei;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.MachinesBlock;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe.CompressorRecipe;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe.ModRecipes;
import mezz.jei.api.IModPlugin;
import mezz.jei.api.JeiPlugin;
import mezz.jei.api.registration.IRecipeCatalystRegistration;
import mezz.jei.api.registration.IRecipeCategoryRegistration;
import mezz.jei.api.registration.IRecipeRegistration;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientPacketListener;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeManager;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.util.List;

@JeiPlugin
public class NewgcJeiPlugin implements IModPlugin {

    @Override
    public @NotNull ResourceLocation getPluginUid() {
        return ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "jei_plugin");
    }

    @Override
    public void registerCategories(IRecipeCategoryRegistration registration) {
        registration.addRecipeCategories(
                new CompressorRecipeCategory(registration.getJeiHelpers().getGuiHelper())
        );
    }

    @Override
    public void registerRecipes(@NotNull IRecipeRegistration registration) {
        RecipeManager manager = getRecipeManager();
        if (manager == null) return;

        List<CompressorRecipe> recipes = manager.getAllRecipesFor(ModRecipes.COMPRESSING.get())
                .stream()
                .map(RecipeHolder::value)
                .toList();

        registration.addRecipes(CompressorRecipeCategory.TYPE, recipes);
    }

    @Override
    public void registerRecipeCatalysts(IRecipeCatalystRegistration registration) {
        registration.addRecipeCatalyst(
                new ItemStack(MachinesBlock.COMPRESSOR_ITEM.get()),
                CompressorRecipeCategory.TYPE
        );
    }

    private static RecipeManager getRecipeManager() {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        ClientPacketListener connection = mc.getConnection();
        if (connection != null) {
            return connection.getRecipeManager();
        }
        if (mc.level != null) {
            return mc.level.getRecipeManager();
        }
        return null;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\magnetometer\MagnetometerClientCache.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.magnetometer;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client.JupiterStormClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client.MarsDustStormClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client.MarsVolcanoClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client.MercurySolarFlareClientCache;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

/**
 * 磁场计客户端数据缓存。每帧计算一次，供 HUD / Screen 读取。
 */
public final class MagnetometerClientCache {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static float strength = 0f;
    private static float angle = 0f;      // 0~360，指向磁北极
    private static float noise = 0f;
    private static String status = "正常";
    private static int statusColor = 0xFF9BB7E0;
    private static float jitterAmount = 0f;

    // 用于"矿脉感应"
    private static boolean ironNearby = false;

    private static long lastUpdateTick = -1;

    private MagnetometerClientCache() {}

    /** 每帧调用一次 */
    public static void tick() {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        Player player = mc.player;
        Level level = mc.level;
        if (player == null || level == null) return;

        long tick = level.getGameTime();
        if (tick == lastUpdateTick) return;
        lastUpdateTick = tick;

        ResourceKey<Level> dim = level.dimension();
        float base = MagnetometerData.getBaseStrength(dim);
        float n = MagnetometerData.getNoise(dim);

        // 事件干扰
        String evt = null;
        float evtBoost = 0f;
        int evtColor = 0xFF9BB7E0;

        // 木星风暴
        float jupiterStrength = JupiterStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (jupiterStrength > 0.5f && dim.equals(com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) {
            evt = "⚡ 风暴干扰";
            evtBoost = jupiterStrength * 0.8f;
            evtColor = 0xFFFF8A3A;
        }

        // 火星火山
        if (MarsVolcanoClientCache.isErupting() && dim.equals(com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) {
            evt = "🌋 局部磁畸变";
            evtBoost = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getEruptionIntensity() * 0.5f;
            evtColor = 0xFFE06B4B;
        }

        // 火星沙尘暴
        float dust = MarsDustStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (dust > 0.5f && dim.equals(com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) {
            evt = "🌪 沙尘噪声";
            evtBoost = dust * 0.3f;
            evtColor = 0xFFC89650;
        }

        // 水星耀斑
        if (MercurySolarFlareClientCache.isFlaring() && dim.equals(com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) {
            evt = "☀ 耀斑脉冲";
            evtBoost = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getIntensity() * 1.0f;
            evtColor = 0xFFFFE0A0;
        }

        strength = base + evtBoost;
        noise = n;

        // 角度：指向磁北
        double northX = MagnetometerData.getNorthX(dim);
        double northZ = MagnetometerData.getNorthZ(dim);
        double dx = northX - player.getX();
        double dz = northZ - player.getZ();

        double targetAngle = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(-dx, dz));
        if (targetAngle < 0) targetAngle += 360;

        // 噪声：指针抖动
        float jitter = n * (1f + evtBoost * 0.5f);
        jitterAmount = jitter;
        angle = (float) (targetAngle + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * jitter * 60);

        // 矿脉感应：附近 16 格有没有铁矿
        ironNearby = checkIronNearby(level, player.blockPosition());

        // 状态文字
        if (evt != null) {
            status = evt;
            statusColor = evtColor;
        } else if (base < 0.05f) {
            status = "磁场失效";
            statusColor = 0xFF8898B0;
        } else if (n > 0.5f) {
            status = "磁场紊乱";
            statusColor = 0xFFFFC060;
        } else {
            status = "正常";
            statusColor = 0xFF9BB7E0;
        }
    }

    private static boolean checkIronNearby(Level level, BlockPos center) {
        // 简单检查：范围内扫描铁矿石
        for (int dx = -8; dx <= 8; dx++) {
            for (int dy = -4; dy <= 4; dy++) {
                for (int dz = -8; dz <= 8; dz++) {
                    BlockPos p = center.offset(dx, dy, dz);
                    var block = level.getBlockState(p).getBlock();
                    if (block == net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks.IRON_ORE
                            || block == net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks.DEEPSLATE_IRON_ORE) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ===== Getter =====
    public static float getStrength()      { return strength; }
    public static float getAngle()         { return angle; }
    public static float getNoise()         { return noise; }
    public static String getStatus()       { return status; }
    public static int getStatusColor()     { return statusColor; }
    public static float getJitterAmount()  { return jitterAmount; }
    public static boolean isIronNearby()   { return ironNearby; }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\client\magnetometer\MagnetometerData.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.magnetometer;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

/**
 * 磁场参数表。每个维度一组参数。
 */
public final class MagnetometerData {

    public static final float STRENGTH_MAX = 3.0f;

    private MagnetometerData() {}

    /**
     * 基础磁场强度（0~3）。
     * 无事件、无干扰时的"本底磁场"。
     */
    public static float getBaseStrength(ResourceKey<Level> dim) {
        if (dim.equals(Level.OVERWORLD))                       return 1.0f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.MOON_LEVEL_KEY))          return 0.05f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY))          return 0.10f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY))       return 3.00f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY))       return 0.40f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.VENUS_LEVEL_KEY))         return 0.02f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.PHOBOS_LEVEL_KEY))        return 0.03f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.DEIMOS_LEVEL_KEY))        return 0.03f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.CERES_LEVEL_KEY))         return 0.06f;
        return 0.5f;
    }

    /**
     * 磁场噪声系数（0~1）。越高指针越不稳。
     */
    public static float getNoise(ResourceKey<Level> dim) {
        if (dim.equals(Level.OVERWORLD))                       return 0.05f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.MOON_LEVEL_KEY))          return 0.50f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY))          return 0.30f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY))       return 1.00f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY))       return 0.15f;
        if (dim.equals(ModDimensions.VENUS_LEVEL_KEY))         return 0.80f;
        return 0.30f;
    }

    /**
     * 磁北极方向（世界坐标里的一个固定点）。
     * 目前所有维度都设成 (0, 0)，玩家朝磁北 = 朝世界原点。
     */
    public static double getNorthX(ResourceKey<Level> dim) { return 0.0; }
    public static double getNorthZ(ResourceKey<Level> dim) { return 0.0; }

    /** 归一化到 0~1 用于进度条 */
    public static float normalize(float strength) {
        return Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, strength / STRENGTH_MAX));
    }

    /** 磁场等级描述 */
    public static String levelText(float strength) {
        if (strength < 0.05f) return "极弱";
        if (strength < 0.3f)  return "弱";
        if (strength < 0.8f)  return "中等";
        if (strength < 1.5f)  return "正常";
        if (strength < 2.2f)  return "强";
        return "极强";
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.config;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModContainer;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModList;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.gui.IConfigScreenFactory;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class ModConfigScreenFactory {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientSetup(FMLClientSetupEvent event) {
        event.enqueueWork(() -> {
            ModContainer container = ModList.get()
                    .getModContainerById(NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
                    .orElseThrow();
            container.registerExtensionPoint(
                    IConfigScreenFactory.class,
                    (IConfigScreenFactory) (mod, parent) -> new NewGalaxyConfigScreen(parent)
            );
        });
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.config;

import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.ModConfigSpec;
import org.apache.commons.lang3.tuple.Pair;

public class NewGalaxyConfig {

    public static final ModConfigSpec SPEC;
    public static final Client CLIENT;

    static {
        Pair<Client, ModConfigSpec> clientPair =
                new ModConfigSpec.Builder().configure(Client::new);
        CLIENT = clientPair.getLeft();
        SPEC = clientPair.getRight();
    }

    public static class Client {
        public final ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue debugMode;

        public Client(ModConfigSpec.Builder builder) {
            builder.comment("调试设置").push("debug");
            debugMode = builder
                    .comment("开启后，在星球 HUD 下方显示调试按钮，可直接切换环境状态")
                    .define("debugMode", false);
            builder.pop();
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\config\NewGalaxyConfigScreen.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.config;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug.DebugState;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.components.Button;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.components.CycleButton;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;

public class NewGalaxyConfigScreen extends Screen {

    private final Screen parent;

    public NewGalaxyConfigScreen(Screen parent) {
        super(Component.literal("新星系 · 设置"));
        this.parent = parent;
    }

    @Override
    protected void init() {
        int cx = this.width / 2;
        int cy = this.height / 2;
        int w = 220;
        int h = 20;

        this.addRenderableWidget(CycleButton.onOffBuilder(
                        NewGalaxyConfig.CLIENT.debugMode.get())
                .create(cx - w / 2, cy - 30, w, h,
                        Component.literal("开启调试按钮"),
                        (btn, val) -> NewGalaxyConfig.CLIENT.debugMode.set(val)));

        this.addRenderableWidget(Button.builder(
                        Component.literal("完成"),
                        btn -> this.onClose())
                .bounds(cx - w / 2, cy + 10, w, h)
                .build());
    }

    @Override
    public void render(GuiGraphics g, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        this.renderBackground(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
        g.drawCenteredString(this.font, this.title, this.width / 2, 30, 0xFFFFFF);
        super.render(g, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
    }

    @Override
    public void onClose() {
        NewGalaxyConfig.SPEC.save();

        // 关闭调试模式时，立即清空调试目标，恢复状态机
        if (!NewGalaxyConfig.CLIENT.debugMode.get()) {
            DebugState.resetAll();
        }

        this.minecraft.setScreen(parent);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.energy;

import net.neoforged.neoforge.energy.EnergyStorage;

public class SimpleEnergyStorage extends EnergyStorage {

    public SimpleEnergyStorage(int capacity, int maxReceive, int maxExtract) {
        super(capacity, maxReceive, maxExtract);
    }

    public int generate(int amount) {
        int accepted = Math.min(capacity - energy, amount);
        energy += accepted;
        return accepted;
    }

    public void setEnergy(int value) {
        this.energy = Math.max(0, Math.min(capacity, value));
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.block.entity.ModBlockEntities;

import net.neoforged.neoforge.capabilities.RegisterCapabilitiesEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class ModCapabilities {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void registerCapabilities(RegisterCapabilitiesEvent event) {
        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.SOLID_FUEL_GENERATOR.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );

        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.OXYGEN_EXTRACTOR.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );

        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.WIRE.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );

        event.registerBlockEntity(
                Capabilities.EnergyStorage.BLOCK,
                ModBlockEntities.COMPRESSOR.get(),
                (be, side) -> be.getEnergyStorage()
        );
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\event\RespawnDimensionHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class RespawnDimensionHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerClone(PlayerEvent.Clone event) {
        if (!event.isWasDeath()) return;

        if (!(event.getOriginal() instanceof ServerPlayer oldPlayer)) return;
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer newPlayer)) return;

        ResourceKey<Level> deathDimension = oldPlayer.level().dimension();
        ServerLevel targetLevel = newPlayer.server.getLevel(deathDimension);
        if (targetLevel == null) return;

        // 用共享出生点的 X/Z，但 Y 用地表高度
        BlockPos sharedSpawn = targetLevel.getSharedSpawnPos();
        int x = sharedSpawn.getX();
        int z = sharedSpawn.getZ();

        // 获取该 X/Z 位置的地表高度
        int surfaceY = targetLevel.getHeight(
                Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING,
                x, z
        );

        // 从地表往上找安全位置
        BlockPos safePos = findSafeSpot(targetLevel, x, surfaceY, z);

        newPlayer.teleportTo(targetLevel,
                safePos.getX() + 0.5,
                safePos.getY(),
                safePos.getZ() + 0.5,
                newPlayer.getYRot(),
                newPlayer.getXRot());
    }

    /**
     * 从给定 Y 开始往上找，直到找到两个连续空气方块。
     */
    private static BlockPos findSafeSpot(ServerLevel level, int x, int startY, int z) {
        for (int y = startY; y < startY + 20; y++) {
            BlockPos pos = new BlockPos(x, y, z);
            BlockPos above = pos.above();

            if (level.isEmptyBlock(pos) && level.isEmptyBlock(above)) {
                return pos;
            }
        }
        // 找不到安全点，就用地表 + 1
        return new BlockPos(x, startY + 1, z);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen;

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class OxygenConfig {

    /** 第一管上限（自身基础氧气） */
    public static final int BASE_MAX_OXYGEN = 100;

    /** 第二管上限（氧气管扩展）—— 初始为空 */
    public static final int TANK_MAX_OXYGEN = 0;

    /** 总上限（硬顶，未来扩展） */
    public static final int ABSOLUTE_MAX_OXYGEN = 5_000_000;

    /** 缺氧伤害间隔（tick） */
    public static final int DAMAGE_INTERVAL = 20;   // 1 秒

    /** 缺氧伤害量 = 玩家最大生命的 1/3 */
    public static final float DAMAGE_FRACTION = 1.0f / 3.0f;

    private static final Map<ResourceKey<Level>, Float> OXYGEN_USAGE = new HashMap<>();

    static {
        // 全部维度都消耗氧气
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(Level.OVERWORLD, 0.05f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(Level.NETHER, 0.05f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(Level.END, 0.05f);

        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.MOON_LEVEL_KEY, 0.05f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY, 0.05f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.PHOBOS_LEVEL_KEY, 0.05f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.DEIMOS_LEVEL_KEY, 0.05f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY, 0.075f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.VENUS_LEVEL_KEY, 0.15f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.CERES_LEVEL_KEY, 0.05f);
        OXYGEN_USAGE.put(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY, 0.1f);
    }

    public static float getUsage(ResourceKey<Level> dim) {
        Float usage = OXYGEN_USAGE.get(dim);
        return usage == null ? 0.05f : usage;   // 默认消耗
    }

    public static boolean hasOxygen(ResourceKey<Level> dim) {
        return getUsage(dim) <= 0.0f;
    }

    /** 永远返回 true（全维度显示） */
    public static boolean isVacuum(ResourceKey<Level> dim) {
        return true;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\event\oxygen\OxygenData.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen;

import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.attachment.IAttachmentHolder;

public class OxygenData {

    public static final Codec<OxygenData> CODEC =
            Codec.INT.xmap(OxygenData::new, OxygenData::getOxygen);

    /** 第一管氧气（自身） */
    private int oxygen;
    /** 第二管氧气（氧气管扩展） */
    private int tankOxygen;
    /** 第二管上限 */
    private int tankMax;
    /** 伤害冷却 */
    private int damageCooldown = 0;

    /** AttachmentType.builder 要求的构造 */
    public OxygenData(IAttachmentHolder holder) {
        this.oxygen = OxygenConfig.BASE_MAX_OXYGEN;
        this.tankOxygen = 0;
        this.tankMax = OxygenConfig.TANK_MAX_OXYGEN;
    }

    /** Codec 要求的构造 */
    public OxygenData(int oxygen) {
        this.oxygen = Math.max(0, Math.min(OxygenConfig.BASE_MAX_OXYGEN, oxygen));
        this.tankOxygen = 0;
        this.tankMax = OxygenConfig.TANK_MAX_OXYGEN;
    }

    // ==================== 第一管 ====================
    public int getOxygen() {
        return oxygen;
    }

    public void setOxygen(int value) {
        this.oxygen = Math.max(0, Math.min(getBaseMax(), value));
    }

    public int getBaseMax() {
        return OxygenConfig.BASE_MAX_OXYGEN;
    }

    // ==================== 第二管 ====================
    public int getTankOxygen() {
        return tankOxygen;
    }

    public void setTankOxygen(int value) {
        this.tankOxygen = Math.max(0, Math.min(getTankMax(), value));
    }

    public int getTankMax() {
        return tankMax;
    }

    public void setTankMax(int value) {
        this.tankMax = Math.max(0, Math.min(OxygenConfig.ABSOLUTE_MAX_OXYGEN, value));
        if (this.tankOxygen > this.tankMax) {
            this.tankOxygen = this.tankMax;
        }
    }

    // ==================== 通用 ====================
    /** 总氧气 = 第一管 + 第二管 */
    public int getTotalOxygen() {
        return oxygen + tankOxygen;
    }

    /** 总上限 = 第一管上限 + 第二管上限 */
    public int getTotalMax() {
        return getBaseMax() + getTankMax();
    }

    /** 是否全满 */
    public boolean isFull() {
        return oxygen >= getBaseMax() && tankOxygen >= getTankMax();
    }

    /** 是否完全耗尽 */
    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return oxygen <= 0 && tankOxygen <= 0;
    }

    /** 第一管百分比 */
    public float getBasePercentage() {
        return (float) oxygen / getBaseMax();
    }

    /** 第二管百分比 */
    public float getTankPercentage() {
        if (getTankMax() <= 0) return 0.0f;
        return (float) tankOxygen / getTankMax();
    }

    // ==================== 消耗 / 补充 ====================
    /** 消耗（先扣第二管，再扣第一管） */
    public void consumeOxygen(float amount) {
        int need = (int) Math.ceil(amount);

        if (tankOxygen > 0) {
            int fromTank = Math.min(tankOxygen, need);
            tankOxygen -= fromTank;
            need -= fromTank;
        }

        if (need > 0) {
            oxygen = Math.max(0, oxygen - need);
        }
    }

    /** 补充（先补第一管，再补第二管） */
    public void addOxygen(int amount) {
        int spaceBase = getBaseMax() - oxygen;
        int toBase = Math.min(spaceBase, amount);
        oxygen += toBase;
        amount -= toBase;

        if (amount > 0 && getTankMax() > 0) {
            int spaceTank = getTankMax() - tankOxygen;
            int toTank = Math.min(spaceTank, amount);
            tankOxygen += toTank;
        }
    }

    // ==================== 伤害冷却 ====================
    public int getDamageCooldown() {
        return damageCooldown;
    }

    public void setDamageCooldown(int value) {
        this.damageCooldown = Math.max(0, value);
    }

    public void tickDamageCooldown() {
        if (damageCooldown > 0) damageCooldown--;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\event\oxygen\OxygenHandler.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class OxygenHandler {

    private static final int CONSUME_INTERVAL = 20;   // 1 秒
    private static final int CONSUME_AMOUNT = 3;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        OxygenData data = player.getData(ModAttachments.OXYGEN.get());
        ResourceKey<Level> dim = player.level().dimension();

        // ★ 创造/旁观模式：不消耗，且自动回满
        if (player.isCreative() || player.isSpectator()) {
            if (!data.isFull()) {
                data.setOxygen(data.getBaseMax());
                data.setTankOxygen(data.getTankMax());
            }
            data.setDamageCooldown(0);
            syncIfNeeded(player, data);
            return;
        }

        // 主世界：不消耗，恢复
        if (dim.equals(Level.OVERWORLD)) {
            if (!data.isFull()) {
                data.addOxygen(10);
            }
            data.setDamageCooldown(0);
            syncIfNeeded(player, data);
            return;
        }

        // 其它维度：消耗
        if (player.tickCount % CONSUME_INTERVAL == 0) {
            data.consumeOxygen(CONSUME_AMOUNT);
        }

        // 没氧气 → 掉血
        if (data.isEmpty()) {
            data.tickDamageCooldown();
            if (data.getDamageCooldown() <= 0) {
                data.setDamageCooldown(OxygenConfig.DAMAGE_INTERVAL);

                float maxHealth = player.getMaxHealth();
                float damage = maxHealth * OxygenConfig.DAMAGE_FRACTION;

                player.hurt(player.damageSources().generic(), damage);

                if (player.tickCount % 60 == 0) {
                    player.displayClientMessage(
                            Component.translatable("message.newgc.oxygen.low")
                                    .withStyle(ChatFormatting.RED),
                            true
                    );
                }
            }
        } else {
            data.setDamageCooldown(0);
        }

        syncIfNeeded(player, data);
    }

    private static void syncIfNeeded(Player player, OxygenData data) {
        if (player instanceof ServerPlayer sp && player.tickCount % 5 == 0) {
            PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(sp,
                    new OxygenSyncPacket(
                            data.getOxygen(),
                            data.getTankOxygen(),
                            data.getTankMax()));
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\event\oxygen\OxygenResetHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class OxygenResetHandler {

    private static final Map<UUID, Integer> CONFIRM_COUNT = new HashMap<>();
    private static final Map<UUID, Long> LAST_PRESS_TIME = new HashMap<>();

    private static final long CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000;
    private static final int REQUIRED_CONFIRMS = 3;

    public static void onResetKeyPressed(ServerPlayer player) {
        UUID uuid = player.getUUID();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        Long lastTime = LAST_PRESS_TIME.get(uuid);
        int count = CONFIRM_COUNT.getOrDefault(uuid, 0);

        if (lastTime == null || now - lastTime > CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            count = 0;
        }

        count++;
        LAST_PRESS_TIME.put(uuid, now);
        CONFIRM_COUNT.put(uuid, count);

        if (count >= REQUIRED_CONFIRMS) {
            OxygenData data = player.getData(ModAttachments.OXYGEN.get());
            data.setTankOxygen(0);
            data.setTankMax(0);

            CONFIRM_COUNT.remove(uuid);
            LAST_PRESS_TIME.remove(uuid);

            player.displayClientMessage(
                    Component.translatable("message.newgc.oxygen.reset")
                            .withStyle(ChatFormatting.GREEN),
                    true
            );

            PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player,
                    new OxygenSyncPacket(
                            data.getOxygen(),
                            data.getTankOxygen(),
                            data.getTankMax()));
        } else {
            int remaining = REQUIRED_CONFIRMS - count;
            player.displayClientMessage(
                    Component.translatable("message.newgc.oxygen.reset_confirm", remaining, count)
                            .withStyle(ChatFormatting.YELLOW),
                    true
            );
        }
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (event.getEntity().level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer sp)) return;

        UUID uuid = sp.getUUID();
        Long lastTime = LAST_PRESS_TIME.get(uuid);
        if (lastTime != null && System.currentTimeMillis() - lastTime > CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            CONFIRM_COUNT.remove(uuid);
            LAST_PRESS_TIME.remove(uuid);
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\event\oxygen\OxygenSyncPacket.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public record OxygenSyncPacket(int oxygen, int tankOxygen, int tankMax) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<OxygenSyncPacket> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "oxygen_sync"));

    public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, OxygenSyncPacket> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.INT, OxygenSyncPacket::oxygen,
                    ByteBufCodecs.INT, OxygenSyncPacket::tankOxygen,
                    ByteBufCodecs.INT, OxygenSyncPacket::tankMax,
                    OxygenSyncPacket::new
            );

    @Override
    public CustomPacketPayload.@NotNull Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(OxygenSyncPacket packet, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            if (Minecraft.getInstance().player != null) {
                OxygenData data = Minecraft.getInstance().player.getData(ModAttachments.OXYGEN.get());
                data.setTankMax(packet.tankMax());
                data.setOxygen(packet.oxygen());
                data.setTankOxygen(packet.tankOxygen());
            }
        });
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public record ResetTankPacket() implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<ResetTankPacket> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "reset_tank"));

    public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, ResetTankPacket> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.unit(new ResetTankPacket());

    @Override
    public CustomPacketPayload.@NotNull Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(ResetTankPacket packet, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            if (context.player() instanceof ServerPlayer sp) {
                OxygenResetHandler.onResetKeyPressed(sp);
            }
        });
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.fluid;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.EntityTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class AcidRainDamageHandler {

    // 伤害间隔：每 20 刻（1 秒）扣一次
    private static final int DAMAGE_INTERVAL = 20;

    // 扣血量比例：1/3 最大生命值
    private static final float DAMAGE_RATIO = 1.0f / 3.0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onEntityTick(EntityTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity living)) return;
        if (living.level().isClientSide()) return;

        // 每 1 秒检查一次
        if (living.tickCount % DAMAGE_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        // 判断实体是否接触酸雨流体
        if (!isTouchingAcidRain(living)) return;

        // 扣最大生命值的 1/3
        float damage = living.getMaxHealth() * DAMAGE_RATIO;
        living.hurt(living.damageSources().generic(), damage);
    }

    /**
     * 检查实体是否接触酸雨流体。
     * 酸雨流体的注册名是 newgc:acid_rain。
     */
    private static boolean isTouchingAcidRain(LivingEntity living) {
        // 检查实体脚部所在方块和身体所在方块是否有酸雨流体
        var fluidState = living.level().getFluidState(living.blockPosition());
        if (!fluidState.isEmpty()) {
            var fluidType = fluidState.getType();
            var key = net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries.FLUID.getKey(fluidType);
            if (key != null && key.getNamespace().equals(NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
                    && key.getPath().startsWith("acid_rain")) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        // 再检查眼睛位置
        var eyeFluid = living.level().getFluidState(
                net.minecraft.core.BlockPos.containing(living.getEyePosition()));
        if (!eyeFluid.isEmpty()) {
            var fluidType = eyeFluid.getType();
            var key = net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries.FLUID.getKey(fluidType);
            if (key != null && key.getNamespace().equals(NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
                    && key.getPath().startsWith("acid_rain")) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.fluid;

import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.extensions.common.RegisterClientExtensionsEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class ModFluidClientExtensions {

        event.registerFluidType(new IClientFluidTypeExtensions() {
            private static final ResourceLocation STILL =
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "block/acid_still");
            private static final ResourceLocation FLOW =
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "block/acid_flow");

            @Override
            public @NotNull ResourceLocation getStillTexture() {
                return STILL;
            }

            @Override
            public @NotNull ResourceLocation getFlowingTexture() {
                return FLOW;
            }

            @Override
            public int getTintColor() {
                return 0xFFD8EE25;
            }
        }, ModFluids.ACID_RAIN_TYPE.get());
    }

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\fluid\ModFluids.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.fluid;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.world.item.*;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.LiquidBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockBehaviour;
import net.minecraft.world.level.material.Fluid;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.SoundActions;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.fluids.BaseFlowingFluid;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.fluids.FluidType;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredBlock;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredItem;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.NeoForgeRegistries;

public class ModFluids {

    public static final DeferredRegister<FluidType> FLUID_TYPES =
            DeferredRegister.create(NeoForgeRegistries.Keys.FLUID_TYPES, NewGalaxyMain.MODID);

    public static final DeferredRegister<Fluid> FLUIDS =
            DeferredRegister.create(Registries.FLUID, NewGalaxyMain.MODID);

    public static final DeferredRegister.Blocks BLOCKS =
            DeferredRegister.createBlocks(NewGalaxyMain.MODID);

    public static final DeferredRegister.Items ITEMS =
            DeferredRegister.createItems(NewGalaxyMain.MODID);

    public static final DeferredRegister<CreativeModeTab> CREATIVE_MODE_TABS =
            DeferredRegister.create(Registries.CREATIVE_MODE_TAB, NewGalaxyMain.MODID);

    // ==================== FluidType ====================
    public static final DeferredHolder<FluidType, FluidType> ACID_RAIN_TYPE =
            FLUID_TYPES.register("acid_rain", () -> new FluidType(FluidType.Properties.create()
                    .descriptionId("fluid.newgc.acid_rain")
                    .sound(SoundActions.BUCKET_FILL, SoundEvents.BUCKET_FILL)
                    .sound(SoundActions.BUCKET_EMPTY, SoundEvents.BUCKET_EMPTY)
                    .sound(SoundActions.FLUID_VAPORIZE, SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH)
                    .density(1200)
                    .viscosity(1100)
                    .temperature(300)));

    // ==================== Fluid 源 + 流动 ====================
    public static final DeferredHolder<Fluid, BaseFlowingFluid.Source> ACID_RAIN_SOURCE =
            FLUIDS.register("acid_rain",
                    () -> new BaseFlowingFluid.Source(acidRainProperties()));

    public static final DeferredHolder<Fluid, BaseFlowingFluid.Flowing> ACID_RAIN_FLOWING =
            FLUIDS.register("acid_rain_flowing",
                    () -> new BaseFlowingFluid.Flowing(acidRainProperties()));

    // ==================== LiquidBlock ====================
    public static final DeferredBlock<LiquidBlock> ACID_RAIN_BLOCK =
            BLOCKS.register("acid_rain", () ->
                    new LiquidBlock(ACID_RAIN_SOURCE.get(),
                            BlockBehaviour.Properties.ofFullCopy(Blocks.WATER)
                                    .noCollission()
                                    .strength(100.0F)
                                    .noOcclusion()));

    // ==================== BucketItem ====================
    public static final DeferredItem<BucketItem> ACID_RAIN_BUCKET =
            ITEMS.register("acid_rain_bucket", () ->
                    new BucketItem(ACID_RAIN_SOURCE.get(),
                            new Item.Properties().craftRemainder(Items.BUCKET).stacksTo(1)));

    // ==================== Properties ====================
    public static BaseFlowingFluid.Properties acidRainProperties() {
        return new BaseFlowingFluid.Properties(
                ACID_RAIN_TYPE,
                ACID_RAIN_SOURCE,
                ACID_RAIN_FLOWING
        ).block(ACID_RAIN_BLOCK).bucket(ACID_RAIN_BUCKET);
    }

    // ==================== Creative Tab ==================
    public static final DeferredHolder<CreativeModeTab, CreativeModeTab> LIQUID_TAB =
            CREATIVE_MODE_TABS.register("liquid_tab", () -> CreativeModeTab.builder()
                    .title(Component.translatable("itemGroup.newgc.liquids"))
                    .icon(() -> new ItemStack(ACID_RAIN_BUCKET.get()))
                    .displayItems((parameters, output) -> {
                        output.accept(ACID_RAIN_BUCKET.get());
                    })
                    .build());

    public static void register(IEventBus modEventBus) {
        FLUID_TYPES.register(modEventBus);
        FLUIDS.register(modEventBus);
        BLOCKS.register(modEventBus);
        ITEMS.register(modEventBus);
        CREATIVE_MODE_TABS.register(modEventBus);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\gui\GuiRenderUtil.java
