package com.tairitsutek.newgc.gui;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;

public final class GuiRenderUtil {

    private GuiRenderUtil() {

    }

    /**
     * 禁用当前绑定纹理的线性过滤，使用最近邻采样。
     * 调用后，当前绑定的 GUI 纹理不会再被平滑插值，像素边界锐利。
     */
    public static void useNearestFilter() {
        RenderSystem.texParameter(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D,
                GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        RenderSystem.texParameter(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D,
                GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
    }

    /**
     * 恢复为线性过滤。如果有需要，可以在渲染结束后调用。
     */
    public static void useLinearFilter() {
        RenderSystem.texParameter(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D,
                GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_LINEAR);
        RenderSystem.texParameter(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D,
                GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_LINEAR);
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\gui\OxygenHud.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.gui;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.ModAttachments;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.event.oxygen.OxygenData;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class OxygenHud {

    private static final int BAR_WIDTH = 26;
    private static final int BAR_HEIGHT = 110;
    private static final int BAR_GAP = 6;
    private static final int MARGIN_RIGHT = 30;
    private static final int MARGIN_BOTTOM = 70;

    private static final int COLOR_BG_OUTER   = 0x40001824;
    private static final int COLOR_BG_INNER   = 0xB3000D14;
    private static final int COLOR_BORDER     = 0xB300B8D0;
    private static final int COLOR_CORNER     = 0xB300D0E0;
    private static final int COLOR_TICK_MAJOR = 0x5000B8D0;
    private static final int COLOR_GRID       = 0x18FFFFFF;

    private static final int COLOR_FILL_HIGH  = 0xB300B8D0;
    private static final int COLOR_FILL_MID   = 0xB3CC9000;
    private static final int COLOR_FILL_LOW   = 0xB3CC1030;
    private static final int COLOR_GLOW_HIGH  = 0x5000B8D0;
    private static final int COLOR_GLOW_MID   = 0x50CC9000;
    private static final int COLOR_GLOW_LOW   = 0x50CC1030;

    private static final int COLOR_TEXT_MAIN  = 0xCC00D8E8;
    private static final int COLOR_TEXT_VALUE = 0x9980D0E0;
    private static final int COLOR_TEXT_HINT  = 0x8060A0B0;

    private static final long PULSE_PERIOD_MS = 1500;
    private static final long SCAN_PERIOD_MS = 2000;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.player == null) return;
        if (mc.options.hideGui) return;

        OxygenData data = mc.player.getData(ModAttachments.OXYGEN.get());

        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int screenWidth = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int screenHeight = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        int totalWidth = BAR_WIDTH * 2 + BAR_GAP;
        int baseX = screenWidth - MARGIN_RIGHT - totalWidth;
        int baseY = screenHeight - MARGIN_BOTTOM - BAR_HEIGHT;

        drawTitle(g, mc, baseX, baseY, totalWidth);

        int x1 = baseX;
        drawBar(g, mc, x1, baseY, data.getOxygen(), data.getBaseMax(), "BASE", true, false);

        int x2 = baseX + BAR_WIDTH + BAR_GAP;
        boolean hasTank = data.getTankMax() > 0;
        drawBar(g, mc, x2, baseY, data.getTankOxygen(), data.getTankMax(), "TANK", false, true);

        drawStatusBar(g, mc, baseX, baseY + BAR_HEIGHT + 4, totalWidth, data, hasTank);
    }

    private static void drawTitle(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y, int width) {
        String title = "◤ OXYGEN ◢";
        int tw = mc.font.width(title);
        int tx = x + width / 2 - tw / 2;
        int ty = y - 34;

        for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
            for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
                if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
                g.drawString(mc.font, title, tx + dx, ty + dy, 0x3000B8D0, false);
            }
        }
        g.drawString(mc.font, title, tx, ty, COLOR_TEXT_MAIN, true);

        int lineY = ty + 12;
        g.fill(x, lineY, x + width, lineY + 1, 0x6000B8D0);
        g.fill(x - 1, lineY - 1, x + 3, lineY + 2, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x + width - 3, lineY - 1, x + width + 1, lineY + 2, COLOR_CORNER);
    }

    private static void drawBar(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y,
                                int current, int max, String label,
                                boolean pulse, boolean showTicks) {
        float pct = max <= 0 ? 0.0f : (float) current / max;

        if (pulse && pct > 0) {
            long time = System.currentTimeMillis();
            double phase = (time % PULSE_PERIOD_MS) / (double) PULSE_PERIOD_MS;
            float pulseFactor = (float) (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(phase * Math.PI * 2));

            int glowColor;
            if (pct > 0.5f) glowColor = applyPulse(COLOR_GLOW_HIGH, pulseFactor);
            else if (pct > 0.25f) glowColor = applyPulse(COLOR_GLOW_MID, pulseFactor);
            else glowColor = applyPulse(COLOR_GLOW_LOW, pulseFactor);

            g.fill(x - 3, y - 3, x + BAR_WIDTH + 3, y + BAR_HEIGHT + 3, glowColor);
        }

        g.fill(x, y, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_BG_OUTER);
        g.fill(x + 1, y + 1, x + BAR_WIDTH - 1, y + BAR_HEIGHT - 1, COLOR_BG_INNER);

        for (int i = 1; i < 8; i++) {
            int gridY = y + (BAR_HEIGHT * i / 8);
            g.fill(x + 1, gridY, x + BAR_WIDTH - 1, gridY + 1, COLOR_GRID);
        }
        g.fill(x + BAR_WIDTH / 2, y + 1, x + BAR_WIDTH / 2 + 1, y + BAR_HEIGHT - 1, COLOR_GRID);

        int fillHeight = (int) (BAR_HEIGHT * pct);
        if (fillHeight > 0) {
            int fillTop = y + BAR_HEIGHT - fillHeight;

            int fillColor;
            if (pct > 0.5f) fillColor = COLOR_FILL_HIGH;
            else if (pct > 0.25f) fillColor = COLOR_FILL_MID;
            else fillColor = COLOR_FILL_LOW;

            g.fill(x + 2, fillTop, x + BAR_WIDTH - 2, y + BAR_HEIGHT - 2, fillColor);
            g.fill(x + 2, fillTop, x + BAR_WIDTH - 2, fillTop + 1, 0x80FFFFFF);

            for (int i = fillTop + 3; i < y + BAR_HEIGHT - 2; i += 4) {
                g.fill(x + 3, i, x + BAR_WIDTH - 3, i + 1, 0x18000000);
            }

            long time = System.currentTimeMillis();
            double scanPhase = (time % SCAN_PERIOD_MS) / (double) SCAN_PERIOD_MS;
            int scanY = fillTop + (int) ((1.0 - scanPhase) * fillHeight);
            if (scanY > fillTop && scanY < y + BAR_HEIGHT - 2) {
                g.fill(x + 2, scanY, x + BAR_WIDTH - 2, scanY + 1, 0x80FFFFFF);
            }
        }

        g.fill(x, y, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + 1, COLOR_BORDER);
        g.fill(x, y + BAR_HEIGHT - 1, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_BORDER);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_BORDER);
        g.fill(x + BAR_WIDTH - 1, y, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_BORDER);

        int corner = 3;
        g.fill(x, y, x + corner, y + 2, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 2, y + corner, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x + BAR_WIDTH - corner, y, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + 2, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x + BAR_WIDTH - 2, y, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + corner, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x, y + BAR_HEIGHT - 2, x + corner, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x, y + BAR_HEIGHT - corner, x + 2, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x + BAR_WIDTH - corner, y + BAR_HEIGHT - 2, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_CORNER);
        g.fill(x + BAR_WIDTH - 2, y + BAR_HEIGHT - corner, x + BAR_WIDTH, y + BAR_HEIGHT, COLOR_CORNER);

        for (int i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
            int tickY = y + (BAR_HEIGHT * i / 4);
            int tickLen = (i == 0 || i == 4) ? BAR_WIDTH - 2 : 5;
            g.fill(x + 1, tickY, x + 1 + tickLen, tickY + 1, COLOR_TICK_MAJOR);
            g.fill(x + BAR_WIDTH - 1 - tickLen, tickY, x + BAR_WIDTH - 1, tickY + 1, COLOR_TICK_MAJOR);
        }

        if (showTicks) {
            for (int i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
                int tickY = y + (BAR_HEIGHT * i / 4);
                int percent = (4 - i) * 25;
                String pctText = percent + "%";
                g.drawString(mc.font, pctText,
                        x + BAR_WIDTH + 5, tickY - 4,
                        0x6000B8D0, false);
            }
        }

        int bw = mc.font.width(label);
        int bx = x + BAR_WIDTH / 2 - bw / 2;
        int by = y - 18;

        g.fill(bx - 2, by - 1, bx + bw + 2, by + 9, 0xB3000D14);
        g.fill(bx - 2, by - 1, bx + bw + 2, by, COLOR_BORDER);
        g.fill(bx - 2, by + 8, bx + bw + 2, by + 9, COLOR_BORDER);

        g.drawString(mc.font, label, bx, by, COLOR_TEXT_MAIN, true);

        String valueText = String.valueOf(current);
        int vw = mc.font.width(valueText);
        int vx = x + BAR_WIDTH / 2 - vw / 2;
        int vy = y + BAR_HEIGHT + 5;

        g.fill(vx - 2, vy - 1, vx + vw + 2, vy + 9, 0xB3000D14);
        g.fill(vx - 2, vy - 1, vx + vw + 2, vy, COLOR_BORDER);
        g.fill(vx - 2, vy + 8, vx + vw + 2, vy + 9, COLOR_BORDER);

        g.drawString(mc.font, valueText, vx, vy, COLOR_TEXT_VALUE, true);
    }

    private static void drawStatusBar(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y, int width, OxygenData data, boolean hasTank) {
        int statusY = y + 20;

        g.fill(x, statusY, x + width, statusY + 1, 0x6000B8D0);

        String symbol;
        int statusColor;

        int total = data.getOxygen() + data.getTankOxygen();
        int totalMax = data.getBaseMax() + data.getTankMax();
        float pct = totalMax <= 0 ? 0.0f : (float) total / totalMax;

        if (pct > 0.5f) {
            symbol = "●";
            statusColor = 0xCC00CC70;
        } else if (pct > 0.25f) {
            symbol = "▲";
            statusColor = 0xCCCC9000;
        } else if (pct > 0) {
            symbol = "◆";
            statusColor = 0xCCCC5000;
        } else {
            symbol = "✖";
            statusColor = 0xCCCC1030;
        }

        int totalPct = (int) (pct * 100);
        String status = symbol + " " + totalPct + "%";

        g.drawString(mc.font, status, x + width / 2 - mc.font.width(status) / 2, statusY + 4, statusColor, true);

        if (!hasTank) {
            String hint = "← 使用氧气罐扩展";
            int hw = mc.font.width(hint);
            g.drawString(mc.font, hint,
                    x + width / 2 - hw / 2, statusY + 16,
                    COLOR_TEXT_HINT, false);
        }
    }

    private static int applyPulse(int argb, float pulseFactor) {
        int a = (argb >> 24) & 0xFF;
        int newA = (int) (a * (0.5f + 0.5f * pulseFactor));
        return (newA << 24) | (argb & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\gui\PlanetStatusHud.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.gui;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client.JupiterStormClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.server.JupiterStormManager;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client.MarsDustStormClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client.MarsVolcanoClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network.MarsVolcanoDebugPayload;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client.MercurySolarFlareClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.client.IoQuakeClientCache;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.network.IoQuakeDebugPayload;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug.DebugState;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug.HudInteractionState;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.config.NewGalaxyConfig;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class PlanetStatusHud {

    private static final int WIDTH      = 210;
    private static final int PADDING    = 10;
    private static final int HEADER_H   = 20;
    private static final int ROW_H      = 15;
    private static final int CARD_GAP   = 6;
    private static final int MARGIN_TOP = 60;
    private static final int MARGIN_RIGHT = 10;
    private static final int CARD_3ROW  = ROW_H * 3 + PADDING * 2;

    private static final int BG          = 0xE00A121E;
    private static final int BG_INNER    = 0x90142436;
    private static final int BORDER_OUT  = 0xFF3A5570;
    private static final int BORDER_IN   = 0xFF1A2A3C;
    private static final int TITLE       = 0xFFB8D0F0;
    private static final int LABEL       = 0xFF8898B0;
    private static final int VALUE       = 0xFFE0ECFF;
    private static final int SEPARATOR   = 0xFF2A3E54;

    private static final int[] PHASE_COLORS = {
            0xFF7A8FA6, 0xFFE0A050, 0xFFE07A3A, 0xFFFF5B3A, 0xFFB07040
    };

    private static final int BAR_BG_TOP  = 0xFF0A1420;
    private static final int BAR_BG_BOT  = 0xFF060C14;
    private static final int BAR_TICK    = 0x60FFFFFF;

    private static final int MARS_DOT    = 0xFFE06B4B;
    private static final int DUST_DOT    = 0xFFC89650;
    private static final int FLARE_DOT   = 0xFFE0EEFF;
    private static final int IO_DOT      = 0xFFD4C84B; // 硫磺黄

    private static final int DEBUG_BTN_W = 40;
    private static final int DEBUG_BTN_H = 16;
    private static final int DEBUG_BTN_GAP = 4;

    private static int lastHudX = 0;
    private static int lastHudY = 0;
    private static int lastHudW = 0;
    private static int lastHudH = 0;
    private static boolean debugButtonsVisible = false;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;
        if (mc.options.hideGui) return;

        if (!NewGalaxyConfig.CLIENT.debugMode.get()) {
            DebugState.resetAll();
        }

        boolean inJupiter = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY);
        boolean inMars    = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY);
        boolean inMercury = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY);
        boolean inIo      = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.IO_LEVEL_KEY);

        if (!inJupiter && !inMars && !inMercury && !inIo) {
            debugButtonsVisible = false;
            return;
        }

        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int screenW = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();

        int x = screenW - WIDTH - MARGIN_RIGHT;
        int y = MARGIN_TOP;

        int jupiterH = inJupiter ? CARD_3ROW : 0;
        int marsH = inMars ? CARD_3ROW * 2 : 0;
        int mercuryH = inMercury ? CARD_3ROW : 0;
        int ioH = inIo ? CARD_3ROW : 0;

        int cardCount = (inJupiter ? 1 : 0) + (inMars ? 2 : 0) + (inMercury ? 1 : 0) + (inIo ? 1 : 0);
        int totalH = HEADER_H + jupiterH + marsH + mercuryH + ioH
                + Math.max(0, cardCount - 1) * CARD_GAP
                + PADDING + 2;

        g.fill(x - 1, y - 1, x + WIDTH + 1, y + totalH + 1, 0x40000000);
        g.fill(x, y, x + WIDTH, y + totalH, BG);
        drawBorder(g, x, y, WIDTH, totalH, BORDER_OUT);

        g.fill(x + 1, y + 1, x + WIDTH - 1, y + 2, BORDER_IN);
        g.fill(x + 1, y + totalH - 2, x + WIDTH - 1, y + totalH - 1, BORDER_IN);

        String planetName;
        if (inJupiter)      planetName = "木星 · 风暴";
        else if (inMars)    planetName = "火星 · 地表";
        else if (inMercury) planetName = "水星 · 耀斑";
        else                planetName = "木卫一 · 地震";

        g.drawString(mc.font, "◈ 星球状态", x + PADDING, y + 6, TITLE, false);
        int nameW = mc.font.width(planetName);
        g.drawString(mc.font, planetName, x + WIDTH - PADDING - nameW, y + 6, TITLE, false);

        g.fill(x + PADDING, y + HEADER_H - 3, x + WIDTH - PADDING, y + HEADER_H - 2, SEPARATOR);

        int cy = y + HEADER_H;
        if (inJupiter) cy = drawJupiterCard(g, mc, x, cy);
        if (inMars) {
            cy = drawVolcanoCard(g, mc, x, cy);
            cy = drawDustStormCard(g, mc, x, cy);
        }
        if (inMercury) cy = drawSolarFlareCard(g, mc, x, cy);
        if (inIo)      cy = drawIoQuakeCard(g, mc, x, cy);

        lastHudX = x;
        lastHudY = y;
        lastHudW = WIDTH;
        lastHudH = totalH;

        if (NewGalaxyConfig.CLIENT.debugMode.get()) {
            debugButtonsVisible = true;
            drawDebugButtons(g, mc, x, y + totalH + 6, inJupiter, inMars, inMercury, inIo);
        } else {
            debugButtonsVisible = false;
        }
    }

    // ================= 木星卡片 =================
    private static int drawJupiterCard(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y) {
        JupiterStormClientCache.Phase phase = JupiterStormClientCache.getPhase();
        int phaseColor = PHASE_COLORS[phase.ordinal()];
        float strength = JupiterStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        int remain = JupiterStormClientCache.getPhaseTimer();

        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + WIDTH - PADDING, y + CARD_3ROW, BG_INNER);
        g.fill(x + PADDING - 1, y, x + PADDING + 4, y + CARD_3ROW,
                0x40FFFFFF & phaseColor | 0x40000000);
        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + PADDING + 3, y + CARD_3ROW, phaseColor);

        int tx = x + PADDING + 9;
        int ty = y + PADDING;
        int rightEdge = x + WIDTH - PADDING - 8;

        g.drawString(mc.font, "■ 木星风暴", tx, ty, TITLE, false);

        String phaseName = phaseName(phase);
        int pW = mc.font.width(phaseName);
        int pillPad = 4;
        int pillX = rightEdge - pW - pillPad * 2;
        int pillY = ty - 1;
        g.fill(pillX, pillY, rightEdge, pillY + 10, 0x60000000 | (phaseColor & 0x00FFFFFF));
        g.drawString(mc.font, phaseName, pillX + pillPad, ty, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);

        ty += ROW_H;
        drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "阶段剩余", formatTicks(remain), VALUE);
        ty += ROW_H;
        drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "风暴强度", String.format("%.2f", strength), VALUE);

        drawStormBar(g, x + PADDING + 9, ty + ROW_H - 5,
                WIDTH - PADDING * 2 - 17 - 8, strength, phaseColor);

        return y + CARD_3ROW + CARD_GAP;
    }

    // ================= 火星 · 火山 =================
    private static int drawVolcanoCard(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y) {
        int eruption = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getEruptionTicks();
        int total = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getEruptionTotalTicks();
        int cooldown = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getCooldownTimer();
        BlockPos vp = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getVolcanoPos();
        boolean erupting = MarsVolcanoClientCache.isErupting();

        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + WIDTH - PADDING, y + CARD_3ROW, BG_INNER);
        g.fill(x + PADDING - 1, y, x + PADDING + 4, y + CARD_3ROW,
                0x40FFFFFF & MARS_DOT | 0x40000000);
        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + PADDING + 3, y + CARD_3ROW, MARS_DOT);

        int tx = x + PADDING + 9;
        int ty = y + PADDING;
        int rightEdge = x + WIDTH - PADDING - 8;

        g.drawString(mc.font, "■ 火星火山", tx, ty, TITLE, false);

        String state = erupting ? "喷发中" : "冷却中";
        int sW = mc.font.width(state);
        int pillPad = 4;
        int pillX = rightEdge - sW - pillPad * 2;
        int pillY = ty - 1;
        int pillColor = erupting ? MARS_DOT : 0xFF4A5A6E;
        g.fill(pillX, pillY, rightEdge, pillY + 10, 0x60000000 | (pillColor & 0x00FFFFFF));
        g.drawString(mc.font, state, pillX + pillPad, ty, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);

        ty += ROW_H;

        if (erupting) {
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "喷发剩余", formatTicks(eruption), VALUE);
            ty += ROW_H;
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "本次总时长", formatTicks(total), VALUE);
            float progress = total > 0 ? (float) eruption / total : 0f;
            drawProgressBar(g, x + PADDING + 9, ty + ROW_H - 5,
                    WIDTH - PADDING * 2 - 17 - 8, progress, MARS_DOT);
        } else {
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "冷却剩余", formatTicks(cooldown), VALUE);
            ty += ROW_H;
            String dist = "—";
            if (vp != null) {
                Vec3 p = mc.player.position();
                double d = Math.sqrt(p.distanceToSqr(
                        vp.getX() + 0.5, vp.getY() + 0.5, vp.getZ() + 0.5));
                dist = String.format("%.1f m", d);
            }
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "喷发点距离", dist, VALUE);
        }

        return y + CARD_3ROW + CARD_GAP;
    }

    // ================= 火星 · 沙尘暴 =================
    private static int drawDustStormCard(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y) {
        MarsDustStormClientCache.Phase phase = MarsDustStormClientCache.getPhase();
        int phaseTimer = MarsDustStormClientCache.getPhaseTimer();
        float strength = MarsDustStormClientCache.getStormStrength();

        int phaseColor = dustPhaseColor(phase);

        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + WIDTH - PADDING, y + CARD_3ROW, BG_INNER);
        g.fill(x + PADDING - 1, y, x + PADDING + 4, y + CARD_3ROW,
                0x40FFFFFF & DUST_DOT | 0x40000000);
        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + PADDING + 3, y + CARD_3ROW, DUST_DOT);

        int tx = x + PADDING + 9;
        int ty = y + PADDING;
        int rightEdge = x + WIDTH - PADDING - 8;

        g.drawString(mc.font, "■ 火星沙尘暴", tx, ty, TITLE, false);

        String phaseName = dustPhaseName(phase);
        int pW = mc.font.width(phaseName);
        int pillPad = 4;
        int pillX = rightEdge - pW - pillPad * 2;
        int pillY = ty - 1;
        g.fill(pillX, pillY, rightEdge, pillY + 10, 0x60000000 | (phaseColor & 0x00FFFFFF));
        g.drawString(mc.font, phaseName, pillX + pillPad, ty, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);

        ty += ROW_H;
        drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "阶段剩余", formatTicks(phaseTimer), VALUE);
        ty += ROW_H;
        drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "沙尘强度", String.format("%.2f", strength), VALUE);

        drawDustStormBar(g, x + PADDING + 9, ty + ROW_H - 5,
                WIDTH - PADDING * 2 - 17 - 8, strength, phaseColor);

        return y + CARD_3ROW + CARD_GAP;
    }

    // ================= 水星 · 太阳耀斑 =================
    private static int drawSolarFlareCard(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y) {
        MercurySolarFlareClientCache.Phase phase = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getPhase();
        int phaseTimer = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getPhaseTimer();
        float intensity = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getIntensity();

        int phaseColor = flarePhaseColor(phase);

        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + WIDTH - PADDING, y + CARD_3ROW, BG_INNER);
        g.fill(x + PADDING - 1, y, x + PADDING + 4, y + CARD_3ROW,
                0x40FFFFFF & FLARE_DOT | 0x40000000);
        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + PADDING + 3, y + CARD_3ROW, FLARE_DOT);

        int tx = x + PADDING + 9;
        int ty = y + PADDING;
        int rightEdge = x + WIDTH - PADDING - 8;

        g.drawString(mc.font, "■ 太阳耀斑", tx, ty, TITLE, false);

        String phaseName = flarePhaseName(phase);
        int pW = mc.font.width(phaseName);
        int pillPad = 4;
        int pillX = rightEdge - pW - pillPad * 2;
        int pillY = ty - 1;
        g.fill(pillX, pillY, rightEdge, pillY + 10, 0x60000000 | (phaseColor & 0x00FFFFFF));
        g.drawString(mc.font, phaseName, pillX + pillPad, ty, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);

        ty += ROW_H;
        drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "阶段剩余", formatTicks(phaseTimer), VALUE);
        ty += ROW_H;
        drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "耀斑强度", String.format("%.2f", intensity), VALUE);

        drawSolarFlareBar(g, x + PADDING + 9, ty + ROW_H - 5,
                WIDTH - PADDING * 2 - 17 - 8, intensity, phaseColor);

        return y + CARD_3ROW + CARD_GAP;
    }

    // ================= 木卫一 · 地震 =================
    private static int drawIoQuakeCard(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y) {
        boolean quaking = IoQuakeClientCache.isQuaking();
        int quakeTicks = IoQuakeClientCache.getQuakeTicks();
        int totalTicks = IoQuakeClientCache.getQuakeTotalTicks();
        int cooldown = IoQuakeClientCache.getCooldownTimer();
        float intensity = IoQuakeClientCache.getQuakeIntensity();

        int dotColor = quaking ? IO_DOT : 0xFF4A5A6E;

        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + WIDTH - PADDING, y + CARD_3ROW, BG_INNER);
        g.fill(x + PADDING - 1, y, x + PADDING + 4, y + CARD_3ROW, 0x40FFFFFF & dotColor | 0x40000000);
        g.fill(x + PADDING, y, x + PADDING + 3, y + CARD_3ROW, dotColor);

        int tx = x + PADDING + 9;
        int ty = y + PADDING;
        int rightEdge = x + WIDTH - PADDING - 8;

        g.drawString(mc.font, "■ 木卫一地震", tx, ty, TITLE, false);

        String state = quaking ? "震动中" : "平静";
        int sW = mc.font.width(state);
        int pillPad = 4;
        int pillX = rightEdge - sW - pillPad * 2;
        int pillY = ty - 1;
        g.fill(pillX, pillY, rightEdge, pillY + 10, 0x60000000 | (dotColor & 0x00FFFFFF));
        g.drawString(mc.font, state, pillX + pillPad, ty, 0xFFFFFFFF, false);

        ty += ROW_H;

        if (quaking) {
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "震动剩余", formatTicks(quakeTicks), VALUE);
            ty += ROW_H;
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "地震烈度", String.format("%.2f", intensity), VALUE);
            float progress = totalTicks > 0 ? (float) quakeTicks / totalTicks : 0f;
            drawProgressBar(g, x + PADDING + 9, ty + ROW_H - 5, WIDTH - PADDING * 2 - 17 - 8, progress, IO_DOT);
        } else {
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "地质平静", "—", VALUE);
            ty += ROW_H;
            drawRow(g, mc, tx, ty, rightEdge, "下次地震预估", formatTicks(cooldown), VALUE);
            drawProgressBar(g, x + PADDING + 9, ty + ROW_H - 5, WIDTH - PADDING * 2 - 17 - 8, 0f, IO_DOT);
        }

        return y + CARD_3ROW + CARD_GAP;
    }

    // ================= 进度条与工具 =================
    private static void drawStormBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, float strength, int phaseColor) {
        int h = 7;
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h / 2, BAR_BG_TOP);
        g.fill(x, y + h / 2, x + w, y + h, BAR_BG_BOT);

        float t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, strength / 2.5f));
        int fillW = (int) (w * t);

        int fillColor;
        if (strength > 1.8f)      fillColor = 0xFFFF4A2A;
        else if (strength > 1.0f) fillColor = 0xFFFF8A3A;
        else if (strength > 0.5f) fillColor = 0xFFE0A050;
        else                      fillColor = phaseColor;

        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + h, fillColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + 1, lighten(fillColor, 0x40));
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + fillW, y + h, darken(fillColor, 0x40));

        for (float tick : new float[]{0.2f, 0.4f, 0.6f, 0.8f}) {
            int tx = x + (int) (w * tick);
            g.fill(tx, y, tx + 1, y + h, BAR_TICK);
        }

        drawBorder(g, x, y, w, h, 0xFF1A2A3C);
        if (fillW > 0 && fillW < w) {
            g.fill(x + fillW, y - 1, x + fillW + 1, y + h + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }
    }

    private static void drawDustStormBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, float strength, int phaseColor) {
        int h = 7;
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h / 2, BAR_BG_TOP);
        g.fill(x, y + h / 2, x + w, y + h, BAR_BG_BOT);

        float t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, strength / 1.5f));
        int fillW = (int) (w * t);

        int fillColor;
        if (strength > 1.0f)      fillColor = 0xFFC89650;
        else if (strength > 0.5f) fillColor = 0xFFA8823C;
        else                      fillColor = phaseColor;

        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + h, fillColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + 1, lighten(fillColor, 0x40));
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + fillW, y + h, darken(fillColor, 0x40));

        for (float tick : new float[]{1f / 3f, 2f / 3f}) {
            int tx = x + (int) (w * tick);
            g.fill(tx, y, tx + 1, y + h, BAR_TICK);
        }

        drawBorder(g, x, y, w, h, 0xFF1A2A3C);
        if (fillW > 0 && fillW < w) {
            g.fill(x + fillW, y - 1, x + fillW + 1, y + h + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }
    }

    private static void drawSolarFlareBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, float intensity, int phaseColor) {
        int h = 7;
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h / 2, BAR_BG_TOP);
        g.fill(x, y + h / 2, x + w, y + h, BAR_BG_BOT);

        float t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, intensity / 1.5f));
        int fillW = (int) (w * t);

        int fillColor;
        if (intensity > 1.0f)      fillColor = 0xFFFF4040;
        else if (intensity > 0.5f) fillColor = 0xFFE0A050;
        else                       fillColor = phaseColor;

        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + h, fillColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + 1, lighten(fillColor, 0x40));
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + fillW, y + h, darken(fillColor, 0x40));

        for (float tick : new float[]{1f / 3f, 2f / 3f}) {
            int tx = x + (int) (w * tick);
            g.fill(tx, y, tx + 1, y + h, BAR_TICK);
        }

        drawBorder(g, x, y, w, h, 0xFF1A2A3C);
        if (fillW > 0 && fillW < w) {
            g.fill(x + fillW, y - 1, x + fillW + 1, y + h + 1, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }
    }

    private static void drawProgressBar(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, float progress, int color) {
        int h = 7;
        progress = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, progress));

        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h / 2, BAR_BG_TOP);
        g.fill(x, y + h / 2, x + w, y + h, BAR_BG_BOT);

        int fillW = (int) (w * progress);
        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + h, color);
        g.fill(x, y, x + fillW, y + 1, lighten(color, 0x40));
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + fillW, y + h, darken(color, 0x40));

        drawBorder(g, x, y, w, h, 0xFF1A2A3C);
    }

    private static String phaseName(JupiterStormClientCache.Phase p) {
        return switch (p) {
            case CALM    -> "平静";
            case RISING  -> "增强";
            case PEAK    -> "峰值";
            case SUDDEN  -> "暴烈";
            case FALLING -> "衰退";
        };
    }

    private static String dustPhaseName(MarsDustStormClientCache.Phase p) {
        return switch (p) {
            case CALM    -> "平静";
            case RISING  -> "扬尘";
            case PEAK    -> "沙暴";
            case FALLING -> "消退";
        };
    }

    private static int dustPhaseColor(MarsDustStormClientCache.Phase p) {
        return switch (p) {
            case CALM    -> 0xFF6A5830;
            case RISING  -> 0xFFA8823C;
            case PEAK    -> 0xFFC89650;
            case FALLING -> 0xFF8A7040;
        };
    }

    private static String flarePhaseName(MercurySolarFlareClientCache.Phase p) {
        return switch (p) {
            case CALM    -> "平静";
            case BUILDUP -> "预警";
            case FLARE   -> "爆发";
            case DECAY   -> "消退";
        };
    }

    private static int flarePhaseColor(MercurySolarFlareClientCache.Phase p) {
        return switch (p) {
            case CALM    -> 0xFF7A8FA6;
            case BUILDUP -> 0xFFE0A050;
            case FLARE   -> 0xFFFF4040;
            case DECAY   -> 0xFFB07040;
        };
    }

    // ================= 调试按钮 =================
    private static void drawDebugButtons(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc,
                                         int x, int y,
                                         boolean inJupiter, boolean inMars, boolean inMercury, boolean inIo) {
        int btnX = x;
        int btnY = y;

        if (inJupiter) {
            String[] names = {"平静", "增强", "峰值", "暴烈", "恢复", "劈雷"};
            int[] phases = {
                    DebugState.JUPITER_CALM,
                    DebugState.JUPITER_RISING,
                    DebugState.JUPITER_PEAK,
                    DebugState.JUPITER_SUDDEN,
                    DebugState.JUPITER_NONE,
                    -999
            };
            int current = DebugState.getJupiterPhase();
            for (int i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
                boolean active = (phases[i] == -999) ? JupiterStormManager.isForceThunder() : phases[i] == current;
                drawDebugButton(g, mc, btnX, btnY, names[i], active);
                btnX += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            }
        } else if (inMars) {
            String[] dustNames = {"平静", "扬尘", "沙暴", "恢复"};
            int[] dustPhases = {
                    DebugState.MARS_DUST_CALM,
                    DebugState.MARS_DUST_RISING,
                    DebugState.MARS_DUST_PEAK,
                    DebugState.MARS_DUST_NONE
            };
            int dustCurrent = DebugState.getMarsDustPhase();
            for (int i = 0; i < dustNames.length; i++) {
                drawDebugButton(g, mc, btnX, btnY, dustNames[i], dustPhases[i] == dustCurrent);
                btnX += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            }

            int row2Y = btnY + DEBUG_BTN_H + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            int btnX2 = x;

            boolean erupting = MarsVolcanoClientCache.isErupting();
            drawDebugButton(g, mc, btnX2, row2Y, "喷发", erupting);
            btnX2 += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;

            drawDebugButton(g, mc, btnX2, row2Y, "冷却", !erupting && MarsVolcanoClientCache.getCooldownTimer() > 0);
        } else if (inMercury) {
            String[] names = {"平静", "预警", "爆发", "消退", "恢复"};
            int[] phases = {
                    DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_CALM,
                    DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_BUILDUP,
                    DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_FLARE,
                    DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_DECAY,
                    DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_NONE
            };
            int current = DebugState.getMercuryFlarePhase();
            for (int i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
                drawDebugButton(g, mc, btnX, btnY, names[i], phases[i] == current);
                btnX += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            }
        } else if (inIo) {
            boolean quaking = IoQuakeClientCache.isQuaking();
            drawDebugButton(g, mc, btnX, btnY, "地震", quaking);
            btnX += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            drawDebugButton(g, mc, btnX, btnY, "平静", !quaking);
        }
    }

    private static void drawDebugButton(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc,
                                        int x, int y, String label, boolean active) {
        int bgColor = active ? 0xFF3A5570 : 0xB0142436;
        int borderColor = active ? 0xFF7AA0C0 : 0xFF2F4157;
        int textColor = active ? 0xFFFFFFFF : 0xC0D0E0;

        g.fill(x, y, x + DEBUG_BTN_W, y + DEBUG_BTN_H, bgColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + DEBUG_BTN_W, y + 1, borderColor);
        g.fill(x, y + DEBUG_BTN_H - 1, x + DEBUG_BTN_W, y + DEBUG_BTN_H, borderColor);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + DEBUG_BTN_H, borderColor);
        g.fill(x + DEBUG_BTN_W - 1, y, x + DEBUG_BTN_W, y + DEBUG_BTN_H, borderColor);

        int textW = mc.font.width(label);
        g.drawString(mc.font, label, x + (DEBUG_BTN_W - textW) / 2, y + (DEBUG_BTN_H - 8) / 2, textColor, false);
    }

    // ================= 点击判定 =================
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onMouseClick(InputEvent.MouseButton.Pre event) {
        if (event.getAction() != 1) return;
        if (!NewGalaxyConfig.CLIENT.debugMode.get()) return;
        if (!debugButtonsVisible) return;
        if (!HudInteractionState.isActive()) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null || mc.screen != null) return;

        double mouseX = mc.mouseHandler.xpos() * mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth() / mc.getWindow().getWidth();
        double mouseY = mc.mouseHandler.ypos() * mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight() / mc.getWindow().getHeight();

        boolean inJupiter = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY);
        boolean inMars    = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY);
        boolean inMercury = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY);
        boolean inIo      = mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.IO_LEVEL_KEY);

        int btnY = lastHudY + lastHudH + 6;
        int btnX = lastHudX;

        if (inJupiter) {
            int[] phases = {
                    DebugState.JUPITER_CALM, DebugState.JUPITER_RISING, DebugState.JUPITER_PEAK,
                    DebugState.JUPITER_SUDDEN, DebugState.JUPITER_NONE, -999
            };
            for (int i = 0; i < phases.length; i++) {
                if (isHover(mouseX, mouseY, btnX, btnY, DEBUG_BTN_W, DEBUG_BTN_H)) {
                    if (phases[i] == -999) {
                        JupiterStormManager.setForceThunder(!JupiterStormManager.isForceThunder());
                    } else {
                        DebugState.setJupiterPhase(phases[i]);
                    }
                    event.setCanceled(true);
                    return;
                }
                btnX += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            }
        } else if (inMars) {
            int[] dustPhases = {
                    DebugState.MARS_DUST_CALM, DebugState.MARS_DUST_RISING,
                    DebugState.MARS_DUST_PEAK, DebugState.MARS_DUST_NONE
            };
            for (int phase : dustPhases) {
                if (isHover(mouseX, mouseY, btnX, btnY, DEBUG_BTN_W, DEBUG_BTN_H)) {
                    DebugState.setMarsDustPhase(phase);
                    event.setCanceled(true);
                    return;
                }
                btnX += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            }

            int row2Y = btnY + DEBUG_BTN_H + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            int btnX2 = lastHudX;

            if (isHover(mouseX, mouseY, btnX2, row2Y, DEBUG_BTN_W, DEBUG_BTN_H)) {
                PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new MarsVolcanoDebugPayload(MarsVolcanoDebugPayload.MODE_ERUPT));
                event.setCanceled(true);
                return;
            }
            btnX2 += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;

            if (isHover(mouseX, mouseY, btnX2, row2Y, DEBUG_BTN_W, DEBUG_BTN_H)) {
                PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new MarsVolcanoDebugPayload(MarsVolcanoDebugPayload.MODE_COOLDOWN));
                event.setCanceled(true);
                return;
            }
        } else if (inMercury) {
            int[] phases = {
                    DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_CALM, DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_BUILDUP,
                    DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_FLARE, DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_DECAY, DebugState.MERCURY_FLARE_NONE
            };
            for (int phase : phases) {
                if (isHover(mouseX, mouseY, btnX, btnY, DEBUG_BTN_W, DEBUG_BTN_H)) {
                    DebugState.setMercuryFlarePhase(phase);
                    event.setCanceled(true);
                    return;
                }
                btnX += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;
            }
        } else if (inIo) {
            int btnX2 = lastHudX;

            // 地震按钮
            if (isHover(mouseX, mouseY, btnX2, btnY, DEBUG_BTN_W, DEBUG_BTN_H)) {
                PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new IoQuakeDebugPayload(IoQuakeDebugPayload.MODE_QUAKE));
                event.setCanceled(true);
                return;
            }
            btnX2 += DEBUG_BTN_W + DEBUG_BTN_GAP;

            // 平静按钮
            if (isHover(mouseX, mouseY, btnX2, btnY, DEBUG_BTN_W, DEBUG_BTN_H)) {
                PacketDistributor.sendToServer(new IoQuakeDebugPayload(IoQuakeDebugPayload.MODE_CALM));
                event.setCanceled(true);
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean isHover(double mx, double my, int x, int y, int w, int h) {
        return mx >= x && mx <= x + w && my >= y && my <= y + h;
    }

    private static void drawRow(GuiGraphics g, Minecraft mc, int x, int y, int rightEdge, String label, String value, int valueColor) {
        g.drawString(mc.font, label, x, y, LABEL, false);
        int vW = mc.font.width(value);
        g.drawString(mc.font, value, rightEdge - vW, y, valueColor, false);
    }

    private static String formatTicks(int ticks) {
        if (ticks <= 0) return "0s";
        float sec = ticks / 20f;
        if (sec >= 60f) {
            int m = (int) (sec / 60);
            int s = (int) (sec % 60);
            return m + "m " + s + "s";
        }
        return String.format("%.1fs", sec);
    }

    private static void drawBorder(GuiGraphics g, int x, int y, int w, int h, int color) {
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, color);
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, color);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, color);
        g.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, color);
    }

    private static int lighten(int color, int amount) {
        int a = (color >>> 24) & 0xFF;
        int r = Math.min(255, ((color >>> 16) & 0xFF) + amount);
        int g = Math.min(255, ((color >>> 8) & 0xFF) + amount);
        int b = Math.min(255, (color & 0xFF) + amount);
        return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
    }

    private static int darken(int color, int amount) {
        int a = (color >>> 24) & 0xFF;
        int r = Math.max(0, ((color >>> 16) & 0xFF) - amount);
        int g = Math.max(0, ((color >>> 8) & 0xFF) - amount);
        int b = Math.max(0, (color & 0xFF) - amount);
        return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
    }
}

    private static class Moon {
        final String nameKey;
        final int orbitA, orbitB;
        final float speed;
        final float phase;
        final int iconSize;
        final int displaySize;
        final ResourceLocation iconTexture;
        final ResourceKey<Level> dimension;
        final String cachedName;

        Moon(String nameKey, int orbitA, int orbitB, float speed, float phase,
             int iconSize, int displaySize, ResourceLocation iconTexture,
             ResourceKey<Level> dimension) {
            this.nameKey = nameKey;
            this.orbitA = orbitA;
            this.orbitB = orbitB;
            this.speed = speed;
            this.phase = phase;
            this.iconSize = iconSize;
            this.displaySize = displaySize;
            this.iconTexture = iconTexture;
            this.dimension = dimension;
            this.cachedName = Component.translatable(nameKey).getString();
        }

        String displayName() { return cachedName; }
        boolean isTeleportable() { return dimension != null; }

        float relX(long openedAt) {
            long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - openedAt;
            float angle = (elapsed / 8000.0f) * speed * (float) (Math.PI * 2) + phase;
            return orbitA * (float) Math.cos(angle);
        }
        float relY(long openedAt) {
            long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - openedAt;
            float angle = (elapsed / 8000.0f) * speed * (float) (Math.PI * 2) + phase;
            return orbitB * (float) Math.sin(angle);
        }
    }

    private static class Planet {
        final String nameKey;
        final String dimId;
        final int orbitA, orbitB;
        final float speed;
        final float phase;
        final int radiusPx;
        final ResourceKey<Level> dimension;
        final ResourceLocation iconTexture;
        final int iconSize;
        final int displaySize;
        final boolean isDwarf;
        final List<Moon> moons;
        final String cachedName;

        Planet(String nameKey, String dimId, int orbitA, int orbitB, float speed, float phase,
               int radiusPx, ResourceKey<Level> dimension,
               ResourceLocation iconTexture, int iconSize, int displaySize,
               boolean isDwarf, List<Moon> moons) {
            this.nameKey = nameKey;
            this.dimId = dimId;
            this.orbitA = orbitA;
            this.orbitB = orbitB;
            this.speed = speed;
            this.phase = phase;
            this.radiusPx = radiusPx;
            this.dimension = dimension;
            this.iconTexture = iconTexture;
            this.iconSize = iconSize;
            this.displaySize = displaySize;
            this.isDwarf = isDwarf;
            this.moons = moons;
            this.cachedName = Component.translatable(nameKey).getString();
        }

        String displayName() { return cachedName; }
        boolean isTeleportable() { return dimension != null; }

        float worldX(long openedAt) {
            long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - openedAt;
            float angle = (elapsed / 8000.0f) * speed * (float) (Math.PI * 2) + phase;
            return orbitA * (float) Math.cos(angle);
        }
        float worldY(long openedAt) {
            long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - openedAt;
            float angle = (elapsed / 8000.0f) * speed * (float) (Math.PI * 2) + phase;
            return orbitB * (float) Math.sin(angle);
        }
    }

    private final List<Planet> planets = new ArrayList<>();

    private static final int SUN_ICON_SIZE = 16;

    private static class Selection {
        Planet planet;
        int moonIndex;
        boolean isMoon() { return moonIndex >= 0; }

        ResourceKey<Level> getDimension() {
            if (isMoon()) return planet.moons.get(moonIndex).dimension;
            return planet.dimension;
        }

        boolean isTeleportable() { return getDimension() != null; }

        String displayName() {
            if (isMoon()) return planet.moons.get(moonIndex).displayName();
            return planet.displayName();
        }
    }

    private enum Mode { MAP, FOCUS_PLANET, FOCUS_MOON }
    private Mode mode = Mode.MAP;

    private Planet focusedPlanet = null;

    public StarMapScreen() {
        super(Component.translatable("gui.newgc.star_map"));
    }

    private int teleportBtnX() { return this.width - BTN_W - BTN_MARGIN_R; }
    private int teleportBtnY() { return BTN_Y; }

    private boolean isTeleportBtnActive() {
        if (toastMessage != null) return false;
        if (selected == null) return false;
        if (!selected.isTeleportable()) return false;
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return false;
        return !mc.level.dimension().equals(selected.getDimension());
    }

    private Selection getTeleportTarget() {
        if (selected == null) return null;
        if (!selected.isTeleportable()) return null;
        return selected;
    }
