📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\deimos\client\DeimosSkyRenderer.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.deimos.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class DeimosSkyRenderer {

    private static final int STAR_COUNT = 3500;
    private static final float STAR_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 贴图 ====================
    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EARTH_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/earth_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation VENUS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/venus_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation MARS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/mars_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation JUPITER_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/jupiter_icon.png");

    // ==================== 天球距离 ====================
    private static final float SKY_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 视角直径 ====================
    private static final float ANGULAR_SCALE = 8.0f;

    private static final float SUN_ANGULAR_REAL     = 1.4f;
    private static final float EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL   = 0.10f;
    private static final float VENUS_ANGULAR_REAL   = 0.10f;
    private static final float JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL = 0.12f;

    // 火星：火卫二比火卫一离火星更远，火星看起来更小（真实约 16.6°，火卫一约 40°）
    private static final float MARS_ANGULAR = 16.6f;

    private static final float SUN_SIZE     = sizeFromAngularDiameter(SUN_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float EARTH_SIZE   = sizeFromAngularDiameter(EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float VENUS_SIZE   = sizeFromAngularDiameter(VENUS_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float JUPITER_SIZE = sizeFromAngularDiameter(JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float MARS_SIZE    = sizeFromAngularDiameter(MARS_ANGULAR, SKY_DISTANCE);

    // ==================== 太阳初始位置（正午位置）====================
    // 火卫二轨道同样几乎在火星赤道面上，太阳中午接近天顶
    private static final float SUN_INIT_AZIMUTH  = 180.0f;   // 正南
    private static final float SUN_INIT_ALTITUDE = 65.0f;    // 高挂天空（正午最高）

    private static float sizeFromAngularDiameter(float angularDiameterDeg, float distance) {
        float radiusRad = (float) Math.toRadians(angularDiameterDeg * 0.5f);
        return distance * (float) Math.tan(radiusRad);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.DEIMOS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);

        // 基于 dayTime：0 = 6:00 日出，6000 = 正午，12000 = 日落，18000 = 午夜
        long dayTime = mc.level.getDayTime();
        float dayFrac = ((dayTime % 24000L) + partialTick) / 24000.0f;

        // 天球旋转角度：
        // dayFrac = 0.0（日出）→ 初始位置
        // dayFrac = 0.25（正午）→ 太阳在初始位置（正南高空）
        float skyRotFrac = dayFrac - 0.25f;
        float skyRotRad = skyRotFrac * 2.0f * (float) Math.PI;

        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));

        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // ========== 第一层：随天球旋转（恒星 + 太阳 + 其它行星）==========
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.rotateX(skyRotRad);
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // 恒星背景
        renderStars();

        // 太阳：初始位置 = 正午位置（南天高空）
        renderCelestialBody(SUN_TEXTURE, SUN_SIZE,
                SUN_INIT_AZIMUTH, SUN_INIT_ALTITUDE,
                1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        // 金星：内侧行星，离太阳较近
        renderCelestialBody(VENUS_TEXTURE, VENUS_SIZE,
                140.0f, 48.0f,
                0.95f, 0.95f, 0.85f);

        // 地球：从火星看是内侧行星，偏蓝
        renderCelestialBody(EARTH_TEXTURE, EARTH_SIZE,
                60.0f, 30.0f,
                0.55f, 0.7f, 1.0f);

        // 木星：外侧行星，明亮
        renderCelestialBody(JUPITER_TEXTURE, JUPITER_SIZE,
                320.0f, 25.0f,
                1.0f, 0.95f, 0.8f);

        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // ========== 第二层：潮汐锁定（火星不随天球旋转）==========
        // 火卫二同样被火星潮汐锁定，火星在天空几乎不动
        renderCelestialBody(MARS_TEXTURE, MARS_SIZE,
                90.0f, 35.0f,
                1.0f, 0.8f, 0.7f);

        // ===== 恢复 =====
        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    /** 渲染恒星背景 */
    private static void renderStars() {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator starTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder starBuffer = starTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);

        RandomSource random = RandomSource.create(20843L);
        for (int i = 0; i < STAR_COUNT; i++) {
            float cosTheta = random.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            float sinTheta = Mth.sqrt(1.0f - cosTheta * cosTheta);
            float phi = random.nextFloat() * Mth.TWO_PI;

            float x = sinTheta * Mth.cos(phi);
            float y = cosTheta;
            float z = sinTheta * Mth.sin(phi);

            Vector3f starPos = new Vector3f(x, y, z).mul(STAR_DISTANCE);
            Quaternionf starRot = new Quaternionf().rotateTo(new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F), starPos);
            float size = 0.06f + random.nextFloat() * 0.05f;
            Vector3f starRight = new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);
            Vector3f starUp    = new Vector3f(0.0F, 1.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);

            float brightness = 0.18f + random.nextFloat() * 0.45f;

            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
        }

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(starBuffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 渲染单个天体 */
    private static void renderCelestialBody(ResourceLocation texture, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg,
                                            float r, float g, float b) {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, texture);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(r, g, b, 1.0f);

        Tesselator tess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder buffer = tess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuadAt(buffer, SKY_DISTANCE, size, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(buffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 根据方位角 + 仰角计算三维位置 */
    private static Vector3f skyPosition(float distance, float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        float az = (float) Math.toRadians(azimuthDeg);
        float al = (float) Math.toRadians(altitudeDeg);
        float cosAl = (float) Math.cos(al);
        float x = distance * (float) Math.sin(az) * cosAl;
        float y = distance * (float) Math.sin(al);
        float z = distance * (float) Math.cos(az) * cosAl;
        return new Vector3f(x, y, z);
    }

    /** 绘制始终面向观察者的四边形 */
    private static void drawCelestialQuadAt(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                            float distance, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        Vector3f center = skyPosition(distance, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);

        Vector3f dir = new Vector3f(center).normalize();
        Vector3f worldUp = new Vector3f(0f, 1f, 0f);
        if (Math.abs(dir.y) > 0.99f) worldUp.set(0f, 0f, 1f);

        Vector3f right = new Vector3f(dir).cross(worldUp).normalize().mul(size);
        Vector3f up    = new Vector3f(dir).cross(right).normalize().mul(size);

        Vector3f tl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).add(up);
        Vector3f bl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f br = new Vector3f(center).add(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f tr = new Vector3f(center).add(right).add(up);

        buffer.addVertex(tl.x, tl.y, tl.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bl.x, bl.y, bl.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(br.x, br.y, br.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(tr.x, tr.y, tr.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }
}


================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\io\client\IoCameraShake.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ViewportEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class IoCameraShake {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // 响应速度（因为有包络线，可以稍微快一点）
    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.1f;

    // 震动幅度：地震的幅度通常比风暴大
    private static final float SHAKE_AMPLITUDE = 3.5f;

    // 震动频率：地震的抖动频率更高、更急促
    private static final float SHAKE_FREQUENCY = 0.35f;

    // 滚转（Roll）幅度，模拟地面倾斜感
    private static final float ROLL_AMPLITUDE = 1.5f;

    private static float smoothedStrength = 0.0f;
    private static float shakeTime = 0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onComputeCameraAngles(ViewportEvent.ComputeCameraAngles event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;

        // TODO: 这里请确保替换为你项目里木卫一的真实维度 Key
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.IO_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        // 【关键修改】直接调用你写好的 Cache 类获取强度
        float strength = IoQuakeClientCache.getQuakeIntensity();

        // 容错处理
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0.0f;
        strength = Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(2.5f, strength));

        smoothedStrength += (strength - smoothedStrength) * SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(smoothedStrength)) smoothedStrength = 0.0f;

        // 如果强度极低（没地震或已经结束），直接跳过
        if (smoothedStrength < 0.05f) return;

        // 每次触发累加时间
        shakeTime += 1.0f;

        // 木卫一的地震：主要是上下颠簸（Pitch）和倾斜（Roll）
        // 使用不同的频率倍数，让运动轨迹看起来不规则

        // 倾斜（Roll） - 模拟地震时地面歪斜
        float waveRoll = (float) Math.sin(shakeTime * SHAKE_FREQUENCY * 0.8f) * smoothedStrength * ROLL_AMPLITUDE;

        // 俯仰（Pitch） - 上下颠簸
        float wavePitch = (float) Math.cos(shakeTime * SHAKE_FREQUENCY * 1.7f) * smoothedStrength * SHAKE_AMPLITUDE;

        // 高频随机抖动（模拟岩石碎裂的震感）
        float jitterPitch = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * smoothedStrength * 1.5f;
        float jitterRoll = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * smoothedStrength * 1.5f;

        // 应用修改
        event.setPitch(event.getPitch() + wavePitch + jitterPitch);
        event.setRoll(event.getRoll() + waveRoll + jitterRoll);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\io\client\IoGravityHandler.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class IoGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "io_low_gravity");

    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 0.183;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInIo = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.IO_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInIo && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInIo && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.client;

public class IoQuakeClientCache {
    private static boolean isQuaking = false;
    private static int quakeTicks = 0;
    private static int quakeTotalTicks = 0;
    private static int cooldownTimer = 0;

    public static void set(boolean quaking, int ticks, int totalTicks, int cooldown) {
        isQuaking = quaking;
        quakeTicks = ticks;
        quakeTotalTicks = totalTicks;
        cooldownTimer = cooldown;
    }

    public static boolean isQuaking() { return isQuaking; }
    public static int getQuakeTicks() { return quakeTicks; }
    public static int getQuakeTotalTicks() { return quakeTotalTicks; }
    public static int getCooldownTimer() { return cooldownTimer; }

    public static float getQuakeIntensity() {
        if (!isQuaking || quakeTotalTicks <= 0) return 0f;
        float progress = 1f - (float) quakeTicks / quakeTotalTicks;
        float envelope = 4f * progress * (1f - progress);
        return 0.25f + 0.75f * envelope;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\io\client\IoSkyEffects.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.client;

import net.minecraft.client.renderer.DimensionSpecialEffects;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class IoSkyEffects extends DimensionSpecialEffects {

    public IoSkyEffects() {
        super(0.0f, false, SkyType.NONE, false, false);
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull Vec3 getBrightnessDependentFogColor(@NotNull Vec3 fogColor, float brightness) {

        float t = Mth.clamp(brightness, 0f, 1f);
        t = t * t * (3f - 2f * t);   // 平滑插值

        float r = 0.02f * t;
        float g = 0.02f * t;
        float b = 0.03f * t;
        return new Vec3(r, g, b);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isFoggyAt(int x, int z) {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public float[] getSunriseColor(float timeOfDay, float partialTick) {
        // 无大气 → 没有日出/日落色彩
        return null;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\io\client\IoSkyRenderer.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class IoSkyRenderer {

    private static final int STAR_COUNT = 3500;
    private static final float STAR_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 贴图 ====================
    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EARTH_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/earth_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation MARS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/mars_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation JUPITER_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/jupiter_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation SATURN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/saturn_icon.png");

    // ==================== 天球距离 ====================
    private static final float SKY_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 视角直径 ====================
    private static final float ANGULAR_SCALE = 8.0f;

    // 木卫一距太阳 5.2 AU，太阳看起来比地球小
    private static final float SUN_ANGULAR_REAL     = 1.4f / 5.2f;   // ≈ 0.27°
    private static final float EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL   = 0.10f / 4.2f;  // 地球在木星附近视角更小
    private static final float MARS_ANGULAR_REAL    = 0.10f / 2.2f;
    private static final float SATURN_ANGULAR_REAL  = 0.12f / 1.5f;

    // ★ 木星：木卫一看木星视角直径 ≈ 19.5°（满月约 0.5°，39 倍）
    // 这是木卫一天空最震撼的景观
    private static final float JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL = 26.0f;

    private static final float SUN_SIZE     = sizeFromAngularDiameter(SUN_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float EARTH_SIZE   = sizeFromAngularDiameter(EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float MARS_SIZE    = sizeFromAngularDiameter(MARS_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float SATURN_SIZE  = sizeFromAngularDiameter(SATURN_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    // 木星不用 ANGULAR_SCALE 放大，因为它本来就已经很大了
    private static final float JUPITER_SIZE = sizeFromAngularDiameter(JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL, SKY_DISTANCE);

    // ==================== 太阳初始位置（正午位置）====================
    // 木卫一轨道在木星赤道面附近，太阳正午高挂
    private static final float SUN_INIT_AZIMUTH  = 180.0f;   // 正南
    private static final float SUN_INIT_ALTITUDE = 65.0f;    // 高挂天空

    private static float sizeFromAngularDiameter(float angularDiameterDeg, float distance) {
        float radiusRad = (float) Math.toRadians(angularDiameterDeg * 0.5f);
        return distance * (float) Math.tan(radiusRad);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.IO_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);

        long dayTime = mc.level.getDayTime();
        float dayFrac = ((dayTime % 24000L) + partialTick) / 24000.0f;

        // 木卫一轨道周期约 42.5 小时，但这里按 MC 标准 20 分钟日循环即可
        float skyRotFrac = dayFrac - 0.25f;
        float skyRotRad = skyRotFrac * 2.0f * (float) Math.PI;

        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));

        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // ========== 第一层：随天球旋转（恒星 + 太阳 + 其它行星）==========
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.rotateX(skyRotRad);
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // 恒星背景
        renderStars();

        // 太阳：初始位置 = 正午位置
        renderCelestialBody(SUN_TEXTURE, SUN_SIZE,
                SUN_INIT_AZIMUTH, SUN_INIT_ALTITUDE,
                1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        // 火星：外侧行星，偏红
        renderCelestialBody(MARS_TEXTURE, MARS_SIZE,
                120.0f, 40.0f,
                1.0f, 0.8f, 0.7f);

        // 土星：更外侧行星，带环
        renderCelestialBody(SATURN_TEXTURE, SATURN_SIZE,
                300.0f, 20.0f,
                1.0f, 0.9f, 0.7f);

        // 地球：内侧行星，偏蓝
        renderCelestialBody(EARTH_TEXTURE, EARTH_SIZE,
                40.0f, 25.0f,
                0.55f, 0.7f, 1.0f);

        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // ========== 第二层：潮汐锁定（木星不随天球旋转）==========
        // 木卫一被木星潮汐锁定，木星在天空几乎不动
        // 木星视角直径约 19.5°，非常巨大
        renderCelestialBody(JUPITER_TEXTURE, JUPITER_SIZE,
                90.0f, 30.0f,
                1.0f, 0.85f, 0.75f);

        // ===== 恢复 =====
        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    /** 渲染恒星背景 */
    private static void renderStars() {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator starTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder starBuffer = starTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);

        RandomSource random = RandomSource.create(31415L);
        for (int i = 0; i < STAR_COUNT; i++) {
            float cosTheta = random.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            float sinTheta = Mth.sqrt(1.0f - cosTheta * cosTheta);
            float phi = random.nextFloat() * Mth.TWO_PI;

            float x = sinTheta * Mth.cos(phi);
            float y = cosTheta;
            float z = sinTheta * Mth.sin(phi);

            Vector3f starPos = new Vector3f(x, y, z).mul(STAR_DISTANCE);
            Quaternionf starRot = new Quaternionf().rotateTo(new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F), starPos);
            float size = 0.06f + random.nextFloat() * 0.05f;
            Vector3f starRight = new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);
            Vector3f starUp    = new Vector3f(0.0F, 1.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);

            float brightness = 0.18f + random.nextFloat() * 0.45f;

            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
        }

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(starBuffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 渲染单个天体 */
    private static void renderCelestialBody(ResourceLocation texture, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg,
                                            float r, float g, float b) {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, texture);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(r, g, b, 1.0f);

        Tesselator tess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder buffer = tess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuadAt(buffer, SKY_DISTANCE, size, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(buffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 根据方位角 + 仰角计算三维位置 */
    private static Vector3f skyPosition(float distance, float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        float az = (float) Math.toRadians(azimuthDeg);
        float al = (float) Math.toRadians(altitudeDeg);
        float cosAl = (float) Math.cos(al);
        float x = distance * (float) Math.sin(az) * cosAl;
        float y = distance * (float) Math.sin(al);
        float z = distance * (float) Math.cos(az) * cosAl;
        return new Vector3f(x, y, z);
    }

    /** 绘制始终面向观察者的四边形 */
    private static void drawCelestialQuadAt(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                            float distance, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        Vector3f center = skyPosition(distance, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);

        Vector3f dir = new Vector3f(center).normalize();
        Vector3f worldUp = new Vector3f(0f, 1f, 0f);
        if (Math.abs(dir.y) > 0.99f) worldUp.set(0f, 0f, 1f);

        Vector3f right = new Vector3f(dir).cross(worldUp).normalize().mul(size);
        Vector3f up    = new Vector3f(dir).cross(right).normalize().mul(size);

        Vector3f tl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).add(up);
        Vector3f bl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f br = new Vector3f(center).add(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f tr = new Vector3f(center).add(right).add(up);

        buffer.addVertex(tl.x, tl.y, tl.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bl.x, bl.y, bl.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(br.x, br.y, br.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(tr.x, tr.y, tr.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.server.IoQuakeManager;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

/**
 * C2S 调试包：客户端请求服务端切换地震状态。
 * mode: 0 = 强制地震，1 = 强制平静
 */
public record IoQuakeDebugPayload(int mode) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final int MODE_QUAKE = 0;
    public static final int MODE_CALM = 1;

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<IoQuakeDebugPayload> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "io_quake_debug"));

    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, IoQuakeDebugPayload> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, IoQuakeDebugPayload::mode,
                    IoQuakeDebugPayload::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(IoQuakeDebugPayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            if (payload.mode() == MODE_QUAKE) {
                IoQuakeManager.debugForceQuake();
            } else {
                IoQuakeManager.debugForceCalm();
            }
        });
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.client.IoQuakeClientCache;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public record IoQuakePayload(
        boolean isQuaking,
        int quakeTicks,
        int quakeTotalTicks,
        int cooldownTimer
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<IoQuakePayload> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "io_quake"));

    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, IoQuakePayload> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.BOOL,     IoQuakePayload::isQuaking,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT,  IoQuakePayload::quakeTicks,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT,  IoQuakePayload::quakeTotalTicks,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT,  IoQuakePayload::cooldownTimer,
                    IoQuakePayload::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(IoQuakePayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() ->
                IoQuakeClientCache.set(
                        payload.isQuaking(),
                        payload.quakeTicks(),
                        payload.quakeTotalTicks(),
                        payload.cooldownTimer()
                )
        );
    }

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.io.network.IoQuakePayload;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.server.ServerLifecycleHooks;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class IoQuakeManager {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // ===== 地震间隔（刻） =====
    // 火卫二/木卫一经常地震，所以间隔比火山短
    private static final int INTERVAL_MIN = 100;    // 5 秒
    private static final int INTERVAL_MAX = 600;    // 30 秒

    // ===== 持续时间（刻） =====
    private static final int DURATION_MIN = 40;     // 2 秒
    private static final int DURATION_MAX = 120;    // 6 秒

    private static final int SYNC_INTERVAL = 5;

    private static int cooldownTimer = 0;
    private static int quakeTicks = 0;
    private static int quakeTotalTicks = 0;
    private static int syncCounter = 0;

    // ===== Getter =====
    public static boolean isQuaking()          { return quakeTicks > 0; }
    public static int getQuakeTicks()          { return quakeTicks; }
    public static int getQuakeTotalTicks()     { return quakeTotalTicks; }
    public static int getCooldownTimer()       { return cooldownTimer; }

    public static float getQuakeIntensity() {
        if (!isQuaking() || quakeTotalTicks <= 0) return 0f;
        float progress = 1f - (float) quakeTicks / quakeTotalTicks;
        // 抛物线包络：中间最强
        float envelope = 4f * progress * (1f - progress);
        return 0.25f + 0.75f * envelope;
    }

    // ===== 调试 =====
    public static void debugForceQuake() {
        ServerLevel ioLevel = findIoLevel();
        if (ioLevel == null) return;

        quakeTicks = DURATION_MIN + RNG.nextInt(DURATION_MAX - DURATION_MIN + 1);
        quakeTotalTicks = quakeTicks;
        cooldownTimer = 0;

        broadcastState(ioLevel);
    }

    public static void debugForceCalm() {
        ServerLevel ioLevel = findIoLevel();
        if (ioLevel == null) return;

        quakeTicks = 0;
        quakeTotalTicks = 0;
        cooldownTimer = INTERVAL_MIN + RNG.nextInt(INTERVAL_MAX - INTERVAL_MIN + 1);

        broadcastState(ioLevel);
    }

    private static ServerLevel findIoLevel() {
        var server = ServerLifecycleHooks.getCurrentServer();
        if (server == null) return null;
        for (ServerLevel level : server.getAllLevels()) {
            if (level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.IO_LEVEL_KEY)) {
                return level;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onServerTick(ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
        ServerLevel ioLevel = findIoLevel();
        if (ioLevel == null) {
            quakeTicks = 0;
            quakeTotalTicks = 0;
            cooldownTimer = 0;
            return;
        }

        if (quakeTicks > 0) {
            quakeTicks--;
            if (quakeTicks <= 0) {
                // 地震结束，进入冷却
                cooldownTimer = INTERVAL_MIN + RNG.nextInt(INTERVAL_MAX - INTERVAL_MIN + 1);
            }
        } else if (cooldownTimer > 0) {
            cooldownTimer--;
        } else {
            startQuake(ioLevel);
        }

        syncCounter++;
        if (syncCounter >= SYNC_INTERVAL) {
            syncCounter = 0;
            broadcastState(ioLevel);
        }
    }

    private static void startQuake(ServerLevel level) {
        if (level.players().isEmpty()) {
            cooldownTimer = 100; // 没人时5秒后再检查
            return;
        }

        quakeTicks = DURATION_MIN + RNG.nextInt(DURATION_MAX - DURATION_MIN + 1);
        quakeTotalTicks = quakeTicks;

        broadcastState(level);
    }

    private static void broadcastState(ServerLevel ioLevel) {
        boolean quaking = quakeTicks > 0;
        IoQuakePayload payload = new IoQuakePayload(
                quaking,
                quakeTicks,
                quakeTotalTicks,
                cooldownTimer
        );
        for (ServerPlayer player : ioLevel.players()) {
            PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, payload);
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\moon\client\MoonGravityHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.moon.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MoonGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "moon_low_gravity");

    /**
     * 月球重力系数（地球 = 1.0）
     * 真实物理：0.165（地球的 1/6）
     * 游戏化：0.3（推荐，和谷神星一致，手感舒适）
     */
    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 0.3;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInMoon = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MOON_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInMoon && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInMoon && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.moon.client;

import net.minecraft.client.renderer.DimensionSpecialEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class MoonSkyEffects extends DimensionSpecialEffects {

    public MoonSkyEffects() {
        super(0.0f, false, SkyType.NONE, false, false);
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull Vec3 getBrightnessDependentFogColor(@NotNull Vec3 fogColor, float brightness) {
        float r = 0.00f + 0.06f * brightness;
        float g = 0.00f + 0.06f * brightness;
        float b = 0.00f + 0.07f * brightness;
        return new Vec3(r, g, b);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isFoggyAt(int x, int z) {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public float[] getSunriseColor(float timeOfDay, float partialTick) {
        return null;
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\moon\client\MoonSkyRenderer.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.moon.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MoonSkyRenderer {

    private static final float ROTATION_SPEED = 1.0f;
    private static final int STAR_COUNT = 3500;
    private static final float STAR_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 贴图 ====================
    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EARTH_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/earth_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation VENUS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/venus_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation MARS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/mars_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation JUPITER_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/jupiter_icon.png");

    // ==================== 天球距离 ====================
    // 全部设为相同距离 100，避免深度问题
    private static final float SKY_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 现实视角直径（度）====================
    // 按真实比例：地球 1.9° 是太阳 0.53° 的约 3.6 倍
    // 但真实太小，游戏里看不见，统一乘以一个"可视化放大系数"
    private static final float ANGULAR_SCALE = 8.0f;   // 放大 8 倍

    private static final float SUN_ANGULAR_REAL     = 1.7f;
    private static final float EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL   = 2.5f;
    private static final float VENUS_ANGULAR_REAL   = 0.05f;
    private static final float MARS_ANGULAR_REAL    = 0.02f;
    private static final float JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL = 0.015f;

    // 放大后的视角直径
    private static final float SUN_ANGULAR     = SUN_ANGULAR_REAL     * ANGULAR_SCALE;
    private static final float EARTH_ANGULAR   = EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL   * ANGULAR_SCALE;
    private static final float VENUS_ANGULAR   = VENUS_ANGULAR_REAL   * ANGULAR_SCALE;
    private static final float MARS_ANGULAR    = MARS_ANGULAR_REAL    * ANGULAR_SCALE;
    private static final float JUPITER_ANGULAR = JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE;

    private static final float SUN_SIZE     = sizeFromAngularDiameter(SUN_ANGULAR,     SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float EARTH_SIZE   = sizeFromAngularDiameter(EARTH_ANGULAR,   SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float VENUS_SIZE   = sizeFromAngularDiameter(VENUS_ANGULAR,   SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float MARS_SIZE    = sizeFromAngularDiameter(MARS_ANGULAR,    SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float JUPITER_SIZE = sizeFromAngularDiameter(JUPITER_ANGULAR, SKY_DISTANCE);

    /**
     * 视角直径 → sprite 半宽
     */
    private static float sizeFromAngularDiameter(float angularDiameterDeg, float distance) {
        float radiusRad = (float) Math.toRadians(angularDiameterDeg * 0.5f);
        return distance * (float) Math.tan(radiusRad);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MOON_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);
        float timeOfDay = mc.level.getTimeOfDay(partialTick);
        float skyRotRad = (float) Math.toRadians(timeOfDay * 360.0f * ROTATION_SPEED);

        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));

        // ========== 第一层：旋转天球（恒星 + 太阳 + 行星）==========
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.rotateX(-skyRotRad);
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // 1. 恒星背景
        renderStars();

        // 2. 太阳
        renderCelestialBody(SUN_TEXTURE, SUN_SIZE,
                180.0f, 45.0f,
                1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        // 3. 金星：永远在太阳 47° 以内，这里取 40°
        renderCelestialBody(VENUS_TEXTURE, VENUS_SIZE,
                140.0f, 48.0f,
                0.95f, 0.95f, 0.85f);

        // 4. 火星：外侧行星，可以出现在任意方向
        renderCelestialBody(MARS_TEXTURE, MARS_SIZE,
                60.0f, 30.0f,
                1.0f, 0.85f, 0.75f);

        // 5. 木星：外侧行星
        renderCelestialBody(JUPITER_TEXTURE, JUPITER_SIZE,
                320.0f, 25.0f,
                1.0f, 0.95f, 0.8f);

        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // ========== 第二层：潮汐锁定地球 ==========
        // 地球位置随月相变化：地球与太阳的角距 = 月相角
        // 新月（timeOfDay=0）：地球和太阳同方向
        // 满月（timeOfDay=0.5）：地球在太阳对面
        // 月球自转周期 = 公转周期，所以地球在月球天上位置几乎固定
        // 我们用一个"地球固定方位角"，让它在观察者坐标系里不随天球转
        // 但为了月相正确，方位角要随 timeOfDay 缓慢改变（周期 8 天里变化一圈）

        // ★ 现实中月球自转周期 27.3 天，地球在月球天空几乎不动
        // 游戏中 timeOfDay 周期是 1 天，为了让玩家能看到月相变化，用更慢的周期
        float earthAzimuth = 90.0f;    // 固定方位角（东方）
        float earthAltitude = 13.0f;   // 固定仰角

        renderCelestialBody(EARTH_TEXTURE, EARTH_SIZE,
                earthAzimuth, earthAltitude,
                1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        // ===== 恢复 =====
        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    /** 渲染恒星背景 */
    private static void renderStars() {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator starTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder starBuffer = starTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);

        RandomSource random = RandomSource.create(20842L);
        for (int i = 0; i < STAR_COUNT; i++) {
            float cosTheta = random.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            float sinTheta = Mth.sqrt(1.0f - cosTheta * cosTheta);
            float phi = random.nextFloat() * Mth.TWO_PI;

            float x = sinTheta * Mth.cos(phi);
            float y = cosTheta;
            float z = sinTheta * Mth.sin(phi);

            Vector3f starPos = new Vector3f(x, y, z).mul(STAR_DISTANCE);
            Quaternionf starRot = new Quaternionf().rotateTo(new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F), starPos);
            float size = 0.06f + random.nextFloat() * 0.05f;
            Vector3f starRight = new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);
            Vector3f starUp    = new Vector3f(0.0F, 1.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);

            float brightness = 0.18f + random.nextFloat() * 0.45f;

            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
        }

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(starBuffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 渲染单个天体 */
    private static void renderCelestialBody(ResourceLocation texture, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg,
                                            float r, float g, float b) {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, texture);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(r, g, b, 1.0f);

        Tesselator tess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder buffer = tess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuadAt(buffer, SKY_DISTANCE, size, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(buffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 根据方位角 + 仰角计算三维位置 */
    private static Vector3f skyPosition(float distance, float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        float az = (float) Math.toRadians(azimuthDeg);
        float al = (float) Math.toRadians(altitudeDeg);
        float cosAl = (float) Math.cos(al);
        float x = distance * (float) Math.sin(az) * cosAl;
        float y = distance * (float) Math.sin(al);
        float z = distance * (float) Math.cos(az) * cosAl;
        return new Vector3f(x, y, z);
    }

    /** 绘制始终面向观察者的四边形 */
    private static void drawCelestialQuadAt(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                            float distance, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        Vector3f center = skyPosition(distance, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);

        Vector3f dir = new Vector3f(center).normalize();
        Vector3f worldUp = new Vector3f(0f, 1f, 0f);
        if (Math.abs(dir.y) > 0.99f) worldUp.set(0f, 0f, 1f);

        Vector3f right = new Vector3f(dir).cross(worldUp).normalize().mul(size);
        Vector3f up    = new Vector3f(dir).cross(right).normalize().mul(size);

        Vector3f tl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).add(up);
        Vector3f bl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f br = new Vector3f(center).add(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f tr = new Vector3f(center).add(right).add(up);

        buffer.addVertex(tl.x, tl.y, tl.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bl.x, bl.y, bl.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(br.x, br.y, br.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(tr.x, tr.y, tr.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.phobos.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class PhobosGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "phobos_low_gravity");

    // 重力系数
    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 0.06;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInPhobos = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.PHOBOS_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInPhobos && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInPhobos && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.phobos.client;

import net.minecraft.client.renderer.DimensionSpecialEffects;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class PhobosSkyEffects extends DimensionSpecialEffects {

    public PhobosSkyEffects() {
        super(0.0f, false, SkyType.NONE, false, false);
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull Vec3 getBrightnessDependentFogColor(@NotNull Vec3 fogColor, float brightness) {
        float t = Mth.clamp(brightness, 0f, 1f);
        t = t * t * (3f - 2f * t);

        float r = 0.07f * t;
        float g = 0.05f * t;
        float b = 0.04f * t;
        return new Vec3(r, g, b);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isFoggyAt(int x, int z) {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public float[] getSunriseColor(float timeOfDay, float partialTick) {
        return null;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Moons\phobos\client\PhobosSkyRenderer.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Moons.phobos.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class PhobosSkyRenderer {

    private static final int STAR_COUNT = 3500;
    private static final float STAR_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 贴图 ====================
    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EARTH_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/earth_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation VENUS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/venus_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation MARS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/mars_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation JUPITER_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/jupiter_icon.png");

    // ==================== 天球距离 ====================
    private static final float SKY_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // ==================== 视角直径 ====================
    private static final float ANGULAR_SCALE = 8.0f;

    private static final float SUN_ANGULAR_REAL     = 1.4f;
    private static final float EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL   = 0.10f;
    private static final float VENUS_ANGULAR_REAL   = 0.10f;
    private static final float JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL = 0.12f;

    // 火星：潮汐锁定，巨大（真实约 40°）
    private static final float MARS_ANGULAR = 40.0f;

    private static final float SUN_SIZE     = sizeFromAngularDiameter(SUN_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float EARTH_SIZE   = sizeFromAngularDiameter(EARTH_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float VENUS_SIZE   = sizeFromAngularDiameter(VENUS_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float JUPITER_SIZE = sizeFromAngularDiameter(JUPITER_ANGULAR_REAL * ANGULAR_SCALE, SKY_DISTANCE);
    private static final float MARS_SIZE    = sizeFromAngularDiameter(MARS_ANGULAR, SKY_DISTANCE);

    // ==================== 太阳初始位置（正午位置）====================
    // 天球会随时间旋转，这是"正午时太阳应该在哪"的初始位置
    // 火卫一（Phobos）几乎在火星赤道面上，所以太阳中午几乎在天顶
    private static final float SUN_INIT_AZIMUTH  = 180.0f;   // 正南
    private static final float SUN_INIT_ALTITUDE = 65.0f;    // 高挂天空（正午最高）

    private static float sizeFromAngularDiameter(float angularDiameterDeg, float distance) {
        float radiusRad = (float) Math.toRadians(angularDiameterDeg * 0.5f);
        return distance * (float) Math.tan(radiusRad);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.PHOBOS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);

        // 基于 dayTime：0 = 6:00 日出，6000 = 正午，12000 = 日落，18000 = 午夜
        long dayTime = mc.level.getDayTime();
        float dayFrac = ((dayTime % 24000L) + partialTick) / 24000.0f;

        // 天球旋转角度：
        // dayFrac = 0.0（日出）→ 初始位置（太阳在正午位置？不对，需要偏移）
        // dayFrac = 0.25（正午）→ 太阳在初始位置（正南高空）
        // 所以要把 dayFrac 平移 -0.25，让 dayFrac=0.25 时角度 = 0
        float skyRotFrac = dayFrac - 0.25f;
        float skyRotRad = skyRotFrac * 2.0f * (float) Math.PI;

        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));

        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // ========== 第一层：随天球旋转（恒星 + 太阳 + 其它行星）==========
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.rotateX(skyRotRad);
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // 恒星背景
        renderStars();

        // 太阳：初始位置 = 正午位置（南天高空）
        renderCelestialBody(SUN_TEXTURE, SUN_SIZE,
                SUN_INIT_AZIMUTH, SUN_INIT_ALTITUDE,
                1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        // 金星：内侧行星，离太阳较近
        renderCelestialBody(VENUS_TEXTURE, VENUS_SIZE,
                140.0f, 48.0f,
                0.95f, 0.95f, 0.85f);

        // 地球：从火星看是内侧行星，偏蓝
        renderCelestialBody(EARTH_TEXTURE, EARTH_SIZE,
                60.0f, 30.0f,
                0.55f, 0.7f, 1.0f);

        // 木星：外侧行星，明亮
        renderCelestialBody(JUPITER_TEXTURE, JUPITER_SIZE,
                320.0f, 25.0f,
                1.0f, 0.95f, 0.8f);

        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // ========== 第二层：潮汐锁定（火星不随天球旋转）==========
        // 火卫一被火星潮汐锁定，火星在天空几乎不动
        renderCelestialBody(MARS_TEXTURE, MARS_SIZE,
                90.0f, 35.0f,
                1.0f, 0.8f, 0.7f);

        // ===== 恢复 =====
        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    /** 渲染恒星背景 */
    private static void renderStars() {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator starTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder starBuffer = starTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);

        RandomSource random = RandomSource.create(20843L);
        for (int i = 0; i < STAR_COUNT; i++) {
            float cosTheta = random.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            float sinTheta = Mth.sqrt(1.0f - cosTheta * cosTheta);
            float phi = random.nextFloat() * Mth.TWO_PI;

            float x = sinTheta * Mth.cos(phi);
            float y = cosTheta;
            float z = sinTheta * Mth.sin(phi);

            Vector3f starPos = new Vector3f(x, y, z).mul(STAR_DISTANCE);
            Quaternionf starRot = new Quaternionf().rotateTo(new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F), starPos);
            float size = 0.06f + random.nextFloat() * 0.05f;
            Vector3f starRight = new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);
            Vector3f starUp    = new Vector3f(0.0F, 1.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);

            float brightness = 0.18f + random.nextFloat() * 0.45f;

            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
        }

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(starBuffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 渲染单个天体 */
    private static void renderCelestialBody(ResourceLocation texture, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg,
                                            float r, float g, float b) {
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, texture);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(r, g, b, 1.0f);

        Tesselator tess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder buffer = tess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuadAt(buffer, SKY_DISTANCE, size, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(buffer.buildOrThrow());
    }

    /** 根据方位角 + 仰角计算三维位置 */
    private static Vector3f skyPosition(float distance, float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        float az = (float) Math.toRadians(azimuthDeg);
        float al = (float) Math.toRadians(altitudeDeg);
        float cosAl = (float) Math.cos(al);
        float x = distance * (float) Math.sin(az) * cosAl;
        float y = distance * (float) Math.sin(al);
        float z = distance * (float) Math.cos(az) * cosAl;
        return new Vector3f(x, y, z);
    }

    /** 绘制始终面向观察者的四边形 */
    private static void drawCelestialQuadAt(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                            float distance, float size,
                                            float azimuthDeg, float altitudeDeg) {
        Vector3f center = skyPosition(distance, azimuthDeg, altitudeDeg);

        Vector3f dir = new Vector3f(center).normalize();
        Vector3f worldUp = new Vector3f(0f, 1f, 0f);
        if (Math.abs(dir.y) > 0.99f) worldUp.set(0f, 0f, 1f);

        Vector3f right = new Vector3f(dir).cross(worldUp).normalize().mul(size);
        Vector3f up    = new Vector3f(dir).cross(right).normalize().mul(size);

        Vector3f tl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).add(up);
        Vector3f bl = new Vector3f(center).sub(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f br = new Vector3f(center).add(right).sub(up);
        Vector3f tr = new Vector3f(center).add(right).add(up);

        buffer.addVertex(tl.x, tl.y, tl.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bl.x, bl.y, bl.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(br.x, br.y, br.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(tr.x, tr.y, tr.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.portal.DimensionTransition;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.event.RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.registration.PayloadRegistrar;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class TeleportHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void register(RegisterPayloadHandlersEvent event) {
        PayloadRegistrar registrar = event.registrar("1");

        registrar.playToServer(
                TeleportRequestPacket.TYPE,
                TeleportRequestPacket.STREAM_CODEC,
                TeleportHandler::handle
        );
    }

    private static void handle(TeleportRequestPacket packet, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            if (!(context.player() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;

            ServerLevel targetLevel = player.server.getLevel(packet.dimension());
            if (targetLevel == null) return;

            if (player.level().dimension().equals(packet.dimension())) return;

            player.changeDimension(new DimensionTransition(
                    targetLevel,
                    net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3.atBottomCenterOf(packet.pos()),
                    net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3.ZERO,
                    player.getYRot(),
                    player.getXRot(),
                    DimensionTransition.DO_NOTHING
            ));
        });
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\network\TeleportRequestPacket.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public record TeleportRequestPacket(ResourceKey<Level> dimension, BlockPos pos)
        implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final Type<TeleportRequestPacket> TYPE =
            new Type<>(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(
                    NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "teleport_request"));

    public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, TeleportRequestPacket> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ResourceKey.streamCodec(Registries.DIMENSION), TeleportRequestPacket::dimension,
                    BlockPos.STREAM_CODEC, TeleportRequestPacket::pos,
                    TeleportRequestPacket::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ceres.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class CeresGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "ceres_low_gravity");

    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 0.3;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInCeres = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.CERES_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInCeres && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInCeres && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

        return false;

        return null;

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ceres.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class CeresSkyRenderer {

    private static final float ROTATION_SPEED = 1.0f;

    // 星星
    private static final int STAR_COUNT = 5000;
    private static final float STAR_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // 贴图
    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EARTH_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/earth_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation MARS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/mars_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation JUPITER_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/jupiter_icon.png");

    // 太阳
    private static final float SUN_SIZE = 4.5f;
    private static final float SUN_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // 地球
    private static final float EARTH_SIZE = 1.2f;
    private static final float EARTH_DISTANCE = 99.0f;
    private static final float EARTH_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(20.0f);
    private static final float EARTH_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(5.0f);

    // 火星
    private static final float MARS_SIZE = 1.5f;
    private static final float MARS_DISTANCE = 99.0f;
    private static final float MARS_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(-30.0f);
    private static final float MARS_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(-8.0f);

    // 木星
    private static final float JUPITER_SIZE = 10.0f;
    private static final float JUPITER_DISTANCE = 98.0f;
    private static final float JUPITER_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(150.0f);
    private static final float JUPITER_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(20.0f);

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.CERES_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);
        float timeOfDay = mc.level.getTimeOfDay(partialTick);

        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));
        mvStack.rotateX((float) Math.toRadians(timeOfDay * 360.0f * ROTATION_SPEED));
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // ==================== 星星 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);   // 星星用默认颜色

        Tesselator starTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder starBuffer = starTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);

        RandomSource random = RandomSource.create(20484L);

        for (int i = 0; i < STAR_COUNT; i++) {
            float cosTheta = random.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            float sinTheta = Mth.sqrt(1.0f - cosTheta * cosTheta);
            float phi = random.nextFloat() * Mth.TWO_PI;

            float x = sinTheta * Mth.cos(phi);
            float y = cosTheta;
            float z = sinTheta * Mth.sin(phi);

            Vector3f starPos = new Vector3f(x, y, z).mul(STAR_DISTANCE);
            Quaternionf starRot = new Quaternionf().rotateTo(new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F), starPos);
            float size = 0.05f + random.nextFloat() * 0.05f;
            Vector3f starRight = new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);
            Vector3f starUp    = new Vector3f(0.0F, 1.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);

            float brightness = 0.15f + random.nextFloat() * 0.35f;

            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
        }

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(starBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 太阳 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, SUN_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator sunTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder sunBuffer = sunTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(sunBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 地球 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, EARTH_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.85f, 0.85f, 0.85f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator earthTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder earthBuffer = earthTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(earthBuffer, EARTH_DISTANCE, EARTH_SIZE, EARTH_OFFSET_X, EARTH_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(earthBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 火星 =====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, MARS_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.9f, 0.85f, 0.8f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator marsTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder marsBuffer = marsTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(marsBuffer, MARS_DISTANCE, MARS_SIZE, MARS_OFFSET_X, MARS_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(marsBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 木星 ======================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, JUPITER_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.9f, 0.85f, 0.75f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator jupiterTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder jupiterBuffer = jupiterTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(jupiterBuffer, JUPITER_DISTANCE, JUPITER_SIZE, JUPITER_OFFSET_X, JUPITER_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(jupiterBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 恢复 =======================
        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);   // ★ 关键：恢复默认颜色
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    private static void drawCelestialQuad(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                          float distance,
                                          float size,
                                          float offsetX,
                                          float offsetY) {
        Vector3f topLeft     = rotateOffset(-size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomLeft  = rotateOffset(-size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomRight = rotateOffset( size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f topRight    = rotateOffset( size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);

        buffer.addVertex(topLeft.x,     topLeft.y,     topLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomLeft.x,  bottomLeft.y,  bottomLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomRight.x, bottomRight.y, bottomRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(topRight.x,    topRight.y,    topRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }

    private static Vector3f rotateOffset(float x, float y, float z,
                                         float offsetX, float offsetY) {
        Vector3f v = new Vector3f(x, y, z);
        if (offsetY != 0) v.rotateZ(offsetY);
        if (offsetX != 0) v.rotateX(offsetX);
        return v;
    }
}
