📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\client\JupiterCameraShake.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ViewportEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class JupiterCameraShake {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.02f;
    private static final float SHAKE_THRESHOLD = 1.3f;
    private static final float SHAKE_AMPLITUDE = 2.5f;
    private static final float SHAKE_FREQUENCY = 0.15f;

    private static float smoothedStrength = 0.0f;
    private static float shakeTime = 0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onComputeCameraAngles(ViewportEvent.ComputeCameraAngles event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        // 改：从客户端缓存读
        float strength = JupiterStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0.0f;
        strength = Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(2.5f, strength));

        smoothedStrength += (strength - smoothedStrength) * SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(smoothedStrength)) smoothedStrength = 0.0f;

        if (smoothedStrength <= SHAKE_THRESHOLD) return;

        float intensity = (smoothedStrength - SHAKE_THRESHOLD) / (2.5f - SHAKE_THRESHOLD);
        intensity = Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(1.0f, intensity));

        shakeTime += 1.0f;

        float waveX = (float) Math.sin(shakeTime * SHAKE_FREQUENCY) * intensity * SHAKE_AMPLITUDE;
        float waveY = (float) Math.cos(shakeTime * SHAKE_FREQUENCY * 1.3f) * intensity * SHAKE_AMPLITUDE * 0.6f;

        float jitterX = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * intensity * 0.5f;
        float jitterY = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * intensity * 0.5f;

        event.setYaw(event.getYaw() + waveX + jitterX);
        event.setPitch(event.getPitch() + waveY + jitterY);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\client\JupiterDustParticles.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.BlockParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class JupiterDustParticles {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // ===== 尘埃参数 =====
    private static final int DUST_RADIUS     = 32;
    private static final int DUST_COUNT      = 40;
    private static final int DUST_HEIGHT_MIN = -8;
    private static final int DUST_HEIGHT_MAX = 24;

    // ===== 漂移速度（加快后）=====
    private static final double DRIFT_SPEED = 0.08;

    // ===== 主风向（和风粒子保持一致）=====
    private static final float WIND_ANGLE = (float) Math.toRadians(35.0f);
    private static final double WIND_BIAS = 0.6;   // 0 = 纯随机，1 = 完全顺风

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (!player.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (!(player.level() instanceof ClientLevel level)) return;

        spawnDust(level, player);
    }

    private static void spawnDust(ClientLevel level, Player player) {
        double dirX = Math.cos(WIND_ANGLE);
        double dirZ = Math.sin(WIND_ANGLE);

        for (int i = 0; i < DUST_COUNT; i++) {
            double dx = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * DUST_RADIUS * 2;
            double dz = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * DUST_RADIUS * 2;
            double dy = DUST_HEIGHT_MIN
                    + RNG.nextDouble() * (DUST_HEIGHT_MAX - DUST_HEIGHT_MIN);

            double px = player.getX() + dx;
            double py = player.getY() + dy;
            double pz = player.getZ() + dz;

            // 速度 = 主风向 * 偏置 + 随机
            double speed = DRIFT_SPEED * (0.6 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.8);
            double randX = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * DRIFT_SPEED;
            double randZ = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * DRIFT_SPEED;
            double randY = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * DRIFT_SPEED * 0.4;

            double vx = dirX * speed * WIND_BIAS + randX * (1 - WIND_BIAS);
            double vz = dirZ * speed * WIND_BIAS + randZ * (1 - WIND_BIAS);
            double vy = randY;

            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            if (roll < 0.55f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.ORANGE_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.BLACK_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            }
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\client\JupiterEnvironmentHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.server.JupiterStormManager;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EquipmentSlot;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorMaterial;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorMaterials;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class JupiterEnvironmentHandler {

    // ===== 无护甲时：伤害间隔范围 =====
    // 平静期：120 刻一次（6 秒，伤害概率低）
    // 暴烈期：8 刻一次（0.4 秒，非常密）
    private static final int DAMAGE_INTERVAL_CALM = 120;
    private static final int DAMAGE_INTERVAL_STORM = 8;

    // ===== 无护甲时：伤害值范围 =====
    // 平静期：0.3 点
    // 暴烈期：8 点（原 7.5，略提）
    private static final float DAMAGE_AMOUNT_CALM = 0.3f;
    private static final float DAMAGE_AMOUNT_STORM = 8.0f;

    // ===== 有护甲时：护甲耐久损耗 =====
    private static final int ARMOR_INTERVAL_BASE = 100;
    private static final int ARMOR_AMOUNT_BASE = 6;

    private static final int MESSAGE_INTERVAL = 20;
    private static final float SMOOTH_FACTOR = 0.01f;

    private static float smoothedStrength = 1.0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (player.isCreative() || player.isSpectator()) return;

        // 目标强度（不再强制下限 0.5）
        float targetStrength = JupiterStormManager.getStormStrength();
        targetStrength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(2.5f, targetStrength));

        // 平滑跟随
        smoothedStrength += (targetStrength - smoothedStrength) * SMOOTH_FACTOR;
        if (Float.isNaN(smoothedStrength)) smoothedStrength = 0f;
        smoothedStrength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(2.5f, smoothedStrength));

        // ===== 归一化：0 = 平静，1 = 暴烈顶点 =====
        float t = smoothedStrength / 2.5f;
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        // ===== 曲线：t^2.5 让平静期伤害几乎为零 =====
        float curve = (float) Math.pow(t, 2.5);

        // ===== 伤害间隔：曲线决定，平静期极长 =====
        int damageInterval = (int) (DAMAGE_INTERVAL_CALM
                - (DAMAGE_INTERVAL_CALM - DAMAGE_INTERVAL_STORM) * curve);
        damageInterval = Math.max(DAMAGE_INTERVAL_STORM, damageInterval);

        // ===== 伤害值：曲线决定 =====
        float damageAmount = DAMAGE_AMOUNT_CALM
                + (DAMAGE_AMOUNT_STORM - DAMAGE_AMOUNT_CALM) * curve;

        // ===== 护甲耐久损耗（随强度）=====
        int armorInterval = Math.max(20,
                (int) (ARMOR_INTERVAL_BASE / (0.5f + smoothedStrength)));
        int armorAmount = Math.max(1,
                (int) (ARMOR_AMOUNT_BASE * (0.5f + smoothedStrength)));

        boolean protectedByArmor = hasFullProtectiveArmor(player);

        if (!protectedByArmor) {
            if (player.tickCount % damageInterval == 0) {
                // 平静期伤害极小，暴烈期致命
                if (damageAmount >= 0.05f) {
                    player.hurt(player.damageSources().generic(), damageAmount);
                }
            }
            if (player.tickCount % MESSAGE_INTERVAL == 0) {
                player.displayClientMessage(
                        Component.translatable("message.newgc.jupiter.no_armor"),
                        true
                );
            }
        } else {
            if (player.tickCount % armorInterval == 0) {
                damageArmorPiece(player, EquipmentSlot.HEAD, armorAmount);
                damageArmorPiece(player, EquipmentSlot.CHEST, armorAmount);
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean hasFullProtectiveArmor(ServerPlayer player) {
        EquipmentSlot[] slots = {
                EquipmentSlot.HEAD,
                EquipmentSlot.CHEST,
                EquipmentSlot.LEGS,
                EquipmentSlot.FEET
        };
        for (EquipmentSlot slot : slots) {
            ItemStack stack = player.getItemBySlot(slot);
            if (!isProtectiveArmor(stack)) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private static boolean isProtectiveArmor(ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return false;
        if (!(stack.getItem() instanceof ArmorItem armor)) return false;
        Holder<ArmorMaterial> mat = armor.getMaterial();
        return mat.is(ArmorMaterials.IRON)
                || mat.is(ArmorMaterials.GOLD)
                || mat.is(ArmorMaterials.DIAMOND)
                || mat.is(ArmorMaterials.NETHERITE);
    }

    private static void damageArmorPiece(ServerPlayer player, EquipmentSlot slot, int amount) {
        ItemStack stack = player.getItemBySlot(slot);
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return;
        if (!(stack.getItem() instanceof ArmorItem)) return;
        if (!stack.isDamageableItem()) return;
        stack.hurtAndBreak(amount, player, slot);
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\client\JupiterGravityHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class JupiterGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "jupiter_high_gravity");

    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 1.2;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInJupiter = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInJupiter && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInJupiter && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import net.minecraft.client.renderer.DimensionSpecialEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class JupiterSkyEffects extends DimensionSpecialEffects {

    public JupiterSkyEffects() {
        super(192.0f, false, SkyType.NONE, false, false);
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull Vec3 getBrightnessDependentFogColor(@NotNull Vec3 fogColor, float brightness) {
        float r = 0.30f + 0.50f * brightness;
        float g = 0.22f + 0.38f * brightness;
        float b = 0.14f + 0.22f * brightness;
        return new Vec3(r, g, b);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isFoggyAt(int x, int z) {
        return true;    // ★ 从 false 改成 true
    }

    @Override
    public float[] getSunriseColor(float timeOfDay, float partialTick) {
        return null;
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class JupiterSkyRenderer {

    private static final float ROTATION_SPEED = 1.0f;

    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation IO_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/io_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EUROPA_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/europa_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation GANYMEDE_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/ganymede_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation CALLISTO_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/callisto_icon.png");

    private static final float SUN_SIZE = 1.0f;
    private static final float SUN_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    private static final float IO_SIZE = 1.5f;
    private static final float IO_DISTANCE = 99.0f;
    private static final float IO_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(30.0f);
    private static final float IO_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(8.0f);

    private static final float EUROPA_SIZE = 1.0f;
    private static final float EUROPA_DISTANCE = 99.5f;
    private static final float EUROPA_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(-40.0f);
    private static final float EUROPA_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(-8.0f);

    private static final float GANYMEDE_SIZE = 1.2f;
    private static final float GANYMEDE_DISTANCE = 100.0f;
    private static final float GANYMEDE_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(150.0f);
    private static final float GANYMEDE_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(-6.0f);

    private static final float CALLISTO_SIZE = 0.8f;
    private static final float CALLISTO_DISTANCE = 100.5f;
    private static final float CALLISTO_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(-150.0f);
    private static final float CALLISTO_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(12.0f);

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);
        float timeOfDay = mc.level.getTimeOfDay(partialTick);

        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));
        mvStack.rotateX((float) Math.toRadians(timeOfDay * 360.0f * ROTATION_SPEED));
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // 太阳
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, SUN_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator sunTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder sunBuffer = sunTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(sunBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // Io
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, IO_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 0.85f, 0.5f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator ioTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder ioBuffer = ioTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuad(ioBuffer, IO_DISTANCE, IO_SIZE, IO_OFFSET_X, IO_OFFSET_Y);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(ioBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // Europa
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, EUROPA_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.95f, 0.95f, 0.9f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator europaTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder europaBuffer = europaTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuad(europaBuffer, EUROPA_DISTANCE, EUROPA_SIZE, EUROPA_OFFSET_X, EUROPA_OFFSET_Y);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(europaBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // Ganymede
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, GANYMEDE_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.85f, 0.85f, 0.85f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator ganymedeTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder ganymedeBuffer = ganymedeTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuad(ganymedeBuffer, GANYMEDE_DISTANCE, GANYMEDE_SIZE, GANYMEDE_OFFSET_X, GANYMEDE_OFFSET_Y);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(ganymedeBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // Callisto
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, CALLISTO_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.7f, 0.68f, 0.6f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator callistoTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder callistoBuffer = callistoTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);
        drawCelestialQuad(callistoBuffer, CALLISTO_DISTANCE, CALLISTO_SIZE, CALLISTO_OFFSET_X, CALLISTO_OFFSET_Y);
        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(callistoBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    private static void drawCelestialQuad(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                          float distance,
                                          float size,
                                          float offsetX,
                                          float offsetY) {
        Vector3f topLeft     = rotateOffset(-size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomLeft  = rotateOffset(-size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomRight = rotateOffset( size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f topRight    = rotateOffset( size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);

        buffer.addVertex(topLeft.x,     topLeft.y,     topLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomLeft.x,  bottomLeft.y,  bottomLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomRight.x, bottomRight.y, bottomRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(topRight.x,    topRight.y,    topRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }

    private static Vector3f rotateOffset(float x, float y, float z,
                                         float offsetX, float offsetY) {
        Vector3f v = new Vector3f(x, y, z);
        if (offsetY != 0) v.rotateZ(offsetY);
        if (offsetX != 0) v.rotateX(offsetX);
        return v;
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug.DebugState;

public final class JupiterStormClientCache {

    public enum Phase {
        CALM, RISING, PEAK, SUDDEN, FALLING
    }

    private static int phaseOrdinal = Phase.CALM.ordinal();
    private static int phaseTimer = 0;
    private static float stormStrength = 0f;

    private JupiterStormClientCache() {}

    public static void set(int ordinal, int timer, float strength) {
        // 调试模式下不覆盖网络同步的值（由 DebugState 接管）
        if (DebugState.getJupiterForcedStrength() >= 0) {
            return;
        }
        phaseOrdinal = ordinal;
        phaseTimer = timer;
        stormStrength = strength;
    }

    public static Phase getPhase() {
        // 调试模式优先
        int debugPhase = DebugState.getJupiterPhase();
        if (debugPhase >= 0) {
            Phase[] v = Phase.values();
            return v[Math.max(0, Math.min(v.length - 1, debugPhase))];
        }

        Phase[] v = Phase.values();
        int i = Math.max(0, Math.min(v.length - 1, phaseOrdinal));
        return v[i];
    }

    public static int getPhaseTimer() {
        // 调试模式不显示"剩余"（永远 0）
        if (DebugState.getJupiterForcedStrength() >= 0) return 0;
        return phaseTimer;
    }

    public static float getStormStrength() {
        // 调试模式：平滑逼近目标
        float debug = DebugState.tickJupiterSmooth();
        if (debug >= 0) return debug;
        return stormStrength;
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\client\JupiterStormGustParticles.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.BlockParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

/**
 * 木星风暴"强风"粒子。
 * 只在风暴强度 ≥ THRESHOLD 时激活，强度越高越猛。
 * 与 JupiterWindParticles 不同：
 *  - 横向速度极快，粒子几乎"横着刮过去"
 *  - 粒子数量指数级增长
 *  - 有"阵风"脉冲
 *  - 只在高强度时激活，避免平静期浪费性能
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class JupiterStormGustParticles {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // 激活阈值：低于这个强度不生成任何粒子
    private static final float THRESHOLD = 1.2f;

    // 生成范围
    private static final int RADIUS = 26;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MIN = -2;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MAX = 20;

    // 粒子数：从阈值开始到 2.5 之间，线性放大
    private static final int BASE_COUNT = 30;
    private static final int MAX_COUNT = 260;
    private static final int MIN_COUNT = 10;

    // 横向速度
    private static final double BASE_SPEED = 2.2;      // 非常快
    private static final double SPEED_BOOST = 1.6;     // 阵风额外加速

    // 风向
    private static final float WIND_ANGLE = (float) Math.toRadians(35.0f);
    private static final double JITTER = 0.22;         // 方向抖动

    // 垂直漂移（基本水平，只有一点点上下）
    private static final double VERTICAL_DRIFT = 0.03;

    // 生成间隔（刻）
    private static final int TICK_INTERVAL = 1;        // 每刻生成，非常密

    // 阵风脉冲状态
    private static long globalTick = 0;
    private static double gustPhase = 0.0;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (!player.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (!(player.level() instanceof ClientLevel level)) return;

        globalTick++;

        if (level.getGameTime() % TICK_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        spawnGust(level, player);
    }

    private static void spawnGust(ClientLevel level, Player player) {
        float strength = JupiterStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0f;
        strength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(2.5f, strength));

        // ===== 阈值判断：低于阈值不生成 =====
        if (strength < THRESHOLD) return;

        // ===== 归一化：阈值 → 0，2.5 → 1 =====
        float t = (strength - THRESHOLD) / (2.5f - THRESHOLD);
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        // ===== 阵风脉冲：t 越高频率越快 =====
        double pulseFreq = 0.25 + t * 1.5;
        gustPhase += pulseFreq;
        double gustEnvelope = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(gustPhase);
        double gustEnvelope2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(gustPhase * 2.7 + 1.3);
        double gust = gustEnvelope * 0.7 + gustEnvelope2 * 0.3;
        // t 越低，脉冲越弱（保持最小风感）
        gust = 0.5 + 0.5 * t + (1 - t) * 0.0 + gust * t * 0.8;

        // ===== 粒子数 =====
        int baseCount = (int) (BASE_COUNT + (MAX_COUNT - BASE_COUNT) * t);
        // 阵风时额外加
        int gustBoost = (int) (t * t * 100 * gustEnvelope);
        int count = baseCount + gustBoost;
        count = Math.min(count, MAX_COUNT);
        count = Math.max(count, MIN_COUNT);

        // ===== 风速 =====
        double speed = BASE_SPEED * (0.6 + t * 0.8) * (1 + gust * SPEED_BOOST * 0.3);

        double dirX = Math.cos(WIND_ANGLE);
        double dirZ = Math.sin(WIND_ANGLE);

        // 暴烈时风向缓慢摆动（形成"沙尘墙翻滚"）
        if (t > 0.3f) {
            double wobble = Math.sin(globalTick * 0.07) * (t - 0.3f) * 0.5;
            double cos = Math.cos(wobble);
            double sin = Math.sin(wobble);
            double nx = dirX * cos - dirZ * sin;
            double nz = dirX * sin + dirZ * cos;
            dirX = nx;
            dirZ = nz;
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            double dx = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;
            double dz = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;
            double dy = HEIGHT_MIN + RNG.nextDouble() * (HEIGHT_MAX - HEIGHT_MIN);

            double px = player.getX() + dx;
            double py = player.getY() + dy;
            double pz = player.getZ() + dz;

            if (!level.isEmptyBlock(BlockPos.containing(px, py, pz))) continue;

            // 横向速度
            double vx = dirX * speed * (0.7 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6)
                    + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * JITTER * speed;
            double vz = dirZ * speed * (0.7 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6)
                    + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * JITTER * speed;

            // 垂直速度：高 t 时粒子被"卷起"
            double vy;
            if (t > 0.5f && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.3f) {
                vy = (t - 0.5f) * 0.6 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.3;
            } else {
                vy = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * VERTICAL_DRIFT;
            }

            // 颜色：暴烈时更多深色
            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            boolean useDark = t > 0.5f ? roll < 0.7f : roll < 0.4f;

            if (!useDark) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.ORANGE_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.BLACK_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            }

            // 偶尔加 ASH 增加颗粒感
            if (t > 0.4f && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.12f * t) {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.ASH,
                        px, py, pz,
                        vx * 0.7, vy + 0.05, vz * 0.7);
            }
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\client\JupiterStormOverlay.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class JupiterStormOverlay {

    private static final float CLIENT_SMOOTH = 0.01f;
    private static final int MAX_ALPHA = 120;

    private static float clientSmoothed = 1.0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        // 改：从客户端缓存读
        float strength = JupiterStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 1.0f;

        float target = Math.max(1.0f, Math.min(2.5f, strength));

        clientSmoothed += (target - clientSmoothed) * CLIENT_SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(clientSmoothed)) clientSmoothed = 1.0f;

        if (clientSmoothed < 1.0f) return;

        float t = (clientSmoothed - 1.0f) / 1.5f;
        t = Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(1.0f, t));
        int alpha = (int) (t * MAX_ALPHA);
        alpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(MAX_ALPHA, alpha));

        if (alpha <= 0) return;

        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int width = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int height = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        int color = (alpha << 24) | 0xCC6600;
        g.fill(0, 0, width, height, color);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.server.JupiterStormManager;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LightningBolt;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.LevelTickEvent;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class JupiterThunderHandler {

    private static final int LIGHTNING_RADIUS = 16;

    // ===== 冷却范围（tick）=====
    private static final int COOLDOWN_MIN_CALM = 120;   // 6s
    private static final int COOLDOWN_MAX_CALM = 240;   // 12s
    private static final int COOLDOWN_MIN_STORM = 2;    // 0.1s
    private static final int COOLDOWN_MAX_STORM = 8;    // 0.4s

    // ===== 归一化参考值 =====
    private static final float NORM_PEAK = JupiterStormManager.PEAK_REFERENCE;
    private static final float NORM_SUDDEN = JupiterStormManager.SUDDEN_REFERENCE;

    /** 线性曲线（curve = t），让中等强度也能有较密的闪电 */
    private static final float CURVE_EXPONENT = 1.0f;

    /** 风暴时冷却最大加速倍数：curve=1 时每 tick 消耗 5 点冷却 */
    private static final int MAX_COOLDOWN_ACCEL = 4;

    private static final int DEBUG_LOG_INTERVAL = 20;

    private static final Map<ResourceKey<Level>, Integer> COOLDOWNS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final Map<ResourceKey<Level>, Float> LAST_STRENGTH = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLevelTick(LevelTickEvent.Post event) {
        // 严格过滤：只处理服务端 + 木星维度
        if (event.getLevel().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!event.getLevel().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        ServerLevel jupiter = (ServerLevel) event.getLevel();
        ResourceKey<Level> key = jupiter.dimension();

        // ===== 强制劈雷调试 =====
        if (JupiterStormManager.isForceThunder()) {
            if (jupiter.players().isEmpty()) return;
            ServerPlayer player = jupiter.players().get(jupiter.random.nextInt(jupiter.players().size()));
            double x = player.getX() + (jupiter.random.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * LIGHTNING_RADIUS;
            double z = player.getZ() + (jupiter.random.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * LIGHTNING_RADIUS;
            int y = jupiter.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, (int) x, (int) z);
            LightningBolt bolt = new LightningBolt(EntityType.LIGHTNING_BOLT, jupiter);
            bolt.moveTo(x, y, z);
            bolt.setVisualOnly(true);
            jupiter.addFreshEntity(bolt);
            System.out.println("[Thunder] FORCED bolt at " + (int) x + "," + y + "," + (int) z);
            return;
        }

        // ===== 读取并平滑强度 =====
        float strength = JupiterStormManager.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0f;
        strength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(NORM_SUDDEN, strength));

        float last = LAST_STRENGTH.getOrDefault(key, strength);
        float smoothed = last + (strength - last) * 0.2f;
        LAST_STRENGTH.put(key, smoothed);

        // 归一化到 0~1
        float t = Math.min(1f, smoothed / NORM_PEAK);
        float curve = (float) Math.pow(t, CURVE_EXPONENT);

        // ===== 冷却连续耗散 =====
        int cooldown = COOLDOWNS.getOrDefault(key, 0);
        if (cooldown > 0) {
            // 每 tick 按强度扣冷却，加速风暴爆发
            int decrement = 1 + Math.round(curve * MAX_COOLDOWN_ACCEL);
            cooldown -= decrement;
            if (cooldown > 0) {
                COOLDOWNS.put(key, cooldown);
                return;
            }
            // 本 tick 冷却归零，直接劈雷
        }

        // ===== 计算新冷却范围（依据当前 curve） =====
        int cooldownMin = Math.round(COOLDOWN_MIN_STORM
                + (COOLDOWN_MIN_CALM - COOLDOWN_MIN_STORM) * (1 - curve));
        int cooldownMax = Math.round(COOLDOWN_MAX_STORM
                + (COOLDOWN_MAX_CALM - COOLDOWN_MAX_STORM) * (1 - curve));

        if (cooldownMax <= cooldownMin) {
            cooldownMax = cooldownMin + 1;
        }

        int newCooldown = cooldownMin + jupiter.random.nextInt(cooldownMax - cooldownMin + 1);
        COOLDOWNS.put(key, newCooldown);

        // ===== 调试日志 =====
        if (jupiter.getGameTime() % DEBUG_LOG_INTERVAL == 0) {
            System.out.println("[Thunder] strength=" + String.format("%.2f", strength)
                    + " smoothed=" + String.format("%.2f", smoothed)
                    + " curve=" + String.format("%.2f", curve)
                    + " cooldown=" + newCooldown
                    + " players=" + jupiter.players().size());
        }

        if (jupiter.players().isEmpty()) return;

        // ===== 决定劈雷数量 =====
        int extra = 0;
        if (curve > 0.5f) {
            // curve 0.5 -> 最多 2 道; curve 1.0 -> 最多 6 道
            int maxExtra = 1 + (int) ((curve - 0.5f) / 0.5f * 5);
            extra = jupiter.random.nextInt(maxExtra + 1);
        }
        int boltCount = 1 + extra;

        // ===== 生成闪电 =====
        for (int i = 0; i < boltCount; i++) {
            ServerPlayer player = jupiter.players().get(jupiter.random.nextInt(jupiter.players().size()));
            double x = player.getX() + (jupiter.random.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * LIGHTNING_RADIUS;
            double z = player.getZ() + (jupiter.random.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * LIGHTNING_RADIUS;
            int y = jupiter.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, (int) x, (int) z);
            LightningBolt bolt = new LightningBolt(EntityType.LIGHTNING_BOLT, jupiter);
            bolt.moveTo(x, y, z);
            bolt.setVisualOnly(true);
            jupiter.addFreshEntity(bolt);
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\client\JupiterWindParticles.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.BlockParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class JupiterWindParticles {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // ===== 生成范围（略小，降低不必要计算）=====
    private static final int RADIUS = 28;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MIN = -6;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MAX = 20;

    // ===== 激活阈值：强度低于此不生成任何粒子 =====
    private static final float THRESHOLD = 0.25f;

    // ===== 粒子数：从阈值到 2.5 线性放大 =====
    private static final int MIN_COUNT = 8;      // 刚激活时
    private static final int MAX_COUNT = 80;     // 硬上限（原 150，砍半）

    // ===== 风速：随强度线性 =====
    private static final double BASE_SPEED = 1.2;
    private static final double MAX_SPEED = 6.0;

    private static final float WIND_ANGLE = (float) Math.toRadians(35.0f);
    private static final double WIND_JITTER = 0.10;
    private static final double VERTICAL_DRIFT = 0.04;

    // ===== 生成间隔（刻）：每 2 刻生成一次，降低 CPU =====
    private static final int TICK_INTERVAL = 2;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (!player.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (!(player.level() instanceof ClientLevel level)) return;

        // 每 2 刻生成一次（tickCount 累加）
        if (player.tickCount % TICK_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        spawnWind(level, player);
    }

    private static void spawnWind(ClientLevel level, Player player) {
        float strength = JupiterStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0f;
        strength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(2.5f, strength));

        // ===== 阈值判断：平静期直接不生成 =====
        if (strength < THRESHOLD) return;

        // ===== 归一化：0 = 阈值，1 = 2.5 =====
        float t = (strength - THRESHOLD) / (2.5f - THRESHOLD);
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        // ===== 粒子数：线性 =====
        int count = (int) (MIN_COUNT + (MAX_COUNT - MIN_COUNT) * t);
        count = Math.min(count, MAX_COUNT);
        count = Math.max(count, MIN_COUNT);

        // ===== 风速：线性 =====
        double windSpeed = BASE_SPEED + (MAX_SPEED - BASE_SPEED) * t;

        double dirX = Math.cos(WIND_ANGLE);
        double dirZ = Math.sin(WIND_ANGLE);
        double perpX = -dirZ;
        double perpZ = dirX;

        double playerX = player.getX();
        double playerY = player.getY();
        double playerZ = player.getZ();

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            // 生成区域：偏上风侧
            double alongWind = -RADIUS + RNG.nextDouble() * (RADIUS * 1.4);
            double perpendicular = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;

            double px = playerX + dirX * alongWind + perpX * perpendicular;
            double pz = playerZ + dirZ * alongWind + perpZ * perpendicular;
            double py = playerY + HEIGHT_MIN + RNG.nextDouble() * (HEIGHT_MAX - HEIGHT_MIN);

            if (!level.isEmptyBlock(BlockPos.containing(px, py, pz))) continue;

            double speed = windSpeed * (0.75 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.5);
            double vx = dirX * speed + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * WIND_JITTER;
            double vz = dirZ * speed + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * WIND_JITTER;
            double vy = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * VERTICAL_DRIFT;

            // ===== 主粒子：简洁配比，减少类型切换 =====
            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            if (roll < 0.5f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.ORANGE_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else if (roll < 0.8f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.BLACK_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else {
                // 少量 LARGE_SMOKE，增加"雾"感但不占太多
                level.addParticle(
                        ParticleTypes.LARGE_SMOKE,
                        px, py, pz,
                        vx * 0.9, vy, vz * 0.9);
            }

            // ===== 火花：只在强度较高时生成，概率随 t 增长 =====
            if (t > 0.3f && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.05f * t) {
                double sparkSpeed = speed * 1.6;
                level.addParticle(
                        ParticleTypes.FLAME,
                        px, py, pz,
                        dirX * sparkSpeed, (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.15,
                        dirZ * sparkSpeed);
            }

            // ===== 闪电微光：只在接近暴烈时生成 =====
            if (t > 0.6f && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.02f * t) {
                double glowSpeed = speed * 2.2;
                level.addParticle(
                        ParticleTypes.END_ROD,
                        px, py, pz,
                        dirX * glowSpeed, (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.2,
                        dirZ * glowSpeed);
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.client.JupiterStormClientCache;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public record JupiterStormPayload(
        int phaseOrdinal,
        int phaseTimer,
        float stormStrength
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<JupiterStormPayload> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "jupiter_storm"));

    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, JupiterStormPayload> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, JupiterStormPayload::phaseOrdinal,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, JupiterStormPayload::phaseTimer,
                    ByteBufCodecs.FLOAT,   JupiterStormPayload::stormStrength,
                    JupiterStormPayload::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(JupiterStormPayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() ->
                JupiterStormClientCache.set(
                        payload.phaseOrdinal(),
                        payload.phaseTimer(),
                        payload.stormStrength()
                )
        );
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\jupiter\server\JupiterStormManager.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.jupiter.network.JupiterStormPayload;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class JupiterStormManager {

    public enum Phase {
        CALM, RISING, PEAK, SUDDEN, FALLING
    }

    // ===== 各阶段强度范围 =====
    private static final float CALM_MIN = 0.0f,   CALM_MAX = 0.15f;
    private static final float RISING_MIN = 0.2f, RISING_MAX = 0.7f;
    private static final float PEAK_MIN = 1.0f,   PEAK_MAX = 1.5f;
    private static final float SUDDEN_MIN = 2.0f, SUDDEN_MAX = 3.0f;
    private static final float FALLING_MIN = 0.3f, FALLING_MAX = 0.8f;

    // ===== 对外暴露的峰值常量（供 JupiterThunderHandler 归一化使用） =====
    /** PEAK 阶段理论上限，也是“有效闪电强度”的参考峰值 */
    public static final float PEAK_REFERENCE = PEAK_MAX;
    /** SUDDEN 阶段理论上限 */
    public static final float SUDDEN_REFERENCE = SUDDEN_MAX;

    // ===== 各阶段持续时间（tick） =====
    private static final int CALM_MIN_TICKS = 300, CALM_MAX_TICKS = 600;
    private static final int RISING_MIN_TICKS = 200, RISING_MAX_TICKS = 400;
    private static final int PEAK_MIN_TICKS = 300, PEAK_MAX_TICKS = 600;
    private static final int SUDDEN_MIN_TICKS = 600, SUDDEN_MAX_TICKS = 1200;
    private static final int FALLING_MIN_TICKS = 300, FALLING_MAX_TICKS = 600;

    // ===== 平滑与波动参数 =====
    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.20f;
    /** 阶段切换时直接 snap 到新目标的比例（0.7 = 一步到位 70%，剩余靠 SMOOTH 补） */
    private static final float PHASE_SNAP = 0.7f;
    /** 阶段内 baseTarget 重随机间隔（tick），让阶段内也有起伏 */
    private static final int BASE_TARGET_REROLL_INTERVAL = 100;
    /** 叠加在 baseTarget 上的正弦波动幅度与周期 */
    private static final float WAVE_AMPLITUDE = 0.1f;
    private static final float WAVE_PERIOD = 0.01f;

    /** 网络同步间隔（tick） */
    private static final int SYNC_INTERVAL = 10;

    private static Phase currentPhase = Phase.CALM;
    private static int phaseTimer = 0;
    private static int globalTick = 0;
    private static float baseTarget = CALM_MIN;
    private static float targetStrength = CALM_MIN;
    private static float stormStrength = CALM_MIN;

    private static int syncCounter = 0;

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // ===== 强制劈雷调试开关 =====
    private static boolean forceThunder = false;

    public static boolean isForceThunder() { return forceThunder; }
    public static void setForceThunder(boolean value) { forceThunder = value; }

    public static float getStormStrength() { return stormStrength; }
    public static Phase getCurrentPhase()  { return currentPhase; }
    public static int getPhaseTimer()      { return phaseTimer; }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onServerTick(ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
        ServerLevel jupiterLevel = null;
        for (ServerLevel level : event.getServer().getAllLevels()) {
            if (level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.JUPITER_LEVEL_KEY)) {
                jupiterLevel = level;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (jupiterLevel == null) {
            stormStrength = CALM_MIN;
            targetStrength = CALM_MIN;
            baseTarget = CALM_MIN;
            currentPhase = Phase.CALM;
            phaseTimer = 0;
            return;
        }

        globalTick++;

        // ===== 阶段推进 =====
        phaseTimer--;
        if (phaseTimer <= 0) {
            switchToNextPhase();
            baseTarget = getRandomStrengthForPhase(currentPhase);
            // 阶段突变：一步到位 snap 到新目标的 70%，剩余 30% 交给 SMOOTH
            stormStrength += (baseTarget - stormStrength) * PHASE_SNAP;
        } else if (globalTick % BASE_TARGET_REROLL_INTERVAL == 0) {
            // 阶段内每 100 tick 重随机一次 baseTarget，让阶段内也有起伏
            baseTarget = getRandomStrengthForPhase(currentPhase);
        }

        // ===== 正弦波动 + 平滑 =====
        float wave = (float) Math.sin(globalTick * WAVE_PERIOD) * WAVE_AMPLITUDE;
        targetStrength = baseTarget + wave;
        stormStrength += (targetStrength - stormStrength) * SMOOTH;

        // ===== 安全钳制 =====
        if (Float.isNaN(stormStrength) || Float.isInfinite(stormStrength)) {
            stormStrength = CALM_MIN;
        }
        stormStrength = Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(SUDDEN_MAX, stormStrength));

        // ===== 调试日志（每 20 tick 一次） =====
        if (globalTick % 20 == 0) {
            System.out.println("[Storm] phase=" + currentPhase
                    + " strength=" + String.format("%.2f", stormStrength)
                    + " target=" + String.format("%.2f", targetStrength));
        }

        // ===== 网络同步 =====
        syncCounter++;
        if (syncCounter >= SYNC_INTERVAL) {
            syncCounter = 0;
            JupiterStormPayload payload = new JupiterStormPayload(
                    currentPhase.ordinal(),
                    phaseTimer,
                    stormStrength
            );
            for (ServerPlayer player : jupiterLevel.players()) {
                PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, payload);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void switchToNextPhase() {
        Phase next;
        switch (currentPhase) {
            case CALM -> {
                float r = RNG.nextFloat();
                if (r < 0.4f)      next = Phase.SUDDEN;
                else if (r < 0.9f) next = Phase.RISING;
                else               next = Phase.PEAK;
            }
            case RISING -> next = RNG.nextFloat() < 0.9f ? Phase.PEAK : Phase.SUDDEN;
            case PEAK -> next = RNG.nextFloat() < 0.7f ? Phase.SUDDEN : Phase.FALLING;
            case SUDDEN -> next = RNG.nextFloat() < 0.4f ? Phase.PEAK : Phase.FALLING;
            case FALLING -> next = Phase.CALM;
            default -> next = Phase.CALM;
        }
        currentPhase = next;
        phaseTimer = getRandomDuration(next);
    }

    private static float getRandomStrengthForPhase(Phase phase) {
        return switch (phase) {
            case CALM -> lerp(CALM_MIN, CALM_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
            case RISING -> lerp(RISING_MIN, RISING_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
            case PEAK -> lerp(PEAK_MIN, PEAK_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
            case SUDDEN -> lerp(SUDDEN_MIN, SUDDEN_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
            case FALLING -> lerp(FALLING_MIN, FALLING_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
        };
    }

    private static int getRandomDuration(Phase phase) {
        return switch (phase) {
            case CALM -> CALM_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(CALM_MAX_TICKS - CALM_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case RISING -> RISING_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(RISING_MAX_TICKS - RISING_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case PEAK -> PEAK_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(PEAK_MAX_TICKS - PEAK_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case SUDDEN -> SUDDEN_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(SUDDEN_MAX_TICKS - SUDDEN_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case FALLING -> FALLING_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(FALLING_MAX_TICKS - FALLING_MIN_TICKS + 1);
        };
    }

    private static float lerp(float a, float b, float t) {
        return a + (b - a) * t;
    }
}

================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ViewportEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsCameraShake {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final double MAX_DISTANCE = 64.0;
    private static final float MAX_AMPLITUDE = 1.6f;
    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.08f;

    private static float smoothedAmplitude = 0f;
    private static float shakeTime = 0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onComputeCameraAngles(ViewportEvent.ComputeCameraAngles event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null || mc.player == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float targetAmplitude = 0f;

        // 改：从 ClientCache 读
        BlockPos vp = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getVolcanoPos();
        float intensity = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getEruptionIntensity();

        if (vp != null && intensity > 0f) {
            Vec3 p = mc.player.position();
            double dx = p.x - (vp.getX() + 0.5);
            double dy = p.y - (vp.getY() + 0.5);
            double dz = p.z - (vp.getZ() + 0.5);
            double dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);

            if (dist < MAX_DISTANCE) {
                double t = dist / MAX_DISTANCE;
                double distanceFactor = 1.0 - t * t;
                targetAmplitude = (float) (MAX_AMPLITUDE * intensity * distanceFactor);
            }
        }

        smoothedAmplitude += (targetAmplitude - smoothedAmplitude) * SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(smoothedAmplitude)) smoothedAmplitude = 0f;
        if (smoothedAmplitude < 0.01f) return;

        shakeTime += 1.0f;

        float jitterX = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * smoothedAmplitude;
        float jitterY = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * smoothedAmplitude;

        float waveX = (float) Math.sin(shakeTime * 0.35f) * smoothedAmplitude * 0.4f;
        float waveY = (float) Math.cos(shakeTime * 0.42f) * smoothedAmplitude * 0.3f;

        event.setYaw(event.getYaw() + jitterX + waveX);
        event.setPitch(event.getPitch() + jitterY + waveY);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\client\MarsClientSetup.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RegisterDimensionSpecialEffectsEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsClientSetup {

    public static final ResourceLocation MARS_SKY =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "mars_sky");

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void registerDimensionEffects(RegisterDimensionSpecialEffectsEvent event) {
        event.register(MARS_SKY, new MarsSkyEffects());
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\client\MarsDustStormCameraShake.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ViewportEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsDustStormCameraShake {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.03f;
    private static final float SHAKE_THRESHOLD = 0.7f;   // 低于此强度不晃
    private static final float MAX_AMPLITUDE = 0.35f;    // 比火山轻
    private static final float SHAKE_FREQUENCY = 0.05f;

    private static float smoothedStrength = 0f;
    private static float shakeTime = 0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onComputeCameraAngles(ViewportEvent.ComputeCameraAngles event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float strength = MarsDustStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0f;
        strength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, strength));

        smoothedStrength += (strength - smoothedStrength) * SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(smoothedStrength)) smoothedStrength = 0f;

        if (smoothedStrength <= SHAKE_THRESHOLD) return;

        float intensity = (smoothedStrength - SHAKE_THRESHOLD) / (1.5f - SHAKE_THRESHOLD);
        intensity = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, intensity));

        shakeTime += 1f;

        // 慢速正弦 + 轻随机
        float waveX = (float) Math.sin(shakeTime * SHAKE_FREQUENCY) * intensity * MAX_AMPLITUDE;
        float waveY = (float) Math.cos(shakeTime * SHAKE_FREQUENCY * 1.2f) * intensity * MAX_AMPLITUDE * 0.6f;

        float jitterX = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * intensity * 0.15f;
        float jitterY = (RNG.nextFloat() - 0.5f) * intensity * 0.15f;

        event.setYaw(event.getYaw() + waveX + jitterX);
        event.setPitch(event.getPitch() + waveY + jitterY);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\client\MarsDustStormClientCache.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug.DebugState;

public final class MarsDustStormClientCache {

    public enum Phase {
        CALM, RISING, PEAK, FALLING
    }

    private static int phaseOrdinal = Phase.CALM.ordinal();
    private static int phaseTimer = 0;
    private static float stormStrength = 0f;

    private MarsDustStormClientCache() {}

    public static void set(int ordinal, int timer, float strength) {
        if (DebugState.getMarsDustForcedStrength() >= 0) {
            return;
        }
        phaseOrdinal = ordinal;
        phaseTimer = timer;
        stormStrength = strength;
    }

    public static Phase getPhase() {
        int debugPhase = DebugState.getMarsDustPhase();
        if (debugPhase >= 0) {
            Phase[] v = Phase.values();
            return v[Math.max(0, Math.min(v.length - 1, debugPhase))];
        }
        Phase[] v = Phase.values();
        int i = Math.max(0, Math.min(v.length - 1, phaseOrdinal));
        return v[i];
    }

    public static int getPhaseTimer() {
        if (DebugState.getMarsDustForcedStrength() >= 0) return 0;
        return phaseTimer;
    }

    public static float getStormStrength() {
        float debug = DebugState.tickMarsDustSmooth();
        if (debug >= 0) return debug;
        return stormStrength;
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\client\MarsDustStormGustParticles.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.BlockParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

/**
 * 火星沙尘暴"强风"粒子。
 * 对标 JupiterStormGustParticles，但：
 *  - 颜色改黄褐（ORANGE / YELLOW / BROWN 混合）
 *  - 风向角度不同
 *  - 阈值适配火星沙尘暴强度范围（0~1.5）
 *  - 只在沙尘暴 ≥ 0.8 时激活
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsDustStormGustParticles {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // 激活阈值：火星沙尘暴最大强度 1.5
    private static final float THRESHOLD = 0.8f;

    // 生成范围
    private static final int RADIUS = 26;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MIN = -2;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MAX = 20;

    // 粒子数
    private static final int BASE_COUNT = 30;
    private static final int MAX_COUNT = 260;
    private static final int MIN_COUNT = 10;

    // 横向速度（比木星稍慢一点，火星沙尘暴更"闷"）
    private static final double BASE_SPEED = 1.8;
    private static final double SPEED_BOOST = 1.4;

    // 风向：火星沙尘暴的风向和 JupiterWindParticles 不同
    // 木星是 35°，火星取 150°（沙尘暴从另一侧刮来）
    private static final float WIND_ANGLE = (float) Math.toRadians(150.0f);
    private static final double JITTER = 0.22;

    // 垂直漂移
    private static final double VERTICAL_DRIFT = 0.03;

    // 生成间隔
    private static final int TICK_INTERVAL = 1;

    // 阵风脉冲状态
    private static long globalTick = 0;
    private static double gustPhase = 0.0;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (!player.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (!(player.level() instanceof ClientLevel level)) return;

        globalTick++;

        if (level.getGameTime() % TICK_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        spawnGust(level, player);
    }

    private static void spawnGust(ClientLevel level, Player player) {
        float strength = MarsDustStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0f;
        strength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, strength));

        // 阈值判断
        if (strength < THRESHOLD) return;

        // 归一化：0.8 → 0，1.5 → 1
        float t = (strength - THRESHOLD) / (1.5f - THRESHOLD);
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        // 阵风脉冲
        double pulseFreq = 0.25 + t * 1.5;
        gustPhase += pulseFreq;
        double gustEnvelope = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(gustPhase);
        double gustEnvelope2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(gustPhase * 2.7 + 1.3);
        double gust = gustEnvelope * 0.7 + gustEnvelope2 * 0.3;
        gust = 0.5 + 0.5 * t + gust * t * 0.8;

        // 粒子数
        int baseCount = (int) (BASE_COUNT + (MAX_COUNT - BASE_COUNT) * t);
        int gustBoost = (int) (t * t * 100 * gustEnvelope);
        int count = baseCount + gustBoost;
        count = Math.min(count, MAX_COUNT);
        count = Math.max(count, MIN_COUNT);

        // 风速
        double speed = BASE_SPEED * (0.6 + t * 0.8) * (1 + gust * SPEED_BOOST * 0.3);

        double dirX = Math.cos(WIND_ANGLE);
        double dirZ = Math.sin(WIND_ANGLE);

        // 暴烈时风向缓慢摆动
        if (t > 0.3f) {
            double wobble = Math.sin(globalTick * 0.07) * (t - 0.3f) * 0.5;
            double cos = Math.cos(wobble);
            double sin = Math.sin(wobble);
            double nx = dirX * cos - dirZ * sin;
            double nz = dirX * sin + dirZ * cos;
            dirX = nx;
            dirZ = nz;
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            double dx = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;
            double dz = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;
            double dy = HEIGHT_MIN + RNG.nextDouble() * (HEIGHT_MAX - HEIGHT_MIN);

            double px = player.getX() + dx;
            double py = player.getY() + dy;
            double pz = player.getZ() + dz;

            if (!level.isEmptyBlock(BlockPos.containing(px, py, pz))) continue;

            double vx = dirX * speed * (0.7 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6)
                    + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * JITTER * speed;
            double vz = dirZ * speed * (0.7 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6)
                    + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * JITTER * speed;

            double vy;
            if (t > 0.5f && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.3f) {
                vy = (t - 0.5f) * 0.6 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.3;
            } else {
                vy = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * VERTICAL_DRIFT;
            }

            // 火星沙尘暴配色：橙 / 黄 / 棕 三种混合
            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            if (roll < 0.45f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.ORANGE_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else if (roll < 0.75f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.YELLOW_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.BROWN_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            }

            // 高强度时加 ASH
            if (t > 0.4f && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.12f * t) {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.ASH,
                        px, py, pz,
                        vx * 0.7, vy + 0.05, vz * 0.7);
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsDustStormOverlay {

    private static final float CLIENT_SMOOTH = 0.01f;
    private static final int MAX_ALPHA = 140;

    private static float clientSmoothed = 0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float strength = MarsDustStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0f;
        strength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, strength));

        float target = strength;
        clientSmoothed += (target - clientSmoothed) * CLIENT_SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(clientSmoothed)) clientSmoothed = 0f;

        if (clientSmoothed < 0.05f) return;

        float t = clientSmoothed / 1.5f;
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));
        int alpha = (int) (t * MAX_ALPHA);
        if (alpha <= 0) return;

        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int width = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int height = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        // 黄褐色：R=0xC8, G=0x96, B=0x50
        int color = (alpha << 24) | 0xC89650;
        g.fill(0, 0, width, height, color);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\client\MarsDustStormParticles.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.BlockParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsDustStormParticles {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final int RADIUS = 24;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MIN = -4;
    private static final int HEIGHT_MAX = 16;

    private static final int BASE_COUNT = 20;
    private static final int MAX_COUNT = 120;
    private static final int MIN_COUNT = 0;

    // ===== 横向速度大幅提高 =====
    private static final double BASE_SPEED = 1.2;   // 原 0.35 → 1.2
    private static final float WIND_ANGLE = (float) Math.toRadians(120.0f);
    private static final double JITTER = 0.15;      // 原 0.06 → 0.15

    // 垂直抖动保持很小（沙尘基本水平吹）
    private static final double VERTICAL_DRIFT = 0.02;

    private static final int TICK_INTERVAL = 3;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (!player.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (!(player.level() instanceof ClientLevel level)) return;

        if (level.getGameTime() % TICK_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        spawnDust(level, player);
    }

    private static void spawnDust(ClientLevel level, Player player) {
        float strength = MarsDustStormClientCache.getStormStrength();
        if (Float.isNaN(strength) || Float.isInfinite(strength)) strength = 0f;
        strength = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, strength));

        if (strength < 0.1f) return;

        int count = (int) (BASE_COUNT * strength);
        count = Math.min(count, MAX_COUNT);
        count = Math.max(count, MIN_COUNT);
        if (count <= 0) return;

        // 风速：强度越高越快，最高约 1.2 * 1.25 = 1.5
        double speed = BASE_SPEED * (0.5 + strength * 0.5);

        double dirX = Math.cos(WIND_ANGLE);
        double dirZ = Math.sin(WIND_ANGLE);

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            double dx = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;
            double dz = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;
            double dy = HEIGHT_MIN + RNG.nextDouble() * (HEIGHT_MAX - HEIGHT_MIN);

            double px = player.getX() + dx;
            double py = player.getY() + dy;
            double pz = player.getZ() + dz;

            if (!level.isEmptyBlock(BlockPos.containing(px, py, pz))) continue;

            // 横向速度：主风向 * 风速 + 随机抖动
            double vx = dirX * speed * (0.7 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6)
                    + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * JITTER;
            double vz = dirZ * speed * (0.7 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6)
                    + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * JITTER;
            // 垂直速度很小
            double vy = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * VERTICAL_DRIFT;

            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            if (roll < 0.5f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.ORANGE_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else if (roll < 0.85f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.YELLOW_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else {
                level.addParticle(
                        new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.BROWN_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            }

            // 高强度时加一点 ASH 颗粒感
            if (strength > 0.9f && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.1f * strength) {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.ASH, px, py, pz, vx * 0.6, vy, vz * 0.6);
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MarsGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "mars_low_gravity");

    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 0.38;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInMars = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInMars && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInMars && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import net.minecraft.client.renderer.DimensionSpecialEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class MarsSkyEffects extends DimensionSpecialEffects {

    public MarsSkyEffects() {
        super(0.0f, false, SkyType.NONE, false, false);
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull Vec3 getBrightnessDependentFogColor(@NotNull Vec3 fogColor, float brightness) {
        // 火星：暗红棕
        float r = 0.05f + 0.30f * brightness;
        float g = 0.02f + 0.13f * brightness;
        float b = 0.01f + 0.07f * brightness;
        return new Vec3(r, g, b);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isFoggyAt(int x, int z) {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public float[] getSunriseColor(float timeOfDay, float partialTick) {
        // ★ 关键修复：返回 null（不画红晕）或长度 4 的 float[]
        return null;

        // 如果要画淡淡橙红日出红晕：
        // return new float[]{0.6f, 0.25f, 0.12f, 1.0f};
    }
}
