package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderLevelStageEvent;
import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsSkyRenderer {

    private static final float ROTATION_SPEED = 1.0f;

    // 火星有小行星带之外的星空视野，星星数量适中
    private static final int STAR_COUNT = 3000;
    private static final float STAR_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // 贴图
    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EARTH_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/earth_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation VENUS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/venus_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation JUPITER_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/jupiter_icon.png");

    // 太阳（火星距太阳 1.52 AU，比地球远，太阳看起来略小）
    private static final float SUN_SIZE = 8.0f;
    private static final float SUN_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // 地球（外侧行星，靠近太阳升起/落下的方向）
    // 火星看地球：距角最大约 47°，多数时候在太阳附近
    private static final float EARTH_SIZE = 1.5f;
    private static final float EARTH_DISTANCE = 99.0f;
    private static final float EARTH_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(35.0f);
    private static final float EARTH_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(8.0f);

    // 金星（内侧行星，也靠近太阳，与地球反向）
    private static final float VENUS_SIZE = 1.2f;
    private static final float VENUS_DISTANCE = 99.0f;
    private static final float VENUS_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(-30.0f);
    private static final float VENUS_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(-6.0f);

    // 木星（外侧行星，位置独立于太阳，出现在"半夜"方向）
    private static final float JUPITER_SIZE = 5.0f;
    private static final float JUPITER_DISTANCE = 98.0f;
    private static final float JUPITER_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(150.0f);
    private static final float JUPITER_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(20.0f);

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);
        float timeOfDay = mc.level.getTimeOfDay(partialTick);

        // ===== 相机 + 昼夜自转矩阵 =====
        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));
        mvStack.rotateX((float) Math.toRadians(timeOfDay * 360.0f * ROTATION_SPEED));
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // ===== 渲染状态 =====
        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // ==================== 1. 星星 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator starTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder starBuffer = starTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);

        RandomSource random = RandomSource.create(30721L);   // 火星专属种子

        for (int i = 0; i < STAR_COUNT; i++) {
            float cosTheta = random.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            float sinTheta = Mth.sqrt(1.0f - cosTheta * cosTheta);
            float phi = random.nextFloat() * Mth.TWO_PI;

            float x = sinTheta * Mth.cos(phi);
            float y = cosTheta;
            float z = sinTheta * Mth.sin(phi);

            Vector3f starPos = new Vector3f(x, y, z).mul(STAR_DISTANCE);
            Quaternionf starRot = new Quaternionf().rotateTo(new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F), starPos);
            float size = 0.06f + random.nextFloat() * 0.05f;
            Vector3f starRight = new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);
            Vector3f starUp    = new Vector3f(0.0F, 1.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);

            // 火星有稀薄大气，星空略亮
            float brightness = 0.20f + random.nextFloat() * 0.40f;

            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
        }

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(starBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 2. 太阳 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, SUN_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator sunTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder sunBuffer = sunTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(sunBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 3. 地球 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, EARTH_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.85f, 0.85f, 0.85f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator earthTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder earthBuffer = earthTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(earthBuffer, EARTH_DISTANCE, EARTH_SIZE, EARTH_OFFSET_X, EARTH_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(earthBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 4. 金星 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, VENUS_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.9f, 0.85f, 0.75f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator venusTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder venusBuffer = venusTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(venusBuffer, VENUS_DISTANCE, VENUS_SIZE, VENUS_OFFSET_X, VENUS_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(venusBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 5. 木星 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, JUPITER_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.9f, 0.85f, 0.75f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator jupiterTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder jupiterBuffer = jupiterTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(jupiterBuffer, JUPITER_DISTANCE, JUPITER_SIZE, JUPITER_OFFSET_X, JUPITER_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(jupiterBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ===== 恢复 =====
        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);   // 关键：恢复默认颜色
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    /** 在天顶平面上，按角度偏移画一个天体四边形 */
    private static void drawCelestialQuad(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                          float distance,
                                          float size,
                                          float offsetX,
                                          float offsetY) {
        Vector3f topLeft     = rotateOffset(-size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomLeft  = rotateOffset(-size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomRight = rotateOffset( size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f topRight    = rotateOffset( size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);

        buffer.addVertex(topLeft.x,     topLeft.y,     topLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomLeft.x,  bottomLeft.y,  bottomLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomRight.x, bottomRight.y, bottomRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(topRight.x,    topRight.y,    topRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }

    /** 把一个天顶平面上的点，按水平/垂直角度偏移旋转 */
    private static Vector3f rotateOffset(float x, float y, float z,
                                         float offsetX, float offsetY) {
        Vector3f v = new Vector3f(x, y, z);
        if (offsetY != 0) v.rotateZ(offsetY);
        if (offsetX != 0) v.rotateX(offsetX);
        return v;
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;

/**
 * 客户端火山状态镜像：由 MarsVolcanoPayload 写入，HUD/相机/粒子读取。
 */
public final class MarsVolcanoClientCache {

    private static BlockPos volcanoPos = null;
    private static int eruptionTicks = 0;
    private static int eruptionTotalTicks = 0;
    private static int cooldownTimer = 0;

    private MarsVolcanoClientCache() {}

    public static void set(BlockPos pos, int remain, int total, int cooldown) {
        volcanoPos = pos;
        eruptionTicks = remain;
        eruptionTotalTicks = total;
        cooldownTimer = cooldown;
    }

    public static BlockPos getVolcanoPos()      { return volcanoPos; }
    public static int getEruptionTicks()        { return eruptionTicks; }
    public static int getEruptionTotalTicks()   { return eruptionTotalTicks; }
    public static int getCooldownTimer()        { return cooldownTimer; }

    public static boolean isErupting() {
        return volcanoPos != null && eruptionTicks > 0;
    }

    /** 喷发强度 0~1，中间最强（抛物线包络） */
    public static float getEruptionIntensity() {
        if (!isErupting() || eruptionTotalTicks <= 0) return 0f;
        float progress = 1f - (float) eruptionTicks / eruptionTotalTicks;
        float envelope = 4f * progress * (1f - progress);
        return 0.25f + 0.75f * envelope;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\client\MarsVolcanoHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.BlockParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MarsVolcanoHandler {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // 爆发期 / 稳定期每刻粒子数
    private static final int BURST_PARTICLES = 60;
    private static final int STEADY_PARTICLES = 35;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        ClientLevel level = mc.level;
        if (level == null || mc.player == null) return;
        if (!level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        if (!MarsVolcanoClientCache.isErupting()) return;

        BlockPos pos = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getVolcanoPos();
        if (pos == null) return;

        spawnEruptionParticles(level, pos);
    }

    private static void spawnEruptionParticles(ClientLevel level, BlockPos pos) {
        double cx = pos.getX() + 0.5;
        double cy = pos.getY() + 0.5;
        double cz = pos.getZ() + 0.5;

        float intensity = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getEruptionIntensity();
        int total = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getEruptionTotalTicks();
        int remain = MarsVolcanoClientCache.getEruptionTicks();
        float progress = total > 0 ? 1f - (float) remain / total : 0f;
        boolean burstPhase = progress < 0.15f;

        // ===== 1) 主喷发柱 =====
        int columnCount = burstPhase
                ? BURST_PARTICLES
                : (int) (STEADY_PARTICLES * (0.5f + intensity));

        for (int i = 0; i < columnCount; i++) {
            double heightFactor = RNG.nextDouble();
            double spread = 0.4 + heightFactor * 2.5;

            double vx = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * spread;
            double vy = 1.5 + RNG.nextDouble() * 2.5 + intensity * 1.5;
            double vz = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * spread;

            double px = cx + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 1.0;
            double py = cy + RNG.nextDouble() * 2.5;
            double pz = cz + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 1.0;

            if (heightFactor < 0.3) {
                float r = RNG.nextFloat();
                if (r < 0.5f) {
                    level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.FLAME, px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
                } else if (r < 0.8f) {
                    level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.LAVA, px, py, pz, vx * 0.6, vy * 0.7, vz * 0.6);
                } else {
                    level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.LARGE_SMOKE, px, py, pz, vx, vy * 0.8, vz);
                }
            } else if (heightFactor < 0.7) {
                float r = RNG.nextFloat();
                if (r < 0.6f) {
                    level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.LARGE_SMOKE, px, py, pz, vx * 1.2, vy * 0.9, vz * 1.2);
                } else if (r < 0.85f) {
                    level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.ASH, px, py, pz, vx, vy * 0.6, vz);
                } else {
                    level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.FLAME, px, py, pz, vx * 0.5, vy * 0.5, vz * 0.5);
                }
            } else {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE, px, py, pz,
                        vx * 1.5, vy * 0.4, vz * 1.5);
            }
        }

        // ===== 2) 熔岩喷泉 =====
        int lavaCount = burstPhase ? 25 : (int) (8 * intensity);
        for (int i = 0; i < lavaCount; i++) {
            double angle = RNG.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2;
            double speed = 0.6 + RNG.nextDouble() * 1.8 * intensity;
            double vx = Math.cos(angle) * speed;
            double vz = Math.sin(angle) * speed;
            double vy = 2.0 + RNG.nextDouble() * 2.0;

            level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.LAVA, cx, cy + 1.0, cz, vx, vy, vz);
        }

        // ===== 3) 地面冲击波 =====
        int ringCount = (int) (20 * intensity) + (burstPhase ? 20 : 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < ringCount; i++) {
            double angle = RNG.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2;
            double r = 1.0 + RNG.nextDouble() * 4.0;
            double px = cx + Math.cos(angle) * r;
            double pz = cz + Math.sin(angle) * r;
            double py = cy + 0.1;

            double vx = Math.cos(angle) * (0.5 + intensity);
            double vz = Math.sin(angle) * (0.5 + intensity);
            double vy = 0.3 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6;

            float r2 = RNG.nextFloat();
            if (r2 < 0.5f) {
                level.addParticle(new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.ORANGE_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else if (r2 < 0.8f) {
                level.addParticle(new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.FALLING_DUST,
                                Blocks.BLACK_CONCRETE.defaultBlockState()),
                        px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.LARGE_SMOKE, px, py, pz,
                        vx * 1.5, vy, vz * 1.5);
            }
        }

        // ===== 4) 核心闪光 =====
        int coreCount = burstPhase ? 15 : (int) (5 * intensity);
        for (int i = 0; i < coreCount; i++) {
            level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.FLAME,
                    cx + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 1.5,
                    cy + RNG.nextDouble() * 2.0,
                    cz + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 1.5,
                    (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.4,
                    0.3 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.6,
                    (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.4);
        }
        if (burstPhase || RNG.nextFloat() < 0.4f) {
            level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.END_ROD, cx, cy + 1.0 + RNG.nextDouble() * 1.5, cz, 0, 0.1, 0);
        }
        if (burstPhase || RNG.nextFloat() < 0.25f) {
            level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.SOUL_FIRE_FLAME,
                    cx + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.8,
                    cy + 0.5 + RNG.nextDouble() * 1.2,
                    cz + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.8,
                    0, 0.2, 0);
        }

        // ===== 5) 高空烟柱 =====
        int plumeCount = burstPhase ? 12 : (int) (4 * intensity);
        for (int i = 0; i < plumeCount; i++) {
            double topY = cy + 6.0 + RNG.nextDouble() * 6.0;
            double spread = 2.0 + RNG.nextDouble() * 3.0;
            level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE,
                    cx + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * spread,
                    topY,
                    cz + (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * spread,
                    (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.3,
                    0.05,
                    (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.3);
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client.MarsDustStormClientCache;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public record MarsDustStormPayload(
        int phaseOrdinal,
        int phaseTimer,
        float stormStrength
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<MarsDustStormPayload> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "mars_dust_storm"));

    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, MarsDustStormPayload> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, MarsDustStormPayload::phaseOrdinal,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, MarsDustStormPayload::phaseTimer,
                    ByteBufCodecs.FLOAT,   MarsDustStormPayload::stormStrength,
                    MarsDustStormPayload::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(MarsDustStormPayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() ->
                MarsDustStormClientCache.set(
                        payload.phaseOrdinal(),
                        payload.phaseTimer(),
                        payload.stormStrength()
                )
        );
    }
}

================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.server.MarsVolcanoManager;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

/**
 * C2S 调试包：客户端请求服务端切换火山状态。
 * mode: 0 = 强制喷发，1 = 强制冷却
 */
public record MarsVolcanoDebugPayload(int mode) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final int MODE_ERUPT = 0;
    public static final int MODE_COOLDOWN = 1;

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<MarsVolcanoDebugPayload> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "mars_volcano_debug"));

    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, MarsVolcanoDebugPayload> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, MarsVolcanoDebugPayload::mode,
                    MarsVolcanoDebugPayload::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(MarsVolcanoDebugPayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            if (payload.mode() == MODE_ERUPT) {
                MarsVolcanoManager.debugForceErupt();
            } else {
                MarsVolcanoManager.debugForceCooldown();
            }
        });
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\network\MarsVolcanoPayload.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.client.MarsVolcanoClientCache;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public record MarsVolcanoPayload(
        boolean erupting,
        BlockPos volcanoPos,
        int eruptionTicks,
        int eruptionTotalTicks,
        int cooldownTimer
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<MarsVolcanoPayload> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "mars_volcano"));

    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, MarsVolcanoPayload> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.BOOL,     MarsVolcanoPayload::erupting,
                    BlockPos.STREAM_CODEC,  MarsVolcanoPayload::volcanoPos,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT,  MarsVolcanoPayload::eruptionTicks,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT,  MarsVolcanoPayload::eruptionTotalTicks,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT,  MarsVolcanoPayload::cooldownTimer,
                    MarsVolcanoPayload::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(MarsVolcanoPayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            BlockPos pos = payload.erupting() ? payload.volcanoPos() : null;
            MarsVolcanoClientCache.set(
                    pos,
                    payload.eruptionTicks(),
                    payload.eruptionTotalTicks(),
                    payload.cooldownTimer()
            );
        });
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\server\MarsDustStormDamageHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MarsDustStormDamageHandler {

    // 沙尘暴强度阈值：低于此不伤害
    private static final float DAMAGE_THRESHOLD = 0.6f;

    // 最大每秒伤害（在强度 1.5 时）
    private static final float MAX_DPS = 2.0f;

    // 伤害间隔（刻）
    private static final int DAMAGE_INTERVAL = 40;   // 2 秒

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (player.isCreative() || player.isSpectator()) return;

        float strength = MarsDustStormManager.getStormStrength();
        if (strength <= DAMAGE_THRESHOLD) return;

        if (player.tickCount % DAMAGE_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        // 归一化：0 = 阈值，1 = 最大强度
        float t = (strength - DAMAGE_THRESHOLD) / (1.5f - DAMAGE_THRESHOLD);
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        float damage = MAX_DPS * t * DAMAGE_INTERVAL / 20f;
        if (damage <= 0.01f) return;

        // 用 generic 伤害，避免火焰免疫
        player.hurt(player.damageSources().generic(), damage);
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\server\MarsDustStormManager.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network.MarsDustStormPayload;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

/**
 * 火星沙尘暴状态机（服务端权威）。
 * 节奏比木星风暴慢，强度范围 0~1.5。
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MarsDustStormManager {

    public enum Phase {
        CALM, RISING, PEAK, FALLING
    }

    // ===== 各阶段强度范围 =====
    private static final float CALM_MIN = 0.0f,   CALM_MAX = 0.15f;
    private static final float RISING_MIN = 0.3f, RISING_MAX = 0.8f;
    private static final float PEAK_MIN = 1.0f,   PEAK_MAX = 1.5f;
    private static final float FALLING_MIN = 0.3f, FALLING_MAX = 0.7f;

    // ===== 各阶段持续时间（刻）——比木星更长、更慢 =====
    private static final int CALM_MIN_TICKS    = 3600, CALM_MAX_TICKS    = 9000;
    private static final int RISING_MIN_TICKS  = 600,  RISING_MAX_TICKS  = 1200;  // 30~60s
    private static final int PEAK_MIN_TICKS    = 1200, PEAK_MAX_TICKS    = 2400;  // 60~120s
    private static final int FALLING_MIN_TICKS = 600,  FALLING_MAX_TICKS = 1200;  // 30~60s

    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.008f;       // 比木星更平滑
    private static final float WAVE_AMPLITUDE = 0.08f;
    private static final float WAVE_PERIOD = 0.005f;

    // ===== 同步间隔 =====
    private static final int SYNC_INTERVAL = 10;

    private static Phase currentPhase = Phase.CALM;
    private static int phaseTimer = 0;
    private static int globalTick = 0;
    private static float baseTarget = CALM_MIN;
    private static float targetStrength = CALM_MIN;
    private static float stormStrength = CALM_MIN;

    private static int syncCounter = 0;

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    // ===== Getter（服务端用）=====
    public static float getStormStrength() { return stormStrength; }
    public static Phase getCurrentPhase()  { return currentPhase; }
    public static int getPhaseTimer()      { return phaseTimer; }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onServerTick(ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
        ServerLevel marsLevel = null;
        for (ServerLevel level : event.getServer().getAllLevels()) {
            if (level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) {
                marsLevel = level;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (marsLevel == null) {
            stormStrength = CALM_MIN;
            targetStrength = CALM_MIN;
            baseTarget = CALM_MIN;
            currentPhase = Phase.CALM;
            phaseTimer = 0;
            return;
        }

        globalTick++;

        phaseTimer--;
        if (phaseTimer <= 0) {
            switchToNextPhase();
            baseTarget = getRandomStrengthForPhase(currentPhase);
        }

        float wave = (float) Math.sin(globalTick * WAVE_PERIOD) * WAVE_AMPLITUDE;
        targetStrength = baseTarget + wave;
        stormStrength += (targetStrength - stormStrength) * SMOOTH;

        if (Float.isNaN(stormStrength) || Float.isInfinite(stormStrength)) {
            stormStrength = CALM_MIN;
        }
        stormStrength = Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(PEAK_MAX, stormStrength));

        // ===== 定期同步 =====
        syncCounter++;
        if (syncCounter >= SYNC_INTERVAL) {
            syncCounter = 0;
            broadcast(marsLevel);
        }
    }

    private static void broadcast(ServerLevel marsLevel) {
        MarsDustStormPayload payload = new MarsDustStormPayload(
                currentPhase.ordinal(),
                phaseTimer,
                stormStrength
        );
        for (ServerPlayer player : marsLevel.players()) {
            PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, payload);
        }
    }

    private static void switchToNextPhase() {
        Phase next;
        switch (currentPhase) {
            case CALM -> next = RNG.nextFloat() < 0.75f ? Phase.RISING : Phase.PEAK;
            case RISING -> next = RNG.nextFloat() < 0.85f ? Phase.PEAK : Phase.FALLING;
            case PEAK -> next = RNG.nextFloat() < 0.7f ? Phase.FALLING : Phase.PEAK;
            case FALLING -> next = Phase.CALM;
            default -> next = Phase.CALM;
        }
        currentPhase = next;
        phaseTimer = getRandomDuration(next);
    }

    private static float getRandomStrengthForPhase(Phase phase) {
        return switch (phase) {
            case CALM -> lerp(CALM_MIN, CALM_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
            case RISING -> lerp(RISING_MIN, RISING_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
            case PEAK -> lerp(PEAK_MIN, PEAK_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
            case FALLING -> lerp(FALLING_MIN, FALLING_MAX, RNG.nextFloat());
        };
    }

    private static int getRandomDuration(Phase phase) {
        return switch (phase) {
            case CALM -> CALM_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(CALM_MAX_TICKS - CALM_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case RISING -> RISING_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(RISING_MAX_TICKS - RISING_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case PEAK -> PEAK_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(PEAK_MAX_TICKS - PEAK_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case FALLING -> FALLING_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(FALLING_MAX_TICKS - FALLING_MIN_TICKS + 1);
        };
    }

    private static float lerp(float a, float b, float t) {
        return a + (b - a) * t;
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\server\MarsVolcanoDamageHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MarsVolcanoDamageHandler {

    // ===== 伤害半径 & 参数 =====
    // 超过这个距离完全无伤
    private static final double MAX_DAMAGE_RADIUS = 48.0;

    // 在喷发点正上方时的最大每秒伤害（以 1 秒 = 20 刻为基准）
    private static final float MAX_DPS = 8.0f;

    // 伤害间隔（刻）：近距离更频繁
    private static final int MIN_INTERVAL = 5;    // 0.25 秒
    private static final int MAX_INTERVAL = 40;   // 2 秒

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (player.isCreative() || player.isSpectator()) return;

        if (!MarsVolcanoManager.isErupting()) return;

        BlockPos vp = MarsVolcanoManager.getVolcanoPos();
        if (vp == null) return;

        Vec3 p = player.position();
        double dx = p.x - (vp.getX() + 0.5);
        double dy = p.y - (vp.getY() + 0.5);
        double dz = p.z - (vp.getZ() + 0.5);
        double dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);

        if (dist > MAX_DAMAGE_RADIUS) return;

        // ---- 归一化距离：0 = 正上方，1 = 边缘 ----
        double t = dist / MAX_DAMAGE_RADIUS;
        // 距离衰减：平方，近处急剧增大
        double distanceFactor = 1.0 - t * t;

        float intensity = MarsVolcanoManager.getEruptionIntensity();

        // ---- 每秒伤害 = 最大DPS × 强度 × 距离衰减 ----
        float dps = MAX_DPS * intensity * (float) distanceFactor;

        // ---- 伤害间隔：近处更频繁 ----
        // interval 从 MIN_INTERVAL（近）到 MAX_INTERVAL（远）
        int interval = (int) (MIN_INTERVAL + (MAX_INTERVAL - MIN_INTERVAL) * t);

        // ---- 每 interval 刻造成 dps * interval/20 的伤害 ----
        if (player.tickCount % interval != 0) return;

        float damageAmount = dps * interval / 20f;
        if (damageAmount <= 0.01f) return;

        DamageSource src = player.damageSources().lava();
        player.hurt(src, damageAmount);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mars\server\MarsVolcanoManager.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.network.MarsVolcanoPayload;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.server.ServerLifecycleHooks;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MarsVolcanoManager {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final int INTERVAL_MIN = 400;
    private static final int INTERVAL_MAX = 1800;
    private static final int DURATION_MIN = 100;
    private static final int DURATION_MAX = 280;

    private static final int SEARCH_RADIUS = 48;
    private static final int SYNC_INTERVAL = 5;

    private static int cooldownTimer = 0;
    private static int eruptionTicks = 0;
    private static int eruptionTotalTicks = 0;
    private static BlockPos volcanoPos = null;
    private static int syncCounter = 0;

    // ===== Getter =====
    public static BlockPos getVolcanoPos()     { return volcanoPos; }
    public static boolean isErupting()         { return volcanoPos != null && eruptionTicks > 0; }
    public static int getEruptionTicks()       { return eruptionTicks; }
    public static int getEruptionTotalTicks()  { return eruptionTotalTicks; }
    public static int getCooldownTimer()       { return cooldownTimer; }

    public static float getEruptionIntensity() {
        if (!isErupting() || eruptionTotalTicks <= 0) return 0f;
        float progress = 1f - (float) eruptionTicks / eruptionTotalTicks;
        float envelope = 4f * progress * (1f - progress);
        return 0.25f + 0.75f * envelope;
    }

    // ===== 调试 =====
    public static void debugForceErupt() {
        ServerLevel marsLevel = findMarsLevel();
        if (marsLevel == null || marsLevel.players().isEmpty()) return;

        ServerPlayer anchor = marsLevel.players().get(RNG.nextInt(marsLevel.players().size()));

        double angle = RNG.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2;
        double dist = 16 + RNG.nextDouble() * SEARCH_RADIUS;

        int x = (int) (anchor.getX() + Math.cos(angle) * dist);
        int z = (int) (anchor.getZ() + Math.sin(angle) * dist);
        int y = marsLevel.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, x, z);

        volcanoPos = new BlockPos(x, y, z);
        eruptionTicks = DURATION_MIN + RNG.nextInt(DURATION_MAX - DURATION_MIN + 1);
        eruptionTotalTicks = eruptionTicks;
        cooldownTimer = 0;

        broadcastState(marsLevel);
    }

    public static void debugForceCooldown() {
        ServerLevel marsLevel = findMarsLevel();
        if (marsLevel == null) return;

        volcanoPos = null;
        eruptionTicks = 0;
        eruptionTotalTicks = 0;
        cooldownTimer = INTERVAL_MIN + RNG.nextInt(INTERVAL_MAX - INTERVAL_MIN + 1);

        broadcastState(marsLevel);
    }

    private static ServerLevel findMarsLevel() {
        var server = ServerLifecycleHooks.getCurrentServer();
        if (server == null) return null;
        for (ServerLevel level : server.getAllLevels()) {
            if (level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) {
                return level;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onServerTick(ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
        ServerLevel marsLevel = findMarsLevel();
        if (marsLevel == null) {
            volcanoPos = null;
            eruptionTicks = 0;
            eruptionTotalTicks = 0;
            cooldownTimer = 0;
            return;
        }

        if (eruptionTicks > 0 && volcanoPos != null) {
            eruptionTicks--;
            if (eruptionTicks <= 0) {
                volcanoPos = null;
                cooldownTimer = INTERVAL_MIN + RNG.nextInt(INTERVAL_MAX - INTERVAL_MIN + 1);
            }
        } else if (cooldownTimer > 0) {
            cooldownTimer--;
        } else {
            startEruption(marsLevel);
        }

        syncCounter++;
        if (syncCounter >= SYNC_INTERVAL) {
            syncCounter = 0;
            broadcastState(marsLevel);
        }
    }

    private static void broadcastState(ServerLevel marsLevel) {
        boolean erupting = volcanoPos != null && eruptionTicks > 0;
        MarsVolcanoPayload payload = new MarsVolcanoPayload(
                erupting,
                erupting ? volcanoPos : BlockPos.ZERO,
                eruptionTicks,
                eruptionTotalTicks,
                cooldownTimer
        );
        for (ServerPlayer player : marsLevel.players()) {
            PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, payload);
        }
    }

    private static void startEruption(ServerLevel level) {
        if (level.players().isEmpty()) return;

        ServerPlayer anchor = level.players().get(RNG.nextInt(level.players().size()));

        double angle = RNG.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2;
        double dist = 16 + RNG.nextDouble() * SEARCH_RADIUS;

        int x = (int) (anchor.getX() + Math.cos(angle) * dist);
        int z = (int) (anchor.getZ() + Math.sin(angle) * dist);
        int y = level.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, x, z);

        volcanoPos = new BlockPos(x, y, z);
        eruptionTicks = DURATION_MIN + RNG.nextInt(DURATION_MAX - DURATION_MIN + 1);
        eruptionTotalTicks = eruptionTicks;

        broadcastState(level);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mars.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.LevelTickEvent;

/**
 * 火星没有大气，强制关闭原版天气（下雨 / 雷暴）。
 * 避免原版天气系统在火星维度生成闪电 + 雷声。
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MarsWeatherHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLevelTick(LevelTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (event.getLevel().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!event.getLevel().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MARS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        ServerLevel level = (ServerLevel) event.getLevel();

        // 每 100 刻检查一次，避免每刻都调
        if (level.getGameTime() % 100 != 0) return;

        if (level.isRaining() || level.isThundering()) {
            level.setWeatherParameters(
                    12000,  // clearTime：保持晴天
                    0,      // rainTime
                    false,  // raining
                    false   // thundering
            );
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MercuryGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "mercury_low_gravity");

    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 0.4;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInMercury = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInMercury && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInMercury && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.*;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;

import org.joml.Matrix4fStack;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MercurySkyRenderer {

    private static final float ROTATION_SPEED = 1.0f;

    // 星星：数量多，尺寸小，亮度偏暗
    private static final int STAR_COUNT = 3500;
    private static final float STAR_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // 贴图
    private static final ResourceLocation SUN_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("minecraft", "textures/environment/sun.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation VENUS_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/venus_icon.png");
    private static final ResourceLocation EARTH_TEXTURE =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath("newgc", "textures/gui/earth_icon.png");

    // 太阳
    private static final float SUN_SIZE = 35.0f;
    private static final float SUN_DISTANCE = 100.0f;

    // 金星（内侧行星，太阳旁边）
    private static final float VENUS_SIZE = 2.5f;
    private static final float VENUS_DISTANCE = 99.0f;
    private static final float VENUS_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(35.0f);
    private static final float VENUS_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(12.0f);

    // 地球（内侧行星，太阳旁边，反向偏移）
    private static final float EARTH_SIZE = 2.0f;
    private static final float EARTH_DISTANCE = 99.0f;
    private static final float EARTH_OFFSET_X = (float) Math.toRadians(-30.0f);
    private static final float EARTH_OFFSET_Y = (float) Math.toRadians(-10.0f);

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderSky(RenderLevelStageEvent event) {
        if (event.getStage() != RenderLevelStageEvent.Stage.AFTER_SKY) return;

        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float partialTick = event.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false);
        float timeOfDay = mc.level.getTimeOfDay(partialTick);

        // ===== 相机 + 昼夜自转矩阵 =====
        Matrix4fStack mvStack = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        mvStack.pushMatrix();
        mvStack.mul(event.getModelViewMatrix());
        mvStack.rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(-90.0f));
        mvStack.rotateX((float) Math.toRadians(timeOfDay * 360.0f * ROTATION_SPEED));
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        // ===== 渲染状态 =====
        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.blendFuncSeparate(
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.SRC_ALPHA,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.SourceFactor.ONE,
                GlStateManager.DestFactor.ZERO
        );

        // ==================== 1. 星星 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);   // 星星用默认颜色

        Tesselator starTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder starBuffer = starTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR);

        RandomSource random = RandomSource.create(10842L);

        for (int i = 0; i < STAR_COUNT; i++) {
            float cosTheta = random.nextFloat() * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            float sinTheta = Mth.sqrt(1.0f - cosTheta * cosTheta);
            float phi = random.nextFloat() * Mth.TWO_PI;

            float x = sinTheta * Mth.cos(phi);
            float y = cosTheta;
            float z = sinTheta * Mth.sin(phi);

            Vector3f starPos = new Vector3f(x, y, z).mul(STAR_DISTANCE);
            Quaternionf starRot = new Quaternionf().rotateTo(new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F), starPos);
            float size = 0.06f + random.nextFloat() * 0.05f;
            Vector3f starRight = new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);
            Vector3f starUp    = new Vector3f(0.0F, 1.0F, 0.0F).rotate(starRot).mul(size);

            float brightness = 0.12f + random.nextFloat() * 0.35f;

            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x - starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y - starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z - starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x + starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y + starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z + starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
            starBuffer.addVertex(starPos.x - starRight.x + starUp.x, starPos.y - starRight.y + starUp.y, starPos.z - starRight.z + starUp.z).setColor(brightness, brightness, brightness, 1.0f);
        }

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(starBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 2. 太阳 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, SUN_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator sunTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder sunBuffer = sunTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex(-SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE,  SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        sunBuffer.addVertex( SUN_SIZE, SUN_DISTANCE, -SUN_SIZE).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(sunBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 3. 金星 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, VENUS_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.85f, 0.85f, 0.85f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator venusTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder venusBuffer = venusTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(venusBuffer, VENUS_DISTANCE, VENUS_SIZE, VENUS_OFFSET_X, VENUS_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(venusBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ==================== 4. 地球 ====================
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexShader);
        RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, EARTH_TEXTURE);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(0.85f, 0.85f, 0.85f, 1.0f);

        Tesselator earthTess = Tesselator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder earthBuffer = earthTess.begin(
                VertexFormat.Mode.QUADS, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX);

        drawCelestialQuad(earthBuffer, EARTH_DISTANCE, EARTH_SIZE, EARTH_OFFSET_X, EARTH_OFFSET_Y);

        BufferUploader.drawWithShader(earthBuffer.buildOrThrow());

        // ===== 恢复 =====
        mvStack.popMatrix();
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);   // ★ 关键：恢复默认颜色，防止污染主世界
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
    }

    /** 在天顶平面上，按角度偏移画一个天体四边形 */
    private static void drawCelestialQuad(BufferBuilder buffer,
                                          float distance,
                                          float size,
                                          float offsetX,
                                          float offsetY) {
        Vector3f topLeft     = rotateOffset(-size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomLeft  = rotateOffset(-size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f bottomRight = rotateOffset( size, distance,  size, offsetX, offsetY);
        Vector3f topRight    = rotateOffset( size, distance, -size, offsetX, offsetY);

        buffer.addVertex(topLeft.x,     topLeft.y,     topLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 0.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomLeft.x,  bottomLeft.y,  bottomLeft.z).setUv(0.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(bottomRight.x, bottomRight.y, bottomRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 1.0f);
        buffer.addVertex(topRight.x,    topRight.y,    topRight.z).setUv(1.0f, 0.0f);
    }

    /** 把一个天顶平面上的点，按水平/垂直角度偏移旋转 */
    private static Vector3f rotateOffset(float x, float y, float z,
                                         float offsetX, float offsetY) {
        Vector3f v = new Vector3f(x, y, z);
        if (offsetY != 0) v.rotateZ(offsetY);
        if (offsetX != 0) v.rotateX(offsetX);
        return v;
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mercury\client\MercurySolarFlareCameraShake.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ViewportEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MercurySolarFlareCameraShake {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.04f;
    private static final float THRESHOLD = 1.0f;      // 超过 1.0 才晃
    private static final float MAX_AMPLITUDE = 0.3f;  // 很轻
    private static final float FREQUENCY = 0.1f;

    private static float smoothed = 0f;
    private static float shakeTime = 0f;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onComputeCameraAngles(ViewportEvent.ComputeCameraAngles event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float intensity = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getIntensity();
        if (Float.isNaN(intensity) || Float.isInfinite(intensity)) intensity = 0f;
        intensity = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, intensity));

        smoothed += (intensity - smoothed) * SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(smoothed)) smoothed = 0f;

        if (smoothed <= THRESHOLD) return;

        float t = (smoothed - THRESHOLD) / (1.5f - THRESHOLD);
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        shakeTime += 1f;

        float waveX = (float) Math.sin(shakeTime * FREQUENCY) * t * MAX_AMPLITUDE;
        float waveY = (float) Math.cos(shakeTime * FREQUENCY * 1.3f) * t * MAX_AMPLITUDE * 0.6f;

        event.setYaw(event.getYaw() + waveX);
        event.setPitch(event.getPitch() + waveY);
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mercury\client\MercurySolarFlareClientCache.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.client.debug.DebugState;

public final class MercurySolarFlareClientCache {

    public enum Phase {
        CALM, BUILDUP, FLARE, DECAY
    }

    private static int phaseOrdinal = Phase.CALM.ordinal();
    private static int phaseTimer = 0;
    private static float intensity = 0f;

    private MercurySolarFlareClientCache() {}

    public static void set(int ordinal, int timer, float value) {
        // 调试模式下不覆盖网络同步的值
        if (DebugState.getMercuryFlareForcedIntensity() >= 0) {
            return;
        }
        phaseOrdinal = ordinal;
        phaseTimer = timer;
        intensity = value;
    }

    public static Phase getPhase() {
        // 调试模式优先
        int debugPhase = DebugState.getMercuryFlarePhase();
        if (debugPhase >= 0) {
            Phase[] v = Phase.values();
            return v[Math.max(0, Math.min(v.length - 1, debugPhase))];
        }
        Phase[] v = Phase.values();
        int i = Math.max(0, Math.min(v.length - 1, phaseOrdinal));
        return v[i];
    }

    public static int getPhaseTimer() {
        if (DebugState.getMercuryFlareForcedIntensity() >= 0) return 0;
        return phaseTimer;
    }

    public static float getIntensity() {
        // 调试模式：平滑逼近目标
        float debug = DebugState.tickMercuryFlareSmooth();
        if (debug >= 0) return debug;
        return intensity;
    }

    public static boolean isFlaring() {
        return getPhase() == Phase.FLARE;
    }
}

================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ViewportEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MercurySolarFlareFogHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onComputeFogColor(ViewportEvent.ComputeFogColor event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float intensity = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getIntensity();
        if (Float.isNaN(intensity) || Float.isInfinite(intensity)) intensity = 0f;
        intensity = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, intensity));

        if (intensity < 0.2f) return;

        float t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, (intensity - 0.2f) / 1.3f));

        // 天空染成淡白蓝色
        float r = event.getRed()   + (0.95f - event.getRed())   * t * 0.7f;
        float g = event.getGreen() + (0.97f - event.getGreen()) * t * 0.7f;
        float b = event.getBlue()  + (1.00f - event.getBlue())  * t * 0.7f;

        event.setRed(r);
        event.setGreen(g);
        event.setBlue(b);
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mercury\client\MercurySolarFlareOverlay.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;

/**
 * 水星耀斑视觉（高逼真版）：
 * 1. 暗角渐晕（边缘变暗）
 * 2. 边缘淡蓝辉光
 * 3. FLARE：CRT 扫描线（双色）
 * 4. FLARE：水平撕裂条（带错位）
 * 5. FLARE：RGB 彩色偏移
 * 6. FLARE：漏液竖条
 * 7. FLARE：中心暖白光晕
 *
 * 所有效果随 intensity 平滑渐入渐出，无突变，不刺眼。
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MercurySolarFlareOverlay {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final float SMOOTH = 0.03f;

    private static float smoothed = 0f;
    private static long effectTick = 0;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onRenderGui(RenderGuiEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (mc.level == null) return;
        if (!mc.level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        float intensity = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getIntensity();
        if (Float.isNaN(intensity) || Float.isInfinite(intensity)) intensity = 0f;
        intensity = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, intensity));

        smoothed += (intensity - smoothed) * SMOOTH;
        if (Float.isNaN(smoothed)) smoothed = 0f;
        if (smoothed < 0.02f) return;

        effectTick++;

        float t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, smoothed / 1.5f));

        GuiGraphics g = event.getGuiGraphics();
        int w = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int h = mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();

        // ===== 1) 暗角（四角压暗）=====
        drawVignette(g, w, h, t);

        // ===== 2) 边缘辉光 =====
        drawEdgeGlow(g, w, h, t);

        // ===== FLARE 阶段独有 =====
        if (MercurySolarFlareClientCache.isFlaring()) {
            float flareT = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, (smoothed - 0.8f) / 0.7f));
            if (flareT <= 0.01f) return;

            // 3) CRT 扫描线
            drawScanlines(g, w, h, flareT);

            // 4) 撕裂条（带错位）
            drawTearBars(g, w, h, flareT);

            // 5) RGB 彩色偏移
            drawChromaticAberration(g, w, h, flareT);

            // 6) 漏液竖条
            drawLiquidLeak(g, w, h, flareT);

            // 7) 中心暖白光晕
            drawCenterGlow(g, w, h, t);
        }
    }

    // ================= 1) 暗角 =================
    private static void drawVignette(GuiGraphics g, int w, int h, float t) {
        int bands = 10;
        int maxBand = Math.min(w, h) / 2;
        for (int i = 0; i < bands; i++) {
            float band = (float) i / bands;
            int inset = (int) (maxBand * band);
            int alpha = (int) (t * 90 * (1f - band));
            if (alpha <= 0) continue;

            int color = (alpha << 24);   // 纯黑
            // 上、下、左、右四条
            g.fill(0, 0, w, inset, color);
            g.fill(0, h - inset, w, h, color);
            g.fill(0, inset, inset, h - inset, color);
            g.fill(w - inset, inset, w, h - inset, color);
        }
    }

    // ================= 2) 边缘辉光 =================
    private static void drawEdgeGlow(GuiGraphics g, int w, int h, float t) {
        int bands = 8;
        int maxInset = Math.min(w, h) / 3;
        for (int i = 0; i < bands; i++) {
            float band = (float) i / bands;
            int inset = (int) (maxInset * (1f - band));
            int alpha = (int) (t * 55 * (1f - band));
            if (alpha <= 0) continue;

            int color = (alpha << 24) | 0xC8E0FF;
            g.fill(0, inset, w, inset + 1, color);
            g.fill(0, h - inset - 1, w, h - inset, color);
            g.fill(inset, 0, inset + 1, h, color);
            g.fill(w - inset - 1, 0, w, inset + 1, color);
        }
    }

    // ================= 3) CRT 扫描线 =================
    private static void drawScanlines(GuiGraphics g, int w, int h, float t) {
        // 双色：暗线 + 稍亮的线，交替
        int darkAlpha = (int) (t * 30);
        int lightAlpha = (int) (t * 12);
        if (darkAlpha <= 0 && lightAlpha <= 0) return;

        int darkColor = (darkAlpha << 24);
        int lightColor = (lightAlpha << 24) | 0xFFFFFF;

        int gap = 3;
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y += gap) {
            g.fill(0, y, w, y + 1, darkColor);
            if (y + 1 < h && lightAlpha > 0) {
                g.fill(0, y + 1, w, y + 2, lightColor);
            }
        }
    }

    // ================= 4) 撕裂条 =================
    private static void drawTearBars(GuiGraphics g, int w, int h, float t) {
        int count = (int) (8 * t);
        count = Math.max(1, Math.min(8, count));

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            int barY = RNG.nextInt(h);
            int barH = 1 + RNG.nextInt(6);

            // 撕裂条位置/宽度：从左边随机到右边随机
            int startX = RNG.nextInt(w / 3);
            int endX = w - RNG.nextInt(w / 3);
            if (endX <= startX) continue;

            // 随机色：红/绿/蓝/白
            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            int rgb;
            if (roll < 0.30f)      rgb = 0xFF3030;   // 红
            else if (roll < 0.55f) rgb = 0x30FF30;   // 绿
            else if (roll < 0.80f) rgb = 0x3030FF;   // 蓝
            else                   rgb = 0xFFFFFF;   // 白

            int alpha = (int) (t * 110);
            if (alpha <= 0) continue;

            int color = (alpha << 24) | rgb;
            g.fill(startX, barY, endX, barY + barH, color);

            // 错位：撕裂条下方 1~2 像素的偏移色
            int offsetY = barY + barH;
            int offsetH = 1 + RNG.nextInt(2);
            int offsetStartX = Math.max(0, startX - 3 + RNG.nextInt(6));
            int offsetEndX = Math.min(w, endX - 3 + RNG.nextInt(6));
            int offsetAlpha = (int) (t * 80);
            if (offsetAlpha > 0 && offsetEndX > offsetStartX) {
                int offsetColor = (offsetAlpha << 24) | 0x000000;
                g.fill(offsetStartX, offsetY, offsetEndX, offsetY + offsetH, offsetColor);
            }

            // 撕裂条上方加一条亮线
            int hiAlpha = (int) (t * 70);
            if (hiAlpha > 0) {
                int hiColor = (hiAlpha << 24) | 0xFFFFFF;
                g.fill(startX, Math.max(0, barY - 1), endX, barY, hiColor);
            }
        }
    }

    // ================= 5) RGB 彩色偏移 =================
    private static void drawChromaticAberration(GuiGraphics g, int w, int h, float t) {
        // 在屏幕上下边缘画两条半透明色条，模拟 RGB 分离
        int alpha = (int) (t * 25);
        if (alpha <= 0) return;

        int redColor = (alpha << 24) | 0xFF0000;
        int cyanColor = (alpha << 24) | 0x00FFFF;

        // 左边缘红，右边缘青
        g.fill(0, 0, 2, h, redColor);
        g.fill(w - 2, 0, w, h, cyanColor);
    }

    // ================= 6) 漏液竖条 =================
    private static void drawLiquidLeak(GuiGraphics g, int w, int h, float t) {
        int count = (int) (4 * t);
        count = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, count));

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            int x = RNG.nextInt(w);
            int barW = 1 + RNG.nextInt(3);

            int startY = RNG.nextInt(h / 3);
            int len = h / 4 + RNG.nextInt(h / 2);

            // 蓝色/紫色渐变
            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            int rgb;
            if (roll < 0.5f) rgb = 0x4060C0;   // 蓝紫
            else             rgb = 0x8040A0;   // 紫

            int alpha = (int) (t * 70);
            if (alpha <= 0) continue;

            // 用多层模拟渐变
            int layers = 6;
            for (int l = 0; l < layers; l++) {
                float lr = (float) l / layers;
                int layerAlpha = (int) (alpha * (1f - lr));
                if (layerAlpha <= 0) continue;
                int color = (layerAlpha << 24) | rgb;
                int y0 = startY + (int) (len * lr);
                int y1 = startY + (int) (len * (lr + 1f / layers));
                g.fill(x, y0, x + barW, y1, color);
            }
        }
    }

    // ================= 7) 中心暖白光晕 =================
    private static void drawCenterGlow(GuiGraphics g, int w, int h, float t) {
        int cx = w / 2;
        int cy = h / 2;
        int maxR = (int) (Math.min(w, h) * 0.55f * t);

        int steps = 20;
        for (int i = steps; i > 0; i--) {
            float ratio = (float) i / steps;
            int r = (int) (maxR * ratio);
            int alpha = (int) (t * 20 * (1f - ratio) * (1f - ratio));
            if (alpha <= 0) continue;
            int color = (alpha << 24) | 0xFFF0C0;
            g.fill(cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r, color);
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mercury\client\MercurySolarFlareParticles.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

/**
 * 水星太阳耀斑粒子：
 * - 白色/淡蓝粒子从天空竖直落下
 * - 模拟"太阳风粒子流"
 * - 只在 BUILDUP 后期 / FLARE / DECAY 前段生成
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class MercurySolarFlareParticles {

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    private static final int RADIUS = 32;
    private static final int HEIGHT_ABOVE = 24;      // 玩家上方生成高度

    private static final int BASE_COUNT = 15;
    private static final int MAX_COUNT = 80;

    // 向下速度：不是自由落体，是"雨"的感觉
    private static final double FALL_SPEED = 1.2;
    private static final double HORIZONTAL_JITTER = 0.05;

    private static final int TICK_INTERVAL = 2;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (!player.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (!(player.level() instanceof ClientLevel level)) return;

        if (level.getGameTime() % TICK_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        spawnFlare(level, player);
    }

    private static void spawnFlare(ClientLevel level, Player player) {
        float intensity = MercurySolarFlareClientCache.getIntensity();
        if (Float.isNaN(intensity) || Float.isInfinite(intensity)) intensity = 0f;
        intensity = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.5f, intensity));

        // 强度低于 0.3 不生成
        if (intensity < 0.3f) return;

        float t = (intensity - 0.3f) / 1.2f;
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        int count = (int) (BASE_COUNT + (MAX_COUNT - BASE_COUNT) * t);
        count = Math.min(count, MAX_COUNT);

        double playerX = player.getX();
        double playerY = player.getY();
        double playerZ = player.getZ();

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            double dx = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;
            double dz = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * RADIUS * 2;

            double px = playerX + dx;
            double pz = playerZ + dz;

            // 在地表上方生成，高度随机
            int groundY = level.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, (int) px, (int) pz);
            double py = Math.max(playerY + HEIGHT_ABOVE, groundY + 8);

            // 竖直向下 + 小抖动
            double vx = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * HORIZONTAL_JITTER;
            double vz = (RNG.nextDouble() - 0.5) * HORIZONTAL_JITTER;
            double vy = -FALL_SPEED * (0.8 + RNG.nextDouble() * 0.4);

            // 颜色：白色 + 淡蓝
            float roll = RNG.nextFloat();
            if (roll < 0.7f) {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.END_ROD, px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            } else {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.FLAME, px, py, pz, vx, vy, vz);
            }

            // 偶尔加个 ASH 做颗粒感
            if (RNG.nextFloat() < 0.1f) {
                level.addParticle(ParticleTypes.ASH, px, py, pz, vx * 2, vy * 0.5, vz * 2);
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.network;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.client.MercurySolarFlareClientCache;
import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.handling.IPayloadContext;

public record MercurySolarFlarePayload(
        int phaseOrdinal,
        int phaseTimer,
        float intensity
) implements CustomPacketPayload {

    public static final CustomPacketPayload.Type<MercurySolarFlarePayload> TYPE =
            new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(
                    ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "mercury_solar_flare"));

    public static final StreamCodec<ByteBuf, MercurySolarFlarePayload> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, MercurySolarFlarePayload::phaseOrdinal,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, MercurySolarFlarePayload::phaseTimer,
                    ByteBufCodecs.FLOAT,   MercurySolarFlarePayload::intensity,
                    MercurySolarFlarePayload::new
            );

    @Override
    public Type<? extends CustomPacketPayload> type() {
        return TYPE;
    }

    public static void handle(MercurySolarFlarePayload payload, IPayloadContext context) {
        context.enqueueWork(() ->
                MercurySolarFlareClientCache.set(
                        payload.phaseOrdinal(),
                        payload.phaseTimer(),
                        payload.intensity()
                )
        );
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MercurySolarFlareDamageHandler {

    // 最大每秒伤害（FLARE 峰值时）
    private static final float MAX_DPS = 10.0f;

    // 伤害间隔（刻）
    private static final int DAMAGE_INTERVAL = 20;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (player.isCreative() || player.isSpectator()) return;

        float intensity = MercurySolarFlareManager.getFlareIntensity();
        if (intensity <= 0.2f) return;

        if (player.tickCount % DAMAGE_INTERVAL != 0) return;

        // 强度 0.2~1.5 映射到 0~1
        float t = (intensity - 0.2f) / 1.3f;
        t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, t));

        float damage = MAX_DPS * t;
        if (damage <= 0.05f) return;

        player.hurt(player.damageSources().generic(), damage);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\mercury\server\MercurySolarFlareManager.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.server;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.mercury.network.MercurySolarFlarePayload;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.ServerTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.network.PacketDistributor;

/**
 * 水星太阳耀斑状态机。
 * 平静期 5~15 分钟，让耀斑成为"罕见事件"。
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class MercurySolarFlareManager {

    public enum Phase {
        CALM, BUILDUP, FLARE, DECAY
    }

    // ===== 各阶段持续时间（刻）=====
    // 平静：5~15 分钟
    private static final int CALM_MIN_TICKS    = 6000, CALM_MAX_TICKS    = 18000;
    // 预警：3~8 秒
    private static final int BUILDUP_MIN_TICKS = 60,   BUILDUP_MAX_TICKS = 160;
    // 爆发：2~5 秒
    private static final int FLARE_MIN_TICKS   = 40,   FLARE_MAX_TICKS   = 100;
    // 消退：5~15 秒
    private static final int DECAY_MIN_TICKS   = 100,  DECAY_MAX_TICKS   = 300;

    private static final int SYNC_INTERVAL = 5;

    private static Phase currentPhase = Phase.CALM;
    private static int phaseTimer = 0;
    private static int globalTick = 0;

    private static int syncCounter = 0;

    private static final RandomSource RNG = RandomSource.create();

    public static Phase getCurrentPhase() { return currentPhase; }
    public static int getPhaseTimer()     { return phaseTimer; }

    public static float getFlareIntensity() {
        return switch (currentPhase) {
            case CALM -> 0f;
            case BUILDUP -> {
                int total = Math.max(1, BUILDUP_MAX_TICKS);
                float progress = 1f - (float) phaseTimer / total;
                yield Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, progress));
            }
            case FLARE -> {
                float wave = (float) Math.sin(globalTick * 0.3) * 0.15f;
                yield 1.0f + wave;
            }
            case DECAY -> {
                int total = Math.max(1, DECAY_MAX_TICKS);
                float progress = (float) phaseTimer / total;
                yield Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, progress));
            }
        };
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onServerTick(ServerTickEvent.Post event) {
        ServerLevel mercuryLevel = null;
        for (ServerLevel level : event.getServer().getAllLevels()) {
            if (level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.MERCURY_LEVEL_KEY)) {
                mercuryLevel = level;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (mercuryLevel == null) {
            currentPhase = Phase.CALM;
            phaseTimer = 0;
            return;
        }

        globalTick++;
        phaseTimer--;

        if (phaseTimer <= 0) {
            switchToNextPhase();
        }

        syncCounter++;
        if (syncCounter >= SYNC_INTERVAL) {
            syncCounter = 0;
            MercurySolarFlarePayload payload = new MercurySolarFlarePayload(
                    currentPhase.ordinal(),
                    phaseTimer,
                    getFlareIntensity()
            );
            for (ServerPlayer player : mercuryLevel.players()) {
                PacketDistributor.sendToPlayer(player, payload);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void switchToNextPhase() {
        currentPhase = switch (currentPhase) {
            case CALM -> Phase.BUILDUP;
            case BUILDUP -> Phase.FLARE;
            case FLARE -> Phase.DECAY;
            case DECAY -> Phase.CALM;
        };
        phaseTimer = getRandomDuration(currentPhase);
    }

    private static int getRandomDuration(Phase phase) {
        return switch (phase) {
            case CALM -> CALM_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(CALM_MAX_TICKS - CALM_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case BUILDUP -> BUILDUP_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(BUILDUP_MAX_TICKS - BUILDUP_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case FLARE -> FLARE_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(FLARE_MAX_TICKS - FLARE_MIN_TICKS + 1);
            case DECAY -> DECAY_MIN_TICKS + RNG.nextInt(DECAY_MAX_TICKS - DECAY_MIN_TICKS + 1);
        };
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\venus\client\VenusEnvironmentHandler.java
