package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.venus.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EquipmentSlot;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorMaterial;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorMaterials;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class VenusEnvironmentHandler {


    private static final int DAMAGE_INTERVAL = 40;

    private static final float DAMAGE_AMOUNT = 1.0f;

    private static final int MESSAGE_INTERVAL = 30;

    private static final int ARMOR_DAMAGE_INTERVAL = 600;

    private static final int ARMOR_DAMAGE_AMOUNT = 2;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;
        if (!player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.VENUS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;
        if (player.isCreative() || player.isSpectator()) return;

        boolean protectedByArmor = hasFullProtectiveArmor(player);

        if (!protectedByArmor) {

            if (player.tickCount % DAMAGE_INTERVAL == 0) {
                player.hurt(player.damageSources().generic(), DAMAGE_AMOUNT);
            }
            if (player.tickCount % MESSAGE_INTERVAL == 0) {
                player.displayClientMessage(
                        Component.translatable("message.newgc.venus.no_armor"),
                        true
                );
            }
        } else {

            if (player.tickCount % ARMOR_DAMAGE_INTERVAL == 0) {
                damageArmorPiece(player, EquipmentSlot.HEAD, ARMOR_DAMAGE_AMOUNT);
                damageArmorPiece(player, EquipmentSlot.CHEST, ARMOR_DAMAGE_AMOUNT);
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean hasFullProtectiveArmor(ServerPlayer player) {
        EquipmentSlot[] slots = {
                EquipmentSlot.HEAD,
                EquipmentSlot.CHEST,
                EquipmentSlot.LEGS,
                EquipmentSlot.FEET
        };

        for (EquipmentSlot slot : slots) {
            ItemStack stack = player.getItemBySlot(slot);
            if (!isProtectiveArmor(stack)) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private static boolean isProtectiveArmor(ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return false;
        if (!(stack.getItem() instanceof ArmorItem armor)) return false;

        Holder<ArmorMaterial> mat = armor.getMaterial();
        return mat.is(ArmorMaterials.IRON)
                || mat.is(ArmorMaterials.GOLD)
                || mat.is(ArmorMaterials.DIAMOND)
                || mat.is(ArmorMaterials.NETHERITE);
    }

    private static void damageArmorPiece(ServerPlayer player, EquipmentSlot slot, int amount) {
        ItemStack stack = player.getItemBySlot(slot);
        if (stack.isEmpty()) return;
        if (!(stack.getItem() instanceof ArmorItem)) return;
        if (!stack.isDamageableItem()) return;

        stack.hurtAndBreak(amount, player, slot);
    }
}

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.venus.client;

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.venus.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeModifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.PlayerTickEvent;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class VenusGravityHandler {

    private static final ResourceLocation GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID =
            ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "venus_high_gravity");

    // 相对主世界（1.0）的重力系数
    private static final double GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER = 0.9;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(PlayerTickEvent.Post event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide()) return;

        boolean isInVenus = player.level().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.VENUS_LEVEL_KEY);
        AttributeInstance gravityAttr = player.getAttribute(Attributes.GRAVITY);
        if (gravityAttr == null) return;

        boolean hasModifier = gravityAttr.hasModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);

        if (isInVenus && !hasModifier) {
            AttributeModifier modifier = new AttributeModifier(
                    GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID,
                    GRAVITY_MULTIPLIER - 1.0,
                    AttributeModifier.Operation.ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE
            );
            gravityAttr.addTransientModifier(modifier);
        } else if (!isInVenus && hasModifier) {
            gravityAttr.removeModifier(GRAVITY_MODIFIER_ID);
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\venus\client\VenusParticleHandler.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.venus.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.DustParticleOptions;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class VenusParticleHandler {

    private static final float SPAWN_CHANCE = 0.6f;
    private static final double RADIUS = 16.0;
    private static final double VERTICAL_RANGE = 12.0;

    private static final Vector3f COLOR_ORANGE = new Vector3f(1.0f, 0.65f, 0.15f);
    private static final Vector3f COLOR_GRAY   = new Vector3f(0.55f, 0.55f, 0.55f);
    private static final Vector3f COLOR_BLACK  = new Vector3f(0.15f, 0.15f, 0.15f);

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientTick(ClientTickEvent.Post event) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        ClientLevel level = mc.level;
        if (level == null || mc.player == null) return;
        if (!level.dimension().equals(ModDimensions.VENUS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        RandomSource random = level.random;
        Vec3 playerPos = mc.player.position();

        int attempts = 6;
        for (int i = 0; i < attempts; i++) {
            if (random.nextFloat() > SPAWN_CHANCE) continue;

            double x = playerPos.x + (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 2 * RADIUS;
            double y = playerPos.y + (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 2 * VERTICAL_RANGE;
            double z = playerPos.z + (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 2 * RADIUS;

            if (!level.isEmptyBlock(BlockPos.containing(x, y, z))) continue;

            double vx = (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.02;
            double vy = random.nextDouble() * 0.015 + 0.005;
            double vz = (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.02;

            int colorChoice = random.nextInt(3);
            Vector3f color = switch (colorChoice) {
                case 0 -> COLOR_ORANGE;
                case 1 -> COLOR_GRAY;
                default -> COLOR_BLACK;
            };

            float size = 1.0f + random.nextFloat() * 1.5f;

            level.addParticle(
                    new DustParticleOptions(color, size),
                    x, y, z,
                    vx, vy, vz
            );

            if (random.nextFloat() < 0.3f) {
                level.addParticle(
                        ParticleTypes.SMOKE,
                        x, y, z,
                        vx * 0.5, vy * 0.5, vz * 0.5
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\Planets\venus\client\VenusSkyEffects.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.venus.client;

import net.minecraft.client.renderer.DimensionSpecialEffects;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class VenusSkyEffects extends DimensionSpecialEffects {

    public VenusSkyEffects() {
        // cloudHeight = Float.NaN → 禁用原版天空的云（金星用自己的酸雨）
        super(Float.NaN, false, SkyType.NONE, false, false);
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull Vec3 getBrightnessDependentFogColor(@NotNull Vec3 fogColor, float brightness) {
        // 金星：明显黄，但不荧光
        float dayR = 0.84f, dayG = 0.74f, dayB = 0.36f;
        float nightR = 0.18f, nightG = 0.15f, nightB = 0.08f;

        float t = Mth.clamp(brightness, 0f, 1f);
        t = t * t * (3f - 2f * t);

        float r = nightR + (dayR - nightR) * t;
        float g = nightG + (dayG - nightG) * t;
        float b = nightB + (dayB - nightB) * t;
        return new Vec3(r, g, b);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isFoggyAt(int x, int z) {
        return true;   // 开启地形雾
    }

    @Override
    public float[] getSunriseColor(float timeOfDay, float partialTick) {
        // 柔和的黄褐日落
        return new float[]{0.88f, 0.62f, 0.28f, 0.40f};
    }
}

================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.venus.client;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import com.tairitsutek.newgc.Planets.ModDimensions;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LightningBolt;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.event.tick.LevelTickEvent;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

@EventBusSubscriber(modid = NewGalaxyMain.MODID)
public class VenusThunderHandler {

    private static final int LIGHTNING_RADIUS = 24;

    private static final Map<ResourceKey<Level>, Integer> COOLDOWNS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLevelTick(LevelTickEvent.Post event) {
        if (event.getLevel().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!event.getLevel().dimension().equals(ModDimensions.VENUS_LEVEL_KEY)) return;

        ServerLevel venus = (ServerLevel) event.getLevel();
        ResourceKey<Level> key = venus.dimension();

        int cooldown = COOLDOWNS.getOrDefault(key, 0);
        if (cooldown > 0) {
            COOLDOWNS.put(key, cooldown - 1);
            return;
        }

        int newCooldown = 400 + venus.random.nextInt(400);
        COOLDOWNS.put(key, newCooldown);

        if (venus.players().isEmpty()) return;

        ServerPlayer player = venus.players().get(venus.random.nextInt(venus.players().size()));
        double x = player.getX() + (venus.random.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * LIGHTNING_RADIUS;
        double z = player.getZ() + (venus.random.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * LIGHTNING_RADIUS;
        int y = venus.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, (int) x, (int) z);

        LightningBolt bolt = new LightningBolt(EntityType.LIGHTNING_BOLT, venus);
        bolt.moveTo(x, y, z);
        bolt.setVisualOnly(true);
        venus.addFreshEntity(bolt);
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\recipe\CompressorRecipe.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe;

import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import net.minecraft.core.HolderLookup;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.ByteBufCodecs;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.Ingredient;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.Recipe;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeSerializer;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeType;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.SingleRecipeInput;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class CompressorRecipe implements Recipe<SingleRecipeInput> {

    private final Ingredient ingredient;
    private final ItemStack result;
    private final int inputCount;

    public CompressorRecipe(Ingredient ingredient, ItemStack result, int inputCount) {
        this.ingredient = ingredient;
        this.result = result;
        this.inputCount = inputCount;
    }

    public Ingredient getIngredient() {
        return ingredient;
    }

    public ItemStack getResult() {
        return result;
    }

    public int getInputCount() {
        return inputCount;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean matches(SingleRecipeInput input, @NotNull Level level) {
        return ingredient.test(input.getItem(0));
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull ItemStack assemble(@NotNull SingleRecipeInput input, HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        return result.copy();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean canCraftInDimensions(int width, int height) {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull ItemStack getResultItem(HolderLookup.@NotNull Provider registries) {
        return result.copy();
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull RecipeSerializer<?> getSerializer() {
        return ModRecipes.COMPRESSING_SERIALIZER.get();
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull RecipeType<?> getType() {
        return ModRecipes.COMPRESSING.get();
    }

    public static final MapCodec<CompressorRecipe> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.mapCodec(instance ->
            instance.group(
                    Ingredient.CODEC_NONEMPTY.fieldOf("ingredient").forGetter(CompressorRecipe::getIngredient),
                    ItemStack.CODEC.fieldOf("result").forGetter(CompressorRecipe::getResult),
                    Codec.INT.optionalFieldOf("inputCount", 1).forGetter(CompressorRecipe::getInputCount)
            ).apply(instance, CompressorRecipe::new)
    );

    public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, CompressorRecipe> STREAM_CODEC =
            StreamCodec.composite(
                    Ingredient.CONTENTS_STREAM_CODEC, CompressorRecipe::getIngredient,
                    ItemStack.STREAM_CODEC, CompressorRecipe::getResult,
                    ByteBufCodecs.VAR_INT, CompressorRecipe::getInputCount,
                    CompressorRecipe::new
            );
}


================================================================================
📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\recipe\CompressorRecipeSerializer.java
================================================================================
package com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe;

import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeSerializer;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class CompressorRecipeSerializer implements RecipeSerializer<CompressorRecipe> {

    @Override
    public @NotNull MapCodec<CompressorRecipe> codec() {
        return CompressorRecipe.CODEC;
    }

    @Override
    public @NotNull StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, CompressorRecipe> streamCodec() {
        return CompressorRecipe.STREAM_CODEC;
    }
}

📄 文件路径: src\main\java\com\tairitsutek\newgc\recipe\ModRecipes.java

package com.tairitsutek.newgc.recipe;

import com.tairitsutek.newgc.NewGalaxyMain;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeSerializer;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.RecipeType;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.registries.DeferredRegister;

public class ModRecipes {

    public static final DeferredRegister<RecipeType<?>> RECIPE_TYPES =
            DeferredRegister.create(Registries.RECIPE_TYPE, NewGalaxyMain.MODID);

    public static final DeferredRegister<RecipeSerializer<?>> RECIPE_SERIALIZERS =
            DeferredRegister.create(Registries.RECIPE_SERIALIZER, NewGalaxyMain.MODID);

    public static final DeferredHolder<RecipeType<?>, RecipeType<CompressorRecipe>> COMPRESSING =
            RECIPE_TYPES.register("compressing",
                    () -> RecipeType.simple(ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(NewGalaxyMain.MODID, "compressing")));

    public static final DeferredHolder<RecipeSerializer<?>, CompressorRecipeSerializer> COMPRESSING_SERIALIZER =
            RECIPE_SERIALIZERS.register("compressing", CompressorRecipeSerializer::new);

    public static void register(IEventBus bus) {
        RECIPE_TYPES.register(bus);
        RECIPE_SERIALIZERS.register(bus);
    }
}
